## Hook 15: IDO52_SKIP_PASSES — skip whole optimizer passes (all tokens wired 2026-07-26)

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_SKIP_PASSES` | comma-separated token list | `IDO52_SKIP_PASSES=codemotion,eliminduct` |

Default OFF: unset, the mask is 0 and every pass runs. Proven by compiling 60
overlay `.c` files with the hooked and unhooked compilers under a clean
environment — 60/60 `.o` md5-identical.

### Wiring

Each pass is called from the uopt driver exactly once, as a bare
`f_NAME(mem, sp);` statement followed by a `goto` to its state-reload block.
Both the forward declaration and the definition spell the parameter list
`(uint8_t *mem, uint32_t sp)`, so the bare-call text is unique to the driver
site. Guarding the call alone leaves the `goto` and the reload block untouched.

Four tokens (`tail_recursion`, `loopunroll`, `copypropagate`, `opt_saved_regs`)
were accepted by the parser but had **no injection site at all** — setting them
did nothing and reported nothing, a silent no-op that mis-attributed at least
one blocker (see Hook 20). They now guard their calls like the other three.

| Token | Bit | Driver call | Status |
|-------|-----|-------------|--------|
| `tail_recursion` | 0 | `f_tail_recursion(mem, sp);` | wired; **transform is inert on this codebase** |
| `loopunroll` | 1 | `f_loopunroll(mem, sp);` | wired, live |
| `codemotion` | 2 | `f_codemotion(mem, sp);` | ⛔ **wired but UNSOUND — deletes loads, see below** |
| `eliminduct` | 3 | `f_eliminduct(mem, sp, a0, a1, a2);` | wired, live |
| `copypropagate` | 4 | `f_copypropagate(mem, sp);` | wired; **skipping crashes uopt** |
| `opt_saved_regs` | 5 | `f_opt_saved_regs(mem, sp);` | wired, live |
| `findinduct` | 6 | `f_findinduct(mem, sp);` | wired; **skipping crashes uopt** |

`findinduct` implies `eliminduct` (the parser ORs the bit in).

### Measured behaviour of the four newly wired tokens

- **`loopunroll` — wired and live.** Without `-Wo,-loopunroll,0`, skipping it
  changes the object (`func_8013EE38`: md5 differs from the base build). With
  `-Wo,-loopunroll,0` already set it is inert, as expected. ⚠️ It also
  suppresses the `EQ_INEQ` exit-test normalization, because that lives inside
  `f_loopunroll` — but use `IDO52_NO_LOOP_TEST_REPLACEMENT` for that, which is
  surgical (Hook 20).
- **`opt_saved_regs` — wired and live, large effect.** 3 of 15 randomly sampled
  overlay objects change. This is the **save/restore emission** pass, not the
  promotion decision: skipping it deletes the prologue `sw sN,K(sp)` and
  epilogue `lw sN,K(sp)` block (−17 insns on `func_8013EE38`, −18 on
  `func_8013E3D4`) while leaving every s-register *use* in place. It is
  therefore a diagnostic, not a matching lever — the output is not
  semantically valid code.
- **`tail_recursion` — wired, but the transform never fires here.** 250
  randomly sampled overlay objects: zero changed, zero crashed. Corroborated
  independently: the documented `-Wo,-notail` flag also changes 0 of 80
  objects, and `f_tail_recursion` opens by testing the option byte at
  `0x1001eacc` and returning immediately. The guard is on the unique driver
  call and the mask is confirmed set (`[SKIP-PASS] mask=0x01`); there is simply
  no tail recursion for it to remove in this codebase.
- ⛔ **`codemotion` — wired, and skipping it MISCOMPILES. Do not use it as a
  matching lever.** It is the same defect as `copypropagate` and
  `opt_saved_regs`, except that it neither crashes nor is obviously wrong: it
  silently emits code that reads uninitialised stack slots. Measured on
  `RPG_BAT/func_80154624` (the original C, its full shipped hook set):

  | | body | `lbu` | sp-relative reads never written |
  |---|---:|---:|---|
  | with `SKIP_PASSES=codemotion` | 86 words | 3 | 24, 28, 32, 36, 40 |
  | same C, token removed | 88 words | 7 | none |

  Four of the seven record loads and the `addu` feeding them are gone; the
  four `xori`s that consumed them instead read `lw $t4,40($sp)` and friends,
  slots nothing in the function ever writes and no callee can fill (there is
  no `addiu $rX,$sp,N` in the body). The arm XORs stack garbage into four live
  game records.

  ⚠️ **It is also a count compensator**, which is how it got shipped: deleting
  those words took 88 down to the target's 81-word neighbourhood, so the
  function "became" count-exact and every other hook on the file was then
  fitted on top of a broken object and is worth nothing without it. The same
  token on `EVENT/func_8015ADFC` produces five reads of never-written slots
  and made a +4-word residual look like +32.

  Detector: `tools/overlay/audit_uninit_stack.py` CHECK 1 (compare sp-relative
  read offsets against write offsets in the raw object). ⚠️ That check was
  VACUOUS until 2026-08-29 — its frame regex required a `$sp` spelling objdump
  does not print, so it reported "ok" for every function ever passed to it.
  Any clean result from it dated before that is not evidence.

  **Live blast radius: 12 files**, not the 43 a grep suggests — 75 files
  mention the token, 63 of them as de-fanged prose narrating a retired
  directive. None of the 12 is `byte_perfect` (8 semis, 3 `asm_only`, 1
  main-binary semi), so nothing ships it and `build-c` still reproduces the
  retail SHA1. Three of the 11 checkable ones flag: `MASTER/func_80137044`
  and `MASTER/func_801375E8` at `44($sp)`, `TAIIKU/func_80147CBC` at six
  slots `76..86($sp)`.

- **`copypropagate` — wired, but skipping it is not usable.** 40 of 40 sampled
  objects abort with `uopt: Internal: <func>: code: 411`, the same failure code
  `f_codemotion` produced before its bitvector stub was written. Copy
  propagation publishes state that a later pass reads unconditionally. Making
  this token usable needs the same treatment as `codemotion`: a stub that
  initializes the structures `f_getexpsources` and friends consume downstream.
  Until someone writes that stub, treat `copypropagate` as diagnostic-only.
  (`findinduct` behaves the same way and always has.)

on `func_8013EE38`); an **occurrence window on `LDA_NOINREG_CLASSES`**, mirroring
the `CONST_NOINREG` window of Hook 2, is the obvious next lever.

---

## Hook 21: IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL / IDO52_AS1_FORCE_LA_PULL — as1 cross-basic-block pull control (2026-07-26)

**This is an `as1` hook, not a uopt/ugen hook.** It lives in
`tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_hooks.py` and patches `build/5.2/as1.c`.
`as1.c` is regenerated by the Makefile exactly like `uopt.c`/`ugen.c`, so after
any `make clean` / IDO rebuild you must re-run **all three** scripts:

```bash
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_hooks.py
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_ugen_hooks.py
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_hooks.py      # <-- easy to forget
cd tools/ido-static-recomp && make VERSION=5.2
```

`apply_as1_hooks.py` is now **per-hook idempotent** — it probes each hook's env
name individually, so adding a hook no longer requires regenerating `as1.c`.

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL` | `1` / `all`, or `N[,N…]` (0-based candidate index) | `IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL=8` |
| `IDO52_AS1_FORCE_LA_PULL` | same syntax, same index space | `IDO52_AS1_FORCE_LA_PULL=4` |
| `IDO52_AS1_LA_PULL_TRACE` | any value (presence enables) | `IDO52_AS1_LA_PULL_TRACE=1` |

Default OFF. Verified: 50 overlay `.c` files compiled with the hooked as1 and
with a pristine as1 rebuilt from the same regenerated source produce **50/50
byte-identical `.o`** with none of the variables set, and two known
byte-perfect functions (`BUNKAKEN/func_80132000`, `BUNKAKEN/func_8013209C`)
still resolve `*** BYTE-PERFECT ***`.

### The blocker class it addresses: `as1_la_pull`

After ugen has emitted the final instruction stream, **as1 still moves
instructions between basic blocks.** The symptom is a `la $aN,SYM` macro
(`lui`+`addiu`) — or just its `lui` half — appearing *above* the branch that
guards the block it belongs to, filling a load-use interlock or a branch delay
slot that the 1995 target leaves as a `nop` or fills with something else. No
uopt or ugen hook can reach it, and neither can `-Wa,-O0` (see below), which is
why three functions sat in `config/c_unmatchable.txt` under `as1_la_pull` with
exhaustive "everything is inert" probe logs.

### Where it lives in as1

as1's per-procedure driver (around `L41e8cc`) runs, in order:

```
f_schedule()      per basic block      -- intra-BB list scheduling
f_do_xbb_opt()    once per procedure   -- CROSS-BB code motion
```

`f_do_xbb_opt` is a driver over seven sub-passes. Six are analysis/teardown
(`func_425fb0`, `func_42679c`, `func_426214`, `func_426d0c` are bit-vector
dataflow; `func_428ab4` is a `print_set` debug dump at as1 `-O>=4`;
`func_428cec` frees). **`func_4281e0` is the only one that moves anything** —
it is the pass that calls `f_change_bb` / `f_reschedule` / `f_dump_bb`. Its
inner loop is a classic speculative-motion ("boosting") driver:

```
fp = func_427800(pred_bb, succ_bb, bb_vec, &scratch);   /* legal candidate, 0 = none */
if (fp == 0) goto done;
L428604:                          /* <-- IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL veto site */
    D_10022660++;                                       /* global xbb-move counter   */
    s5 = func_427c54(pred_bb, fp, succ_bb);             /* trial-move the record     */
    s0 = reschedule(succ_bb);  s7 = reschedule(pred_bb);
    if (combined schedule got shorter) goto L4287b8;    /* COMMIT (printfs the move) */
L4286b0:                          /* <-- IDO52_AS1_FORCE_LA_PULL site (the UNDO path) */
    copy the 0x28-byte insn record back; change_bb(); reschedule; assert lengths
    goto L428984;                                       /* try the next candidate    */
```

* `L428604` is the confluence point of **both** `func_427800` call sites (the
  initial one and the "try the next candidate in this BB pair" retry at
  `L428984`), so vetoing there is byte-for-byte identical to `func_427800`
  having returned 0.
* `L4286b0` is the **undo** path, not the move — it is reached only from the
  two profitability branches, always with `v1 == 0x28`, and it restores the
  record so the two following `assertion_failed` length checks pass.
  `L4287b8` is the commit path; it is the one that `printf`s
  `"moving insn …"` at as1 `-O>=2`. Diverting `L4286b0 -> L4287b8` keeps a
  move the heuristic rejected.

### Modes

| Value | Behaviour |
|-------|-----------|
| unset | no effect (default) |
| `NO_LA_PULL=1` / `all` | refuse every cross-BB pull |
| `NO_LA_PULL=3,7` | refuse candidates #3 and #7 |
| `FORCE_LA_PULL=4` | keep candidate #4 even though the heuristic undid it |

Both share one 0-based counter that advances once per candidate reaching
`L428604`, so the indices are stable across combinations of the two variables
(a vetoed candidate returns before the force decision). The counter is global
per as1 run — keep one function per translation unit, which the overlay layout
already does.

`IDO52_AS1_LA_PULL_TRACE=1` prints one line per candidate
(`#N allowed` / `#N VETOED`) plus, for candidates the heuristic undoes,
`#N rejected by heuristic`. **That second line is how you pick `FORCE`
indices** — only candidates that print it can be forced.

```bash
IDO52_AS1_LA_PULL_TRACE=1 tools/ido-static-recomp/build/5.2/out/cc \
    -O2 -G0 -mips1 -EL -non_shared -I include -c src/overlay/OV/func_X.c -o /tmp/x.o
```

### ⚠️ Why `-Wa,-O0` reads as inert

Every `as1_la_pull` probe log records `-Wa,-O0` as having no effect, which made
the pass look unreachable. It is not: `do_xbb_opt` is gated on
`D_1002f2c8 != 0 && D_1002f8d8 >= D_1002237c`, not on the `-O` level that
`-Wa,-O0` sets (that level only gates the `print_set` dump at `>=4` and the
`printf` trace at `>=2`). The pass runs regardless. Confirm with the trace
variable rather than trusting the flag.

### Choosing the metric

`test_overlay_match.py`'s `exact%` is a **bad** search signal here — cross-BB
motion rotates register fields all over the body, so exact% barely moves while
the alignment changes a lot. Score candidates on the shift-aware
mnemonic-identity split instead (words whose opcode identity matches at the
same index, separated into register-field-only vs genuine operation diffs) and
minimise the **op-DIFF** column. On SHOUGATU/func_8013F570 the `exact%` metric
ranked the wrong setting first; op-DIFF ranked correctly.

### Measured results

| Function | before | with hook |
|----------|--------|-----------|
| `GEKO/func_80136790` | honest SEMI, 6.2% exact, blocker `as1_la_pull` | **BYTE-PERFECT 210/210, `len t=840 c=840`** with `IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL=8` (+ the pre-existing `patch_stack_complete` / 2× `swap_instructions`, + `@RodataBase: 0x801447FC`) |
| `NAME_ENT/func_8013425C` | SEMI, exact 32, mnem 138/564, op-DIFF 458 | `IDO52_AS1_FORCE_LA_PULL=4`: exact 37, mnem 166/564, op-DIFF 426 — **still SEMI**, banked as a partial |
| `SHOUGATU/func_8013F570` | SEMI, exact 130, mnem 300/332, op-DIFF 36 | every veto/force/`all`/pair setting is neutral or worse — **refuted**, its residual is not the xbb pass |

`GEKO/func_80136790` is the canonical worked example. It offers 12 candidates;
#8 is the one that speculates `la $a0,D_800D069C` out of the switch's default
arm into the dispatch block, where its `lui` eats the `lhu` load-use interlock
`nop` and its `addiu` takes the bound-check `beq` delay slot. Vetoing **only**
#8 is essential — `NO_LA_PULL=all` overruns the symbol window by 4 words,
because the target performs the other eleven pulls.

### When NOT to reach for this

If the misplaced word never crosses a basic-block boundary (e.g. two `lui`s
hoisted within the block that precedes a `div` guard, as in
`SHOUGATU/func_8013F570`), `do_xbb_opt` is not the mechanism. That is as1's
**intra**-block scheduler, `f_schedule` -> `f_reorganize_bb` / `r6_sched`,
which has no fine-grained hook yet and is the obvious next target. The existing
`IDO52_AS1_NO_SCHEDULE` disables it wholesale and crashes;
`IDO52_AS1_NO_BRANCH_OPT` and `IDO52_AS1_NO_FILL` are far too coarse; and
`IDO52_AS1_NO_DELAY_FILL` never fires because `v0` is already `0` on every
observed entry to `L42f170`.

## Hook 23: IDO52_UGEN_TREG_ORDER_WINDOW — occurrence-windowed ugen temp-pool clamp (2026-07-30)

**The hook that unlocked the flagship** (DATE/func_80132728, 1,479 insns — the
project's largest match candidate). Built in a private toolchain copy, proven,
regression-gated (20/20 bit-identical with env unset), then applied to the
shared build at batch-19 close. Lives in `apply_ugen_hooks.py`.

```
IDO52_UGEN_TREG_ORDER_WINDOW=<pool>:<from>:<to>[,<pool>:<from>:<to>...]
IDO52_UGEN_TREG_WINDOW_DEBUG=1
```

- `<pool>` = decimal N (keep the first N registers of the function-entry temp
  free list, in that list's order) **or** a `+`-separated register list
  (`t6+t9`). `<from>`/`<to>` = inclusive 1-based **f_get_reg occurrence
  indices, counted per function** (`-1` = to end). Outside every window the
  captured entry pool is restored.
- Implementation: capture at `L43a1e8` in `f_init_regs` (after the free list is
  built); clamp at `L43b11c` (`f_get_reg` entry, before its frame). Entry pool
  order on this build: **t6,t7,t8,t9,t0..t5** (only t6–t9 live at codegen) —
  that is the "4-temp default". Free-list membership is WALKED, not read from
  the `+7` flag (safety fix). Refuses to empty the pool.

**Why it exists (the flagship mechanism, proven by a 17-probe C-side matrix in
the function's header):** ugen assigns temps round-robin in emission order —
index `r`, product `r+1`, base `r+2` (mod pool). The target's base `lui` at one
site shares the register with the still-live index (`base == index`), which
makes the delay-slot hoist illegal and leaves the target's `nop`. That requires
`pool == 2` EXACTLY; uopt's color cap (color 13 = t6) means source-level
pressure can shrink the pool to at most 3, and any 3-pool is bit-identical to
the 4-pool (3 does not divide the rotation). Structurally unreachable from C —
the hook is the only path.

**Flagship recipe:** `IDO52_PRECOLOR_LIVEIN=a0:25,a1:185
IDO52_UGEN_TREG_ORDER_WINDOW=t6+t9:17:17` (drop the old `t8:56` — it bought
count parity with a spurious `sw`; occurrence is 17 without it; `t6+t9` beats a
bare `2` because t9 is the register the target actually uses). Raw body goes
1478 → exactly 1479; window [68:92] 10/24 → 24/24 mnemonic; ≥3-temp sites
[545..552]/[~1074] preserved.

**Caveats:** occurrence indices are body-sensitive — re-derive after ANY C edit
(a 1–200 sweep takes ~15s); pool=1 can starve the allocator and abort ugen
loudly (use N≥2); windows apply independently per function in a TU. **Not a
general lever**: probed inert on SHOUGATU/func_8013F570, TT/func_801489F4,
NAME_ENT/func_80140C74, TAIIKU/func_801355EC. Most promising future candidate:
TAIIKU/func_801355EC (2-temp software pipeline).

---

## ⭐ Const-web def placement: FORM_SITE at the SEED, not Hook 24 (2026-08-28)

**Read this before reaching for Hook 24.** Hook 24 addresses *block-entry
range-head reloads*. A const web whose def is hoisted to the entry block
usually has no reload at all — `IDO52_RELOAD_DUMP` and `IDO52_RELOAD_EMIT_TRACE`
print NOTHING, and the web has no colored slot in `IDO52_DUMP_WEBS`, so there is
no bitpos to aim at. Hook 24 simply cannot see it.

The lever is `IDO52_FORM_SITE=const:VAL:STMT:0` applied at the statement that
**seeds** the web.

### Diagnosis (two minutes, entirely from the trace)

```bash
IDO52_FORM_TRACE=1 cc -O2 -G0 -mips1 -EL -non_shared -c f.c -o /dev/null 2>&1 \
  | grep 'val=0xN '
```
Each line is `[FORMQ const] q=<query> stmt=<stmt> op=.. val=.. type=.. desc=.. bit=.. res=..`.

- The **seed** is the query with `bit=65535` and `res=1`. Its `stmt=` is the key.
- Every later query with `bit=<N>` is a **join** — denying those does nothing,
  because membership comes from the descriptor cache and never re-consults
  `f_constinreg`. This is why FORM_SITE denial "provably fires and changes
  nothing" when aimed at a joining statement.
- Two lines with the same `val` but different `desc=` are two descriptors, i.e.
  the u8/s8 typing split (`type=8` uchar vs `type=6` int).

### Why the seed decides the def position

The web's live range starts where it was seeded. globalcolor then places the
def at a block dominating every use — if the seed is an early, unrelated use of
the same literal, that dominator is the function entry, and the def is hoisted
far above the first real use.

### Worked example — `touzyou_check` (2026-08-28)

The literal 3 is the `% 3` divisor AND the mode constant in arms 4, 5 and 7.
`FORMQ` showed the web seeded at `stmt=9` (the modulo) with the three compares
joining. `IDO52_FORM_SITE=const:3:9:0` moved the def into arm 4 where retail
has it, **and** made the divisor a plain `li $at,3` with no divide-by-zero or
overflow checks — so it also replaced the `IDO52_CONST_NOINREG_VALUES=3:8:8`
that file previously needed for the count. One directive instead of two.

⚠️ **The seed denial can shift which literal is held.** On the same function it
exposed a second, independent split: `sub` declared `u8 *` gave the `*sub =`
store constants their own uchar descriptor, so they re-materialized instead of
reusing the compare web. Declaring that ONE parameter `s8 *` merged them
(28/216 -> 86/216); making both parameters `s8 *` dropped it to 44/216. Fix the
seed first, then re-read FORM_TRACE for the descriptor split.

## Hook 24: IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB / IDO52_CONST_DEF_EDGE — const-web def placement (2026-08-01)

Research investigation 2026-08-01. All work in a private toolchain copy
(`<scratch>/placement-hook/ido-private`); nothing in the repo was touched.
**Result: the forced-const-web def-placement blocker is SOLVED** — the
EVENT/func_8014C3A0 specimen now reproduces all 9 of the target's `li ,9`
defs at the exact target word positions with the exact target body count.

---

## 1. `f_constinreg` is PURE — "forced yes" and "native yes" are identical

Reading the recompiled `f_constinreg` (uopt.c ~193228) end-to-end: every path
converges on `MEM_U8(sp+39) = <0|1>; goto L4853ac`, and `L4853ac` only reloads
`ra`/`gp` and returns that byte. No bookkeeping, no side table, no allocation.
**The Hook-16 early return therefore cannot differ from a native yes**, and a
"context-byte override" would compute the same value. The def-placement
asymmetry recorded in Hook 16's notes has a completely different cause (§3/§4)
and that note should be corrected.

Decision inputs (confirmed by instrumented tracing, not just reading):
`a0` = operand TYPE byte (8 = ==/!= compare const, 6 = sltiu-immediate),
`a2` = value, `v1 = MEM_U8(sp+59)` = **context byte** (the caller's 5th stack
argument): ctx 0 → no; 1 → yes iff value ≥ 0x8000; 2 → yes iff < 0; 3 → yes;
4 → no; 5 → yes iff NOT a power of two. Value < −32768 or ≥ 0x10000 → yes.
Flag bytes 0x1001eabc / 0x1001eb2c and the type-indexed masks 0x200C0000 /
0x10620000 / 0x5000000 gate the always-yes / never / ctx paths.

## 2. There are exactly TWO query phases, and Hook-16 windows address them separately

Tagging all five call sites (specimen: 1214 queries) gives a clean split:

| queries | site | pass | a "yes" causes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1..607 | uopt.c 139233 (`func_4623c4`, the tree walker `makelivranges` runs) | live-range formation | `f_newbit` allocates the const's web bit (cached in the descriptor at `desc+2`, so all uses of one descriptor share ONE bit), then `f_formlivbb` marks it live in the current bb |
| 608..1214 | uopt.c 41302 (`f_gen_outparcode`) | reemission | `f_inreg` (regdata `node+0x44`) → REGOP, else `f_genloadnum` |

(139077/139095 in `func_462260` are never reached in this TU.)

So a Hook-16 occurrence index in the first half forces **web formation**, in
the second half **emission**; here the emission mirror of index N is N+34.
`9:-1:6:6` / `9:-1:16:16` are web-phase-only, which is why their uses still
emit `loadnum` — `cir=0` short-circuits before `f_inreg` is consulted.

The context byte at the live-range site is the walker's `a1` argument, set per
statement by `makelivranges` from an operator-class lookup (mask tables at
0x1000110c → 2, 0x100010fc → 1, 0x100010ec → 5, else 0/3). **ctx is a property
of the enclosing statement's ucode operator**, which is why the same literal 9
is queried with ctx 3 (native yes) at some sites and ctx 1 (native no) at
others inside one function.

## 3. Two independent def emitters

Instrumenting every `f_genrlodrstr` / `f_genloadnum` call site shows the
specimen's `li ,9` words come from exactly two places:

- **per-use `loadnum`** — `f_gen_outparcode`'s cir=0 path, and
- **range-head defs** — a *block-entry reload loop*: for each color 1..35 it
  reads the block node's regdata slot `node + 0x44 + (color-1)*4` and tests a
  bit in a per-block **reload bitmap** at `node + 208`
  (byte `((color-1)>>3)`, bit `7 - ((color-1)&7)`, MSB-first — the same bit
  order the WEB_BIND_BBS hook uses for `node+0x34`). Slot non-zero **and** bit
  set → `f_genrlodrstr(0x6d, color, ichain)` → `f_genloadnum` → `li color,K`
  as the block's FIRST instruction.

There is also a third, edge-based path (`f_put_in_fallthru_bb` /
`f_put_in_jump_bb` → per-block pending lists at `+236` / `+240` / `+228`,
consumed by reemit's `func_425188`), which `globalcolor` uses for its own
PRE-style placements. In the specimen those lists are empty for the const webs
— **the range-head defs are entirely the `+208` bitmap.**

Verification: clearing the whole `+208` bitmap post-globalcolor drops the body
from 1716 to 1696 words and removes 7 of the 10 `li ,9` defs; an
`IDO52_RELOAD_DUMP` listing of (bb, color, ichain) reproduces exactly the 7
traced `f_genrlodrstr` calls for web 964.

## 4. Hook 24 — `IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB` (+ `IDO52_CONST_DEF_EDGE`)

`IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB=BITPOS:BB[:+|-][,...]`, applied post-globalcolor:
- `-` clears the reload bit → **suppresses** that block's range-head def;
- `+` (default) writes the regdata slot with the web's ichain and sets the bit
  → **adds** a range-head def at that block's first instruction.

Pairing `-` on the natural block with `+` on a dominating block **moves** the
def. Because a block's first instruction is emitted immediately after the
predecessor's branch word, a `+` on the block that starts right after a bail
branch produces exactly the target's shape: the def sits in the branch's
**delay slot**, executes on both the taken and fall-through paths, and
therefore dominates every use in the region — including the ones the bail edge
jumps to. Once the dominating def exists, the downstream per-region defs can be
`-`-suppressed and the extra merge-point def disappears.

`IDO52_CONST_DEF_EDGE=BITPOS:BB[:f|j|e]` is the companion for the pending-list
paths. It provably emits real defs (e.g. `964:216:f` adds a `li ,9` at word
708), but a def dominated by an existing def of the same web is
dead-code-eliminated by ugen, so DEF_BB — which can also *remove* the
dominating def — is the tool for range-head placement. The `e` (`+228`) form is
inert: that consumer reaches the block node by a different indirection.

Both hooks drive uopt's own emitters; **no output word is ever patched**.
Both refuse an occupied color slot by default (`*_FORCE` overrides).

### `IDO52_CONST_DEF_EDGE_FORCE` / `IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB_FORCE` — the occupancy-guard overrides (documented 2026-09-26)

Both live in `apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py`, inside the same insertion
functions as the hooks they override; any non-empty value enables them
(`=1` by convention).  They are global for the TU, not per entry, env-gated,
default-off and inert when unset (the guard code only runs when the parent
hook has entries).  Neither adds an emitter of its own — the record still
goes through uopt's `f_put_in_*_bb` pending lists / the `+208` reload
bitmap, so nothing is patched into the output.

- **`IDO52_CONST_DEF_EDGE_FORCE`** skips the EDGE hook's interference walk.
  By default, before appending the pending record for bitpos B on block BB,
  the hook visits every other web's live-range and refuses when ANOTHER web
  already has B's colour (`lr+0x20`) AND is live in BB (its LiveUnit chain
  names BB): `[CONST-DEF-EDGE] bitpos B: bb BB color C OCCUPIED by another
  live web, skip`.  Defining C on that edge would clobber a value
  globalcolor proved non-interfering.  With FORCE set the walk is skipped
  and the record is appended regardless.  The other preconditions stay: the
  bitpos must have a liverange, a usable colour (1..35) and an ichain, and BB
  must be in the block index.
- **`IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB_FORCE`** is the `+` form's equivalent: by default a
  `+` refuses when the block's regdata slot for that colour already holds a
  DIFFERENT ichain (`[CONST-DEF-BB] bitpos B: bb BB color C slot OCCUPIED
  (ichain), skip`); with FORCE the slot is overwritten.  The `-` form has no
  guard and needs no override.  No user at the time of writing.

**Soundness is the caller's obligation** — FORCE is only legitimate when the
occupancy report is a false positive for the position the def actually
lands on: the other web's range covers the block but not the edge, or it is
dead on every path from the inserted def to its uses.  Check the final
object for every read of the register after the inserted def, and record
the check in the header.  One user, `src/main/matched/func_800A1E40.c`
(`IDO52_CONST_DEF_EDGE=134:25:j,134:26:j IDO52_CONST_DEF_EDGE_FORCE=1`
alongside `CONST_DEF_BB=134:39:-,134:32:-`): bb26 reported colour 4 (`$a1`)
OCCUPIED; the header records why the force is sound (after 0x800A2014 every
read of `a1` is an `addu tX,a1,..` of the ring base, each path to one passes
a ring-base def on the n<=0 edge / rem==0 jump block / rem preheader, and
the len==0 arm redefines `a1` itself).  Bitposes and bb numbers shift with
every env change, so re-derive them rather than copying that line.
BB numbers are Hook 19's numbering and are **uopt-side**: verified byte-identical
reload maps with and without `IDO52_UGEN_TREG_ORDER_WINDOW=t6+t9:15:15`, so
that ugen temp-rotation knob composes freely with these.

### Workflow

1. `IDO52_RELOAD_DUMP=1` → list every `(bb, color, ichain)` range-head reload.
   Pick the web's ichain (correlate with `IDO52_DUMP_WEBS` / the trace-5 bitpos).
2. Compile once and locate the block that owns the unwanted def: it is the
   block whose FIRST instruction is that `li`.
3. The wanted block is the one whose first instruction is at the target's def
   position; blocks are numbered in emission order, so it is normally the block
   starting right after the bail branch (2 conditional branches earlier in the
   specimen).
4. `IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB=<bitpos>:<natural>:-,<bitpos>:<wanted>:+`.
5. **Soundness is the caller's obligation**: a `-` is only legal when another
   def of the same web dominates every use the removed def served. Verify the
   emitted window against the target and check there is no intervening write to
   the allocated register on any path that reaches a use.

### Worked example — EVENT/func_8014C3A0 (the project's largest function)

```
IDO52_CONST_INREG_VALUES=9:-1:6:6,9:-1:16:16,12:-1:58:58   (existing @CompileEnv)
IDO52_WEB_BIND_BBS=952:402                                  (existing @CompileEnv)
IDO52_UGEN_TREG_ORDER_WINDOW=t6+t9:15:15                    (Hook 23)
IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB=964:101:-,964:99:+,964:108:-,964:228:-,964:226:+
```
- web bitpos 964 = the `{9|0}` compare-const web, color 3.
- g==2 arm: natural range-head def at bb101 (word 348) moved to bb99 (word
  **344**, the `bne st,10` delay slot); the merge-point def at bb108 (word 371)
  suppressed — it is now dominated by the bb99 def.
- girl==5 arm: bb228 (word 753) → bb226 (word **749**).

| | body | raw exact | `li ,9` positions |
|---|---|---|---|
| target | 1712 | — | `324 344 459 730 749 927 941 1125 1335` |
| @CompileEnv only | 1712 | 502 | `323 347 370 459 730 753 927 941 1125 1335` |
| + TREG window | 1716 | 128 | `324 348 371 460 731 754 928 942 1126 1336` |
| **+ CONST_DEF_BB** | **1712** | **708** | `324 344 459 730 749 927 941 1125 1335` ✅ |

Windows `[335..380]` and `[740..765]` are **mnemonic-identical to the target**
except for the pre-existing arm-top `lui/lbu` load-triple *interleave*
(`lui,lui,lui,lbu,lbu,lbu` vs `lui,lbu,lui,lbu,lui,lbu` — the documented
`swap_instructions` reorder residual, unrelated to def placement). Everything
else differs only in register fields and relocation immediates (the paintable
class): the def is `addiu $a0,$zero,9` where the target has
`addiu $a1,$zero,9`.

Soundness of that window was checked by hand: every path that reaches the
`st==9` use at word 371 (the three bail branches at 343/346/348 and the guards
at 352/356/358/360) passes through word 344 and contains no write to `$a0`;
the only `$a0` clobber (word 365, the `func_80011E28(96)` argument) is on the
fall-through path that leaves via the unconditional `b` at 369.

## 5. Regression discipline

With `IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB` / `IDO52_CONST_DEF_EDGE` unset the patched toolchain
is bit-identical to the unpatched private build: **22/22 byte_perfect
EVENT+DATE functions**, each compiled with its own `@CompileEnv`, including
**6 that use `IDO52_CONST_INREG_VALUES`** (3 EVENT, 3 DATE).
Evidence: `regression_evidence.txt`.

## 6. Still open

- The specimen's remaining residuals are unchanged by this work: the arm-top
  `lui/lbu` interleave (17 reorder windows, `swap_instructions`), the
  `xori`/`xor` codemotion-availability case at word 1294, the stack-slot remap,
  and the register-field diffs for the paint endgame.
- The BB number of the "wanted" block must be found empirically (compile, read
  `IDO52_RELOAD_DUMP`, locate the block by its first instruction). A
  bb-number → emitted-word-index dump would make this mechanical.

## 7. Hook 24b — the block index now covers post-split webs (2026-09-25)

**Defect.** `ido52_apply_const_def_edge` builds its bb → graph-node index by
walking every web's LiveUnit chain, and it bounded that walk by
`0x1001c8a4`. That is the **pre-colour** web count. Webs cut off by `f_split`
during `f_globalcolor`, and by Hooks 34/39/43/60, get their bitpos from
`f_newbit`, which grows **`0x1001cad8`** instead (the count Hooks 37/38 and
`ido52_gc_bitpos_of_lr` already use). A block in which only such a web is live
was never indexed. `CONST_DEF_EDGE` then printed `bb N not found`,
`CONST_DEF_BB` printed `bb N unusable (… nd=00000000)`, and `RELOAD_DUMP` was
silent for those blocks. The reproducer is main/func_800A26AC: its s0/s1 mask
webs are bitpos 42/43 with `0x1001c8a4` = 31, so bb 10/11/13/22/23 could not
be addressed.

**Fix** (`apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py`, fresh-apply text plus a
`HOOK24B_UPGRADES` in-place upgrade for a `uopt.c` that already carries
Hook 24; idempotent, prints `applied: Hook24b` once):
- the index walk runs to `max(0x1001c8a4, 0x1001cad8)`, clamped at 100000;
- every read is probed with `ido52_mem_readable`: the bittab entry (the walk
  stops at the first unmapped one), the liverange, each LiveUnit, and the node;
- the first node seen for a block wins, so a later entry cannot displace it;
- the `CONST_DEF_EDGE` interference guard ("another web of this colour is live
  in the target block") scans the same extended range, so a post-split web
  holding the colour now blocks the def, as it should.

**Inert when unset.** The function returns before the index is built unless a
`CONST_DEF_BB`/`CONST_DEF_EDGE` entry is configured. Gate after the rebuild:
`build_level3_c.py --no-cache` Mismatched 0, SHA1
2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b. All 19 functions in the regression
set are BYTE-PERFECT, including both Hook 24 users, EVENT/func_8014C3A0 and
main/func_800A0210, whose entries are all pre-split webs.

**First consumer.** func_800A26AC native-as1 recipe: **115/117, Body 117,
Fixup 0** on the shared compiler (52/117 is shipped). The residual is 2 words,
`subu v0,t2,t3 ; andi a0,v0,0x3f` against retail's
`subu a0,t2,t3 ; andi a0,a0,0x3f`. See that file's header for the recipe and
the recipe. What was measured on the residual (scratch copies only):
- ugen emits `subu $2 ; and $2,$2,63 ; move $4,$2 ; beq $2`. The masked value
  is its own web (bitpos 31, v0), and `n` (bitpos 28, a0) is a copy of it. as1
  folds the move into `andi a0,v0`, which leaves the `subu` in v0.
- `UGEN_DEST_TEMP=27` puts the `subu` in a pool temp (t4). Adding Hook 36c
  `TEMP_REG=19:a0` + `TEMP_REG_INPLACE=occ:19` gives `subu a0 ; and $2,$4,63`,
  but as1 then does NOT fold the move (`andi v0,a0 ; beqz v0 ; move a0,v0`).
  With `DEST_TEMP=27,28` and `TEMP_REG=19:a0,20:a0` ugen emits
  `subu $4 ; and $4,$4 ; move $2,$4 ; move $4,$2`, and as1 folds the and into
  v0. The as1 copy-prop trace shows the `move a0,v0` candidate as `allowed` in
  every case, so as1's own legality test is what declines the fold.
- `FORCE_WEB_COLOR …,31:a0` is accepted and gives `subu a0 ; andi a1,a0`,
  because `n` is pushed to a1 (110/117). Colouring 28 and 31 both a0 is
  refused, because the webs interfere. The only overlap is `n = w31`: from the
  copy to the `beq` they hold the same value. Closing the residual therefore
  needs a **copy-coalesce** colour force, one that ignores interference which
  exists only between a copy's source and destination. No such primitive
  exists.
- Inert spellings: `n = a - b; n &= 0x3f`, `n = a; n = (n - b) & 0x3f`, and
  `!(n == 0)`, all 115/117. `n > 0` scores 114. `if (n)` and `% 64` give
  Body 115. `u32 n` renumbers the webs (90/117 with the fitted keys).

## Hook 25: IDO52_CMOT_KILL_AVAIL -- per-block codemotion availability break (2026-08-01)

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_CMOT_KILL_AVAIL` | `IDX[:BB][,...]` | `IDO52_CMOT_KILL_AVAIL=714:402,715:402` |
| `IDO52_CMOT_AVAIL_TRACE` | `1` (or `2`) | `IDO52_CMOT_AVAIL_TRACE=1` |

Clears expression `IDX` from basic block `BB`'s codemotion **ppin** set
(`block+0x12c`) at the head of `f_codemotion`'s final per-block loop (label
`L4105bc`), after `ppin |= subinsert` and before
`subdelete = (ppin - X) INTERSECT antlocs` is formed. The block therefore keeps
its own local computation instead of reusing the value computed by an earlier
block. Omit `:BB` (or pass `-1`) to kill the expression in every block.
Default OFF; unset, the compiler is bit-identical to the unhooked build.

NOTE (2026-08-01): Hooks 24/25 are applied by tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py (Hook 24, runs on the generated uopt.c AFTER apply_uopt_hooks.py) and apply_uopt_hooks.py itself (Hook 25). The post-clean rebuild chain is now: rm build/5.2/uopt.c && make VERSION=5.2 c_files && python3 apply_uopt_hooks.py && python3 apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c && python3 apply_ugen_hooks.py && python3 apply_as1_hooks.py && make VERSION=5.2. First consumer + full worked derivation: EVENT/func_8014C3A0 (BYTE-PERFECT 2026-08-01, see its EOF notes; also documents the IDO line-number tie-break doctrine — directives must live BELOW the code).

## Hook 26: IDO52_RELOAD_SUPPRESS — emission-order range-head reload veto (2026-08-01)

IDO52_RELOAD_SUPPRESS (range-head reload veto, occurrence-addressed).
 *     Format: BITPOS:N[,BITPOS:N...].  Skips the N-th (1-based) block-entry
 *     reload emission of the web at BITPOS.  Addresses the SAME emitter as
 *     IDO52_CONST_DEF_BB ':-' but at the emission point instead of the
 *     per-block bitmap, so it reaches blocks that are not in the
 *     LiveUnit-derived bb index (a block where no web is live has no
 *     LiveUnit and therefore no bb number to name).  Enumerate the
 *     occurrences with IDO52_RELOAD_EMIT_TRACE.
#define RELOAD_SUP_MAX 16
static struct { int bitpos; int n; } ido52_reload_sup[RELOAD_SUP_MAX];
static int ido52_reload_sup_count;
static uint32_t ido52_reload_seen_ic[64];
static int ido52_reload_seen_n[64];
static int ido52_reload_seen_count;'''


| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_RELOAD_SUPPRESS` | `BITPOS:N[,...]` (N = 1-based emission order per web) | `IDO52_RELOAD_SUPPRESS=879:4` |
| `IDO52_RELOAD_EMIT_TRACE` | presence enables | lists every range-head reload as `[RLD-EMIT] n= bb= color= ichain=` |

Vetoes the N-th block-entry range-head reload of a const web AT THE EMISSION
POINT, so it reaches blocks Hook 24's LiveUnit-derived bb index cannot name
(a bb with no LiveUnit in any web — e.g. DATE/func_80132728's bb 408). Can
only REMOVE an emission, never invent a word. Soundness is the caller's
obligation (the value must genuinely reach every use on every path — verify
against the target bytes). First win: DATE/func_80132728 1478/1479 -> 
BYTE-PERFECT with zero register drift, refuting its filed const_web_ra_survival
diagnosis (the web needed no re-colouring — only its redundant 4th reload
removed). Generalised 3-step recipe for any 'one extra li' residual:
(1) IDO52_RELOAD_EMIT_TRACE + the Q1 bb-trace to identify whether the extra
def is a Q1-loadnum (-> cir window + WEB_BIND combo), a bb-indexed reload
(-> Hook 24 ':-'), or an unindexed-bb reload (-> this hook); (2) apply the
matching knob; (3) strict full-length byte compare. Applied by
apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py (auto-detects and grafts onto a Hook-24 uopt.c).

---

## Hook 27: as1 cross-BB boost controls — IDO52_AS1_XBB_BUDGET / XBB_VOLATILE_OK / NO_FALLTHRU_FILL (2026-08-01)

Applied by tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_delay_hooks.py (run AFTER
apply_as1_hooks.py in the rebuild chain). as1's f_do_xbb_opt is the ONLY
pass that can fill a load-use interlock bubble or take a branch-delay
occupant from the branch-TARGET successor; it fails silently when (a) its
10-candidate per-destination budget is spent before the wanted successor
is scanned (IDO52_AS1_XBB_BUDGET=<n> overrides) or (b) the instruction
carries the .set volatile pin byte (+33) that a C-side volatile lever
created (IDO52_AS1_XBB_VOLATILE_OK=all|N[,N..] — occurrence-indexed,
only WIDENS the search; legality/profitability still decided by as1).
IDO52_AS1_XBB_TRACE=1|2 enumerates candidates/occurrences;
IDO52_AS1_NO_FALLTHRU_FILL=all|N vetoes the fall-through delay fill;
IDO52_AS1_BOPT_TRACE traces the branch-likely converter (mips1: inert).
First win: ENDING/func_8013A318 (1,162 insns) +2 surplus -> BYTE-PERFECT.
23/23-object bit-identical regression with env unset (incl. 9 files
using existing AS1 hooks). Env-off = bit-identical is the standing gate.

## Hook 28: IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB — per-web pre-gc forbidden-color additions (2026-08-02)

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB` | `BITPOS:reg[+reg...][,...]` | `IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB=442:s0+s1+s2+s3+s4+s5+s6+s7+s8` |
| `IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB_DEBUG` | presence | prints `forbid 0xOLD -> 0xNEW` per hit |

ORs extra colors into ONE web's forbidden bitvector (`lr+40`) at the head of
`f_globalcolor`'s per-web loop (label `L46e804`, same site as the old — and
still-inert — global `IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS`), BEFORE `f_updatecolorsleft` /
`f_isconstrained` run. The web is matched by its bittab liverange pointer
(`bittab[bitpos*8+4] == s5`), so only the named web is affected and the
coloring decision itself — including range splitting, spill choices of OTHER
webs, and range-head reload emission — re-derives around the constraint.
This is the missing primitive from the TEL/func_8013D758 wall ("make s7/s8
unavailable to the 5-web so globalcolor splits it") and differs from every
prior lever: PRECOLOR_WEB's effective path is post-gc rebind (no re-color
cascade), NOINREG windows kill the register form entirely (unchecked $at
divs), and the global EXCLUDE_REGS mask uses machine-reg bit numbering that
the forbidden field does not read (verified inert).

Encodings: reg names parse in the PRECOLOR rc space (v0=1..t9=16,
s0=17..s8/fp=25), and the forbidden bitvector at `lr+40` is indexed **by that
same rc value**, in MSB-first (inverted) bit order — `1u << (~rc & 31)`.
Ground truth is `f_updatecolorsleft`, which reads
`word = lr + 40 + 4*(colour>>5)` and takes the colour when
`(word << (colour & 31)) < 0`, i.e. bit `31 - colour`.

⛔ **2026-08-09 CORRECTION — the parser was OFF BY THREE.** It used to store
`1u << (~(rc-3) & 31)`, on the (wrong) theory that the vector uses the
printregs "dump space". It does not: only the *listing* does. The effect was
that a spec named the colour THREE SLOTS TOO HIGH — **`t3` forbade `$t0`**.
Proven by sweep on a fixture: at the single bitpos where the hook bit, only
the spec `t3` moved a web coloured `$t0`; after the fix only `t0` does, and
the live DEBUG mask decodes back to the named register for t0/t3/s5/a2.
Round-trip check: `python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/test_hook28_regspec.py
[--live]`.
**Any `@CompileEnv: IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB=` written before 2026-08-09 was
authored against the buggy offset and now means a different register set** —
low names (v0..a3, rc 1..3) even wrapped around to colours 29..31. Re-derive
the spec rather than trusting the old one.

The `>>>active<<<` dump's color column IS dump-space: active_color + 3 = rc
(e.g. active 19 = s5, active 22 = s8) — that is the listing convention that
misled the original encoding note, and it does not apply to `lr+40`.

First result (TEL/func_8013D758, the +21 const-5-web wall): excluding all
s-regs for the 5-web (bitpos 442) and the base-pointer web (285) flipped the
whole allocation to the target's shape in one step — 29 `li v0,5` range-head
reload defs (target has ~30), the `year` param un-spilled into its s-reg, the
D_800EE760 base left s4 — raw count 798 -> 796 with the diff hunks now the
target's own structure. Regression: env-off text/reloc/rodata/data sections
bit-identical; GEKO/func_80142BCC re-verified ✅ BYTE-PERFECT.

Applied by apply_uopt_hooks.py (statics + parser + L46e804 site extension are
all mirrored there; rebuild chain unchanged).

## Hook 29: IDO52_AS1_KEEP_ANDI — as1 narrow-op simplifier veto (2026-08-06)

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_ANDI` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N...]` (0-based; a bare number is an index, NOT all) | `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_ANDI=occ:38,39` |
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_ANDI_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-KANDI] #N word=XXXXXXXX [-> VETOED]` |

**This is the KEEP_NARROW_TRUNC closer (TODO item 0), and the banked spec's
"uopt narrowing lowering" attribution was WRONG.** Stage-A/B proof on a
2-shape minimal repro: cfe/uopt keep the AND node; ugen EMITS the target's
`andi rd,rs,0xff` (+ write-back move); **as1 -O2's `func_416ca8`** — the
pass-II andi/ori narrow-op simplifier inside `f_do_passII_opt`/`func_419268`
— decays it to a move (which forward copy-prop then deletes; that is why
`IDO52_AS1_NO_COPY_PROP=all` keeps a `move` but never the `andi`, and why
as1 -O1 keeps both andis). Found by env-gated bisect over the 13
pass-II sub-transforms.

The veto sits at func_416ca8's word-examination point (label L416cf4, after
the +34 movability guard) and takes the function's own `return 0` no-change
exit. Candidates are counted per translation unit; pass II iterates, so one
source andi typically appears TWICE (veto both, e.g. `occ:38,39`). Same
index-shift caveat as NO_COPY_PROP. Applied by apply_as1_hooks.py (Hook 8
section). env-off = bit-identical (8-function regression incl. files using

## Hook 30: IDO52_FORM_SITE / IDO52_EMIT_SITE / IDO52_FORM_TRACE — site-keyed pre-colouring web-formation control (2026-08-08)

Applied by **`tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_precolor_hooks.py`** (run
AFTER `apply_uopt_hooks.py` and `apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py`; the post-clean
rebuild chain gains one step):

```bash
rm build/5.2/uopt.c && make VERSION=5.2 c_files
python3 apply_uopt_hooks.py
python3 apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c
python3 apply_uopt_precolor_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c      # <-- NEW
python3 apply_uopt_copyprop_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c      # <-- Hook 32
python3 apply_ugen_hooks.py && python3 apply_as1_hooks.py
python3 apply_as1_delay_hooks.py build/5.2/as1.c
make VERSION=5.2
```

⚠️ **Functions whose `@CompileEnv` uses `IDO52_FORM_SITE`/`IDO52_EMIT_SITE`
(EVENT/func_80151714, NAME_ENT/func_80135174) require this patch in the
shared toolchain** — an unpatched uopt silently ignores the vars and the
verify demotes them.

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_FORM_TRACE` | `1` or `2` | `IDO52_FORM_TRACE=1` |
| `IDO52_FORM_SITE` | `kind:val:stmt[-stmtTo]:res[,...]` | `IDO52_FORM_SITE=const:2:28:0` |
| `IDO52_EMIT_SITE` | `const:val:bb:occ:res[,...]` | `IDO52_EMIT_SITE=const:0:63:1:1` |

Default OFF; with all three unset every shim is a read-only no-op —
bit-identical proven on the TEL baseline and a 12-function byte_perfect
regression across 8 overlays (GEKO, DATE, EVENT, BUNKA_SD, ETC, SHUGAKU,
ENDING, BUNKASAI), each compiled with its own hook-heavy `@CompileEnv`.

### The model: web formation is a per-STATEMENT query stream

`f_makelivranges` walks the optimized itab statement list; for each operand
the recursive walker `func_4623c4` queries **register candidacy** —
`f_constinreg` at its const leaf case (L462438), `f_ldainreg` at its LDA leaf
case (L462524). A *yes* allocates the web bit (`f_newbit`, cached at
`desc+2` so all uses of one descriptor share a bit) and marks it live in the
current bb (`f_formlivbb`). These formation-phase verdicts decide whether a
value is a web **before globalcolor ever runs** — the pre-colouring class.

The hook maintains a **statement ordinal** (reset per proc, incremented at
the statement-loop head L4669b8). Because the itab walk is fixed input, the
ordinal does NOT feedback-renumber when queries are forced — the property
every occurrence-window sweep lacked (NAME_ENT W2's 410-compile 2-D sweep,
EVENT/func_80151714's 17-combo sweep). Ordinals DO shift on any source
edit: re-derive with `IDO52_FORM_TRACE=1` after touching the C.

### Trace format

```
[FORMQ const] q=9 stmt=28 op=0x26 val=0x2 type=6 ctx=3 desc=0x1005f1b8 bit=65535 res=1
[FORMQ lda]   q=4 stmt=72 op=0x64 off=0xfffffefc class=1 ctx=0 desc=0x1005def8 bit=65535 res=1
[NEWBIT walk_const] bit=32 lr=0x1005f1b8 a1=0x0 stmt=28
[EMITQ const] val=0x0 bb=63 res=0
[FORMLIV s8] bit=15 desc=0x1005dec8 bb=0x1005d518 stmt=82      (trace level 2)
```

- `stmt` = statement ordinal (the site key); `op` = that statement's ucode
  opcode (0x7b store, 0x64 call-ish, 0x26 compare/branch, 0x7f loop-test…).
- `bit=65535` = descriptor has no web bit yet; a res=1 then allocates one.
- `[NEWBIT <site>]` fires at ALL 11 `f_newbit` call sites (`isearch1/2`,
  `trep_image`, `searchstore`, `walk_const`, `walk_lda`, `walk_3`,
  `f463d04`, `f465580`, `split`) — bits created at `isearch*` (stmt=0)
  predate makelivranges: they are the CSE-append-time candidacy switch.
- `[FORMLIV sN]` (level 2) attributes every liveness mark to its caller
  site: s13 ≈ first-computation mark, s8 ≈ expression REUSE mark — the
  marks that extend a live range across calls.
- `lda` queries print the sp-relative frame offset as `off` (class 1) or
  0 for symbol LDAs (class 4).

### Semantics of the override

- `IDO52_FORM_SITE=const:VAL:STMT:0` — deny web formation for value VAL at
  that statement: the use does not join/extend the web, the live range
  starts (or the value rematerializes) elsewhere.
- `...:1` — force formation: web bit allocated + liveness marked there.
  ⚠️ A formation FORCE usually needs its **emission mirror** forced too
  (Hook 24 §2's two-phase split): `IDO52_EMIT_SITE=const:VAL:BB:OCC:1`
  forces the emission-phase `f_constinreg` verdict at the OCC-th query of
  (VAL, BB) so `f_inreg` is consulted (REGOP when bound). All matching
  entries' occurrence counters advance on every query, so multiple windows
  over one (val,bb) share one index space. BB numbers are the Q1 trace's
  `bb=` field. There is no emission-side LDA query (f_ldainreg is
  formation-only); denied LDAs recompute at each use naturally.

### Wins (all resolver ✅ BYTE-PERFECT, len t=c, this investigation)

| Function | Recipe | What it proves |
|---|---|---|
| **EVENT/func_80151714** (48/48) | `IDO52_CONST_INREG_VALUES=1,2,3 IDO52_FORM_SITE=const:2:28:0` | The web/compare SPLIT no occurrence window could express: denying the COMPARE statement's formation query makes the const-2 range start inside the guard (the target's late `li t0,2` def) while the compare re-emits the `$at` literal. Retired from `c_unmatchable.txt` (`held_constant`). |
| **NAME_ENT/func_80135174** (220/220) | `IDO52_FORM_SITE=lda:0:142:0,lda:0:155:0,lda:0:168:0` | The "symbol-windowed global-LDA demotion" wanted-hook, verbatim: three one-query end-pointer descs denied at their loop-compare statements; D_800B9B30 keeps s1, s2 save/restore vanishes. (Plus sanctioned scoped `cse_lui_at` for the documented as1 lui-share gap.) |
| **TEL/func_80139DC8** (87/87) | `IDO52_CMOT_KILL_AVAIL=15,17` (Hook 25!) | The trace, not the override, closed this: it showed the sp-buffer webs are created at `isearch1` (f_ldainreg's verdict is IGNORED — blocking class 1 is bit-identical), and kept alive by s8 REUSE marks whose availability is codemotion-derived — i.e. reachable by the EXISTING Hook 25. Also surfaced two C misreads (frame pads, case-3 `return`). |

### Measured limits (honest negatives)

- **Zero-cost single-block webs (NAME_ENT/func_8014343C W2)**: FORM_SITE
  force + EMIT_SITE force both verifiably fire, but globalcolor's cost
  model refuses to colour a one-block web of constant 0 ($zero is free):
  no def, `IDO52_RELOAD_DUMP` empty, output bit-identical. The 1995
  `move t3,zero` implies their COPY-PROP kept `n` a real variable — W2 is
  a copy-prop-divergence wall; wanted primitive: site-keyed copy-prop veto
  in `f_copypropagate` (unbuilt).
- **ixa stride coupling (GEKO/func_80134A40)**: the global-LDA promote IS
  a formation query (refuting the TAIIKU-2/GEKO "never queried" notes) and
  single-site denial kills the $fp hoist — but the ixa lowering then
  abandons the sll/subu/sll ×56 form for `li;multu;mflo`, exactly as
  class-wide blocking did. Wall narrowed to the stride-lowering side.
- **Call-return carrier colouring (RPG_BAT/func_80144490 `precolor_carrier`)**:
  variable/return webs form without any candidacy query — out of this
  hook's key space entirely (their bits come from `isearch*`/`searchstore`).
- The TEL finding generalizes a diagnostic rule: **an "LDA_NOINREG is
  inert" probe log does NOT mean the address never forms a web** — check
  `[NEWBIT isearch1]` for the ichain; if the bit predates makelivranges,
  the availability levers (Hook 25) are the reachable surface.

## Hook 32: IDO52_COPYPROP_TRACE / IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE — site-keyed copy-fold veto, two families (2026-08-08)

Applied by **`tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_copyprop_hooks.py`** (run
AFTER `apply_uopt_precolor_hooks.py`; the post-clean rebuild chain gains
one step):

```bash
rm build/5.2/uopt.c && make VERSION=5.2 c_files
python3 apply_uopt_hooks.py
python3 apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c
python3 apply_uopt_precolor_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c
python3 apply_uopt_copyprop_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c   # <-- NEW (Hook 32)
python3 apply_ugen_hooks.py && python3 apply_as1_hooks.py
python3 apply_as1_delay_hooks.py build/5.2/as1.c
make VERSION=5.2
```

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_COPYPROP_TRACE` | `1` or `2` | `IDO52_COPYPROP_TRACE=2` |
| `IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE` | `stmt[:deny]` \| `lod:q[:deny]` (comma list; `*` = all) | `IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE=96,lod:15` |

Default OFF; with both unset every shim is a read-only no-op —
bit-identical proven TWICE on a 12-object direct-`cc` A/B (TEL x4, GEKO
x3, NAME_ENT, EVENT, TT, DATE, ENDING — incl. Hook 25/30 + as1-hook
`@CompileEnv` users), plus a 12-function `test_overlay_match` regression
across 7 overlays (TEL/NAME_ENT/EVENT/GEKO x4/DATE x2/ENDING/RPG_BAT),
all `✅ BYTE-PERFECT` after the rebuild.

### ⭐ THE HEADLINE FINDING: "f_copypropagate folds the copy" is REFUTED for the cross-bb class

The wanted-hook was filed (three consumers) as a site-keyed veto in
`f_copypropagate`.  Building the trace disproved the attribution:

- `f_copypropagate` (pass entry L416734) is the **"COPY PROPAGATION" +
  "REMOVAL OF REDUNDANT STORES"** phase pair in the `-l` listing.  Its
  statement walk (head L416a58) rebuilds per-statement expression
  *images* via the memoized builder `func_414394` (variable-leaf case
  L414420: reuse the recorded node at entry+20 iff stamp entry+24 ==
  global 0x1001cacc) and records store images via `f_searchstore`
  (L416b58); its tail sweep (L419358, the `NO_DEADSTORE_GLOBALS` site)
  deletes redundant stores.
- **On GEKO/func_80134A40's `cand = best` fold, the ENTIRE pass is
  output-inert**: denying the copy's store record (stmt 96), denying
  EVERY statement (`IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE=*`), all produce bit-identical
  objects.  The tail sweep kills 7 stores on that function — the copy is
  not among them.
- The fold is decided BETWEEN this pass and `f_makelivranges`: the copy
  statement still exists in the itab at reg-alloc prep (probe-verified),
  but with statement flags +29/+31 == 0 (the dead-store source cure —
  `best = 0;` after the copy — flips them to 1/1 and the copy then forms
  a web and survives).  Forcing the flags at the makelivranges loop head
  is inert — they are consumed earlier (setter cluster: the loop-analysis
  family `f_oneloopblockstmt`/`f_oneloopblockexpr` at ~167k, and the
  `*killprev`/`*killed` family at ~107k).  **The cross-bb fold's true
  decision site remains unlocated — the GEKO consumer is NOT closable by
  this hook**; the flag trail above is the next campaign's starting
  point.

### What Hook 32 actually provides

**Family 1 — `stmt` (f_copypropagate walk veto).**  Site key = statement
ordinal from the fixed walk (reset per proc at L416734, ++ at L416a58;
`[CPSTMT]` at trace level 2 lists them).  `deny` zeroes the statement's
image: STORE path (L416ae8) → no `f_searchstore` record (`[CPS store]
... res=DENY`); two-operand path (L416e48) both images zeroed; leaf memo
reuse (L414420) forced MISS.  Behaviorally identical to a natural
stamp-mismatch (= the dead-store cure's in-pass effect).  Built, proven
side-effect-clean — kept for the sub-class that IS in-pass (RPG_BAT
join-phi `ules{node|0}` guards; untested on a live specimen).

**Family 2 — `lod:q` (bb-local isearch LOD-substitution gate; the
same-bb copy-collapse mechanism).**  In `f_readnxtinst` (the LOCAL-opt
itab builder, opcode dispatch at L43e33c), a load of a scalar variable
whose last in-bb store recorded a still-valid value tree REUSES that
tree — the use references the RHS item and the intermediate variable
evaporates.  Gate = L43e8f0 (screens: `f_bigtree` > 20 nodes,
`f_treekilled`, and kind-4/count-1 `f_has_ilod`/`f_constexp` routes);
natural fail path = L43e9f0 (fresh var-load item + value-was-read mark).
`deny` takes exactly that path.  Trace: `[CPQ lod] q=N ent=... kind=3
off=<frame-offset> mt=... val=... vkind=... vkilled=... stmt=...` — `q`
is a GLOBAL arrival ordinal (ticks on both verdicts and only while
TRACE/SITE set, so denial never renumbers later sites; it is per-uopt-run,
not per-proc — fine for single-function overlay files).  Variable
identity: `off` = entry+40 (frame offset), `mt` = entry+44
(memtype:block).  Entry field map (probe-verified): +0 kind (3=isvar,
6=ilod), +52 current-value tree, +56 last-store stmt record; stmt flags
+29/+31 = value-usable (cleared by the `*killprev` walk on alteration —
the dead-store blocker's actual lever at build time).

### Consumer verdicts (2026-08-08 build investigation)

| Consumer | Verdict |
|---|---|
| GEKO/func_80134A40 (`cand = best`, cross-bb: uses after calls) | **Out of reach of both families** (fold decided post-copypropagate, pre-makelivranges; see headline finding). 106/108 wall stands; honest semi unchanged. |
| TT/func_8013D988 (same-bb write-back or-copies) | **Gate is live and site-active**: 39 `[CPQ lod]` arrivals; full single-q sweep found q13 (+0.3% exact), q15 (const-record deny, +2 words = the `or $s2,$zero` family, but with remat surplus → symbol-window overrun), q4/5/6 regressive; `lod:*` = exact 290/290 window count (was 288/290) at 7.2%. The 2-word copy deficit is REACHABLE but couples to rematerialization; needs per-site pairing with the scheduling residual — left for the TT owner with the sweep table in the file's wall note. |
| NAME_ENT/func_8014343C W2 (`move t3,zero`) | Trace-only (file being edited elsewhere): 85 arrivals; denying the two loads sharing a const-0 value item (q41/42 on the current shape) flips a zero-move web's REGISTER (00001825→00004025 + web reshuffle, same size) — the gate is live in this function but those q's are not W2's site; W2's n-loads need the owner's correlation sweep (ordinals shift on any source edit — re-derive). |

### Practical notes

- Derive `q`/`stmt` ordinals with the SAME env family you will deny with
  (the lod counter only ticks while TRACE or SITE is set — trace-run and
  deny-run ordinals match; env-unset runs never tick).
- Ordinals shift on ANY source edit — re-derive after touching the C.
- `IDO52_SKIP_PASSES=copypropagate` CRASHES uopt (code 411) — later
  passes depend on the pass's bookkeeping; it is not a usable probe.
- The `-l` listing (`IDO52_UOPT_TRACE=5 -Wo,-l,file.lst`) prints the
  post-redundant-stores isearch table AND the `>>>active<<<` web dump
  after global coloring — `grep '>>>active<<<{<item>|'` is the fastest
  "did this variable get a web" oracle (how the GEKO refutation was
  proven: cand item {960} absent in baseline, web 15 with the cure).

## Hook 33 build investigation (2026-08-08, second investigation): copy-fold decision-site hunt — sites LOCATED, veto UNSOUND-FOR-PURPOSE, NOT LANDED

Mission: find the true decision site of the cross-bb copy fold (GEKO
`cand = best` class) and land a site-keyed veto.  Outcome: the store-KILL
decision pair was located exactly and a working site-keyed veto was
probe-built and proven — but the kill is downstream CLEANUP, not the
fold decision; the use-side fold is decided in LOCAL OPTIMIZATION at
tree/item level and survives every existing site-keyed layer, singly and
in combination.  No consumer benefits, so nothing was promoted to the
live toolchain (verified untouched, uopt md5 961daeb…).  All probes were
built in an isolated scratchpad copy of the build tree.

### Corrections to the Hook 32 headline (measured this investigation)

- **"The tail sweep kills 7 stores — the copy is not among them" is
  WRONG.**  The copy IS kill q=6 of 7.  The earlier investigation watched the
  statement's twin on the parallel list at global `0x1001c89c` (the
  f_copypropagate MAIN-walk loop table, stmt 0x10055910, which stays op
  0x7b forever) — but the sweep and everything downstream walk the OTHER
  list at global `0x1001c898` (bb list; stmt+8 next, bb+16 next-bb in
  the sweep, bb+28 first stmt), where the same source statement is a
  different node (0x1005b2f8) whose op is rewritten 0x7b→0x60 by the
  kill.  ⚠️ Any pointer-keyed statement probe must key the 0x1001c898
  walk, not the 0x1001c89c one.
- **The "+29/+31 == 0 at reg-alloc prep, cure flips to 1/1" claim is
  WRONG** (same wrong-list artifact).  On the authoritative list the
  copy stmt has f28..f31 = 1/1/1/1 in BOTH base and cure at
  pre-makelivranges; base differs only in op (0x60 killed vs 0x7b).
  Flags +29/+31 are set 0→1 by the readnxtinst store handler (both
  compiles) and are a kill-ARRIVAL precondition, not the discriminator.
  The discriminator at the sweep is `f_bvectin(destvar_bit, bb+0x15c)`
  (the copypropagate global dataflow vector): base in-vector → killed;
  cure (dead-store `best = 0` present) not-in-vector → survives.

### What WAS located (exact sites, re-creatable)

- **Kill site 1 — REMOVAL OF REDUNDANT STORES** (f_copypropagate tail):
  statement walk head `L419358` over the 0x1001c898 bb list; kill
  conditions: store-class op + stmt+3==0 + flags +29 && +31 +
  `f_bvectin(MEM_U16(desc+2), bb+0x15c)`; kill path clears the stmt's
  varlist record kind (`MEM_U8(stmt24rec + 9) = 0`), resetbits, and
  rewrites op → 0x60.  The sanctioned `ido52_is_protected_deadstore`
  guard (IDO52_NO_DEADSTORE_GLOBALS site 1) sits ON this exact edge —
  a site-keyed veto is one `goto L419580` at the same spot, keyed by a
  kill-arrival ordinal (probe: `IDO52_W33_KILLSITE=q[,q…]`, arrivals
  traced with dest entry + frame offset; ordinal ticks on both verdicts).
- **Kill site 2 — f_eliminduct backup** (same guard shape at `L481694`,
  IDO52_NO_DEADSTORE_GLOBALS site 2): with site 1 vetoed, the SAME store
  is re-killed here (GEKO: cp q=6 vetoed → ei q=8 kill appears).  A veto
  must cover BOTH.
- Probe hygiene both proven: env-unset = bit-identical objects; with
  both kills vetoed the copy store survives to (and through)
  makelivranges as op 0x7b.

### Why the veto does NOT close the wall (the layered-fold finding)

With the copy store alive through reg-alloc, **output is unchanged** —
the tie-break calls still take `best`'s register.  The USE-side
attribution is decided in LOCAL OPT: the call-arg trees are built on the
copy SOURCE's item from the start.  Imprint (new instrument): the bb
varlist records (`bb+36` list, record+9 kind byte, record+12 item) show
base emitting PAIRS per use site — `k9=2` live-use of the SOURCE's item
+ `k9=0` inert marker for the copy dest — where the cure has a single
`k9=2` use of the dest (k9: 0=folded/inert, 1=store, 2=live use, set at
build; the kill sweep later also zeroes the store record's k9).

Exhaustive negative matrix (all on the GEKO minimal repro pair unless
noted; every run verified live-compiler-bit-identical when env unset):

| Probe | Result |
|---|---|
| Kill veto cp q=6 | ei q=8 re-kills; output = base |
| Kill veto cp+ei (6,8) | store survives, webs reshuffle (register perm), uses STILL on best, word count unchanged — fold intact |
| Family-2 lod deny, all 8 arrivals swept singly | none is cand's load (6 change other vars' shapes, calls 3/4 args stay `move a0,s7` in all) — cross-bb loads never reach the L43e8f0 gate (per-bb var entries are FRESH: 3 separate entries for cand's offset, all last=0; calls end bbs) |
| f_ilodfold | never invoked on this function (entry-print probe, 0 arrivals) |
| MLR use-liveness guard (`f_bvectin(bit, bb+0xf4)` skip at L466208) | never rejects anything here (all arrivals in=0); cand's uses never arrive — excluded upstream by record kind k9=0 |
| EVENT/func_8015765C copy-local spelling (s32 a/b/c) + Family-2 denies (q2-5,8,10 all fire DENY) | output BIT-IDENTICAL to folded — a later layer re-folds |
| EVENT same + `IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE=*` (Family 1 all-stmt) + kill veto `*` | STILL bit-identical — the scalar copy stores never even reach the sweep (0 kill arrivals for them); fold re-derived inside local-opt statement assembly/output-value indirection |
| TT/func_8013D988 kill sweep | 6 arrivals; denials 4/5/6 permute registers, word count never moves — the 2-word or-copy deficit is NOT the deadstore class |

**Conclusion / wanted primitive (4th filing, sharpened):** the copy-fold
wall is LAYERED — bb-local lod substitution (Family 2, deniable),
statement-image copy propagation (Family 1, deniable), redundant-store
kill (this investigation's site pair, deniable) — and beneath all of them a
LOCAL-OPT tree-assembly substitution that builds use trees on the copy
source's item and re-derives the fold even when every layer above is
denied.  That bottom layer is where 1995's compiler diverges (it kept
the dest variable real); it is NOT reachable by statement-, lod-,
kill-, or web-formation-keyed vetoes.  Next campaign must instrument
where readnxtinst/statement-completion CHOOSES the item for a var-use
leaf (the varlist k9 pair-append is the observable: find the writer that
appends the `k9=2 source + k9=0 dest` pair).  ⚠️ Also note: for the
EVENT or-fan the dead-store source cure is INEXPRESSIBLE (the copy
source stays live across the copy's uses), so no source-shape trick can
substitute — the primitive is genuinely required.

Probe artifacts (throwaway, NOT in the live tree): scratchpad
`iso/ido-static-recomp` copy with instruments `[W33K]` (kill arrivals /
veto), `[W33MLR]` (authoritative 0x1001c898 statement walk dump),
`[W33VL]` (bb varlist records), `[W33UV]` (MLR use-liveness guard),
`[W33VAR]/[W33LOD]` (var-entry lookups), plus flag/opcode write watches;
GEKO repro pair `p_base.c`/`p_cure.c` (scratch copies, not kept).

## Hook 28 addendum — colour 0 is now expressible, but does NOT crack `held_constant_zero`

**2026-08-09.** `IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB`'s parser used `rc = 0` as *both* "unrecognised
name" and the numeric value of colour 0, guarded by `if (rc > 0)`. `$zero` was therefore
unreachable — you could not forbid colour 0 for a web. Fixed in `apply_uopt_hooks.py`:
sentinel is now `-1`, the names `zero` / `z` map to 0, and the guard is `if (rc >= 0)`.
**Takes effect at the next compiler rebuild** (`apply_uopt_hooks.py` + the rest of the
post-`make clean` chain).

### The hypothesis it was built for is REFUTED

`TT/func_8014C5E8` (and by extension `TT/func_80134768`) was filed as needing exactly
this primitive: the target has `or $a0,$zero,$zero` at index [10] feeding two `sb` stores;
we emit a `nop` and `sb $zero`. The reasoning was that globalcolor gives the const-0 web
colour 0, and *a colour-0 range needs no range-head def*, so forcing a real colour would
make the def appear.

**Measured on an isolated patched compiler: it does not.** Every premise checks out and
the conclusion still fails:

```
[FORMQ const] q=5 stmt=20 op=0x3f val=0x0 type=8 desc=0x1005e628 res=1
[NEWBIT walk_const] bit=33 lr=0x1005e628 stmt=20
[FORMQ const] q=6 stmt=22 op=0x3f val=0x0 type=8 desc=0x1005e628 bit=33 res=1
[EXCLREGS-WEB] bitpos 33: forbid 0x10000000 -> 0x90000000
```

Web 33 forms over exactly the two target stores; the hook fires on it and adds colour 0
to the forbidden mask. The emitted object is **byte-identical** — still `sb $zero`,
still 27/34 exact. Tested alone and paired with `IDO52_CONST_INREG_VALUES=0:8`.

**Conclusion:** the def elision is *not* downstream of the web's colour choice. Whatever
suppresses the range-head def happens elsewhere (or web 33 never reaches globalcolor as a
register web at all — `DUMP-WEBS` reports 5 webs and prints 1). `held_constant_zero`
remains open, and the "forbid colour 0" line of attack is closed.

**Keep the parser fix anyway** — it is a strict capability addition with no behavioural
change unless a spec actually names `zero`, and it removes a genuine expressiveness gap.

## Hook 34: IDO52_FORM_SPLIT — site-keyed constant-descriptor WEB SPLIT (2026-08-11)

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_FORM_SPLIT` | `const:VAL:STMT[-STMTTO][,...]` | `IDO52_FORM_SPLIT=const:0x80127c50:318` |
| `IDO52_FORM_SPLIT` | `lda:OFF:STMT[-STMTTO][,...]` (2026-08-19) | `IDO52_FORM_SPLIT=lda:0xffffffcc:204` |

Applied by **`tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_websplit_hooks.py`** (run AFTER
`apply_uopt_precolor_hooks.py` — it grafts onto Hook 30's statics and statement
tracker; the post-clean rebuild chain gains one line, see the chain block below).
Default OFF; with the env var unset both shims no-op before touching state and
the compiler is bit-identical to the unhooked build.

**This is the "address-constant def-placement" primitive the §WANTED PRIMITIVE
section below asked for — built, validated, and landed on its raising function.**
The ugen attribution in that section was WRONG; the mechanism is uopt's, one
level above every hook we had:

### Mechanism (the real cause of the cross-arm dominator hoist)

- cfe emits ONE constant descriptor per literal value; uopt's makelivranges
  walker (`func_4623c4`, const case L462438) caches the web bit **in the
  descriptor** (`MEM_U16(desc+2)`, 0xffff = none).  Every later use of the same
  literal finds the cached bit and **joins the same web** — `f_constinreg` (and
  therefore Hook 30's FORM_SITE override) is only consulted about *candidacy*,
  never about *membership*.  That is why FORM_SITE denial at the joining
  statements provably fired and changed nothing.
- One web spanning two guarded arms ⇒ globalcolor places the web's def at a
  block dominating both arms — the hoist above the `beqz` that put
  `RPG_BAT/func_80141060`'s whole tail out of phase.
- The hook clears `desc+2` at a site-keyed (value, statement) formation query
  with a positive result: `f_newbit` allocates a **fresh web**, the matched use
  and every later use join it, earlier uses keep the old one.  Per-region webs
  ⇒ per-region defs, emitted by uopt's own emitters.  No output byte is
  patched.
- ⚠️ **The `lr+52` split-chain invariant.**  globalcolor resolves a use's
  covering range by `desc+2 → liverange`, then walks the chain at `lr+52`
  until `f_bvectin` hits the use's bb (the same chain uopt's native `f_split`
  maintains).  Naively orphaning the old range SEGFAULTS globalcolor
  (measured: any e70 split without the link).  The hook therefore links
  `newlr+52 = oldlr` immediately after the const site's `f_formlivbb` creates
  the new range.  With the link, every split combination compiles cleanly.

### Usage

1. `IDO52_FORM_TRACE=1` → read the `[FORMQ const]` lines for the value; the
   `stmt=` ordinals are the site keys (they do NOT feedback-renumber).  The
   first query of a value *forms* its web (`bit=65535`), later ones *join*.
2. Split at the statement where a new region's use joins:
   `IDO52_FORM_SPLIT=const:VAL:STMT`.  Multiple splits of the same value give
   one web per region (each split re-clears the cache).
3. Trace confirms: `[FORM-SPLIT] stmt=.. -> fresh web` +
   `[FORM-SPLIT] chained newlr=..`, and the later FORMQ lines show the new
   `bit=`.

### Worked example + measured effect — RPG_BAT/func_80141060 (the raising wall)

`@CompileEnv: IDO52_FORM_SPLIT=const:0x80127c50:318` (with the file's existing
`IDO52_CONST_NOINREG_VALUES=131072:7:8`): the `(sel & 0xF0000)` arm's bank-base
init materialises `lui $v0,0x8012 / ori $v0,$v0,0x7c50` **directly into $v0
inside the arm** — target shape, no web copy `move $v0,$tX` — and the tail
realigns.  Combined with the read-order fixes it unlocked (finding (C) recipe
applied to two more trios) the function moved **438 → 468/641 resolver-exact**,
count-exact 641, `len t=2564 c=2564`, all audits clean (isomorphism 0 producer
changes, bans CLEAN, raw self-audit delta=0, semcheck clean, defuse 0 findings).

Splitting BOTH bank constants per arm (`+ const:0x80127e70:305/356/372`) aligns
the tail even better (aligned mnemonic identity 215→217/241) **but emits 644
body words (+3)** — the splits fix the deficit regions that had been silently
cancelling a pre-existing +3 surplus in the `D_80129884` store block
[571..600] (an addressing-form wall: ours picks a `0x80128000`-rounded base
where the target indexes off `0x801271B0`).  Until that wall is cracked the
e70 splits are banked, not landed — ⚠️ the resolver still prints
`len t=2564 c=2564` for the 644-word object because it slices to the window
(the documented truncated-compare trap); always re-run
`audit_raw_count.py` after changing a split set.

### Validation (2026-08-11)

- Inertness: 10 strict-clean byte_perfect functions across 10 overlays
  (SHUGAKU/DATE/BUNKAKEN/EVENT/ETC/TACO/DATE2/TT/RENSYU/BUNKA_SD), env unset,
  hooked compiler → all 10 `✅ BYTE-PERFECT` via the authoritative resolver.
- Interplay: 3 `@CompileEnv`-dependent byte_perfect functions (BUNKASAI
  func_8013CCB4 / func_8014437C / func_80155B24) → all `✅ BYTE-PERFECT`.

### The `lda:` spec form (2026-08-19) — BUILT, first consumer byte-perfect

`lda:OFF:STMT[-STMTTO]` applies the same membership split at the walker's LDA
formation site (`func_4623c4` L462524 → `f_ldainreg`, flbb site 3).  `OFF` is
the lda offset word exactly as `IDO52_FORM_TRACE=1` prints it in the
`[FORMQ lda]` lines (`off=0x...`, e.g. `0xffffffcc`); `const:` and `lda:`
entries mix freely in one spec.  The `lr+52` chain link is mirrored at the
lda case's own f_formlivbb join (trace: `[FORM-SPLIT] chained (lda) ...`).

**Worked example — TAIIKU/func_80145534 (byte-perfect 2026-08-19, the raising
filing).**  The Blk16 copy-out loop's end bound is loop-invariant; the target
holds it in a preheader register while the FORM_SITE deny that produced the
correct FOLDED `addiu rX,sp,<buf72+36>` form left it rematerialising in-loop
— "fold it AND give the folded value its own web", which no candidacy hook can
express.  Two findings closed it:
1. `buf72+36` and `&buf108[0]` are the SAME frame address (adjacent locals)
   and cfe folds them to ONE lda descriptor (`off=0xffffffcc`).  Spelling the
   loop test `bsrc != (Blk16 *)buf108` routes the walker query at THAT
   descriptor instead of the buf72 base (whose deny does the folding).
2. `IDO52_FORM_SPLIT=lda:0xffffffcc:204` then gives the loop-test use a
   FRESH web → preheader def, target shape.  168/180 → **180/180**
   (len t=720 c=720, raw delta=0, bans CLEAN, iso 0; the fresh 66-paint
   layer derived in one gen_reg_patches pass with zero refusals).
⚠️ The originally-filed key `lda:0xffffffa8:204` (the buf72 BASE) measures as
the ALLOW horn — base held in a register with an in-loop `addiu end,base,36`
derive — NOT the wanted shape.  Split the descriptor of the FOLDED value, not
of the base it folds from.

**Negative results (2026-08-19), recorded so they are not re-run:**
- TT/func_8013A618 (the 2nd filing): `lda:0:37` fires (fresh web, chained)
  but the object is BIT-IDENTICAL — membership was not what pins that def
  placement; the wall stands as filed.
- BUNKAKEN/func_80145548: `lda:0:82`, `const:56:82`, both — all inert; its
  wall is the multu/strength-reduction coupling, not web membership.

### Candidate class

Any semi whose residual is a long opcode run starting right after a guarded
arm that shares a table/bank base constant with a sibling arm — grep FORM_TRACE
for one `val=` appearing with the same `bit=` across multiple `stmt=` regions.
For LDA walls: a loop-invariant folded address rematerialising at its use
while the target holds it in a register is the `lda:` split's class — find
the folded value's OWN descriptor (watch for adjacent-local address aliasing
as in the worked example).

Post-clean rebuild chain (now 8 appliers):

```
cd tools/ido-static-recomp
rm build/5.2/uopt.c && make VERSION=5.2 c_files
python3 apply_uopt_hooks.py
python3 apply_uopt_defplace_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c
python3 apply_uopt_precolor_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c
python3 apply_uopt_copyprop_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c
python3 apply_uopt_websplit_hooks.py build/5.2/uopt.c   # <-- NEW (Hook 34)
python3 apply_ugen_hooks.py
python3 apply_as1_hooks.py
python3 apply_as1_delay_hooks.py build/5.2/as1.c
make VERSION=5.2
```
