## Hook 35: IDO52_AS1_TIE_* / IDO52_AS1_XBB_SKIP_CAND — as1 scheduler tie-break override + boost source-preference veto (2026-08-11)

**This is the "as1 delay-fill / lui-source-preference" wanted primitive, BUILT — and
its three filing walls are now measured to be REGISTER-ASSIGNMENT walls, not as1-choice
walls.** Two hook families, both env-gated, default-off, applied by the existing
appliers (no new script; the post-clean chain is unchanged):

| Env | Applier | Values |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_TIE_SRC_ORDER` | `apply_as1_hooks.py` (its Hook 9) | `bb:N[,N...]` (preferred) \| `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `-N[,...]` |
| `IDO52_AS1_TIE_TAKE` / `IDO52_AS1_TIE_REJECT` | `apply_as1_hooks.py` | `N[,N...]` (tie-break occurrence indices) |
| `IDO52_AS1_TIE_TRACE` | `apply_as1_hooks.py` | presence — `[AS1-TIE] #N bb=K cand@pos=word prio=P slat=S \| best@... [-> verdict]` |
| `IDO52_AS1_XBB_SKIP_CAND` | `apply_as1_delay_hooks.py` | `N[,N...]` — skip the N-th boost candidate func_427800 would return |
| `IDO52_AS1_XBB_PICK_TRACE` | `apply_as1_delay_hooks.py` | presence — `[AS1-XBB-PICK] #N src= dst= i= insn= [-> SKIPPED]` |

### Decision sites and safety argument

- **TIE_***: func_4314ec's step-4 EQUAL-START-TIME tie-break — the same two spellings
  Hook 7 instruments (L43189c `f_is_node_better` pass a1=1, L4318b8 node+44 priority
  pass a1=0). Candidate and incumbent are both READY IN THE SAME CYCLE, so any steered
  outcome is a schedule as1's own tie-break could have produced; dependence legality is
  untouched. `SRC_ORDER` makes the node earlier in ORIGINAL program order (node+20) win,
  overriding the critical-path-height compare that front-loads long load chains.
  **Branches/jumps are excluded from the SRC_ORDER rule** (measured: forcing a trailing
  store to its program-order slot ahead of the block's `b` left the delay slot a nop,
  +1 word per arm).
- **XBB_SKIP_CAND**: func_427800's SUCCESS exit (the `v0 = s2` return of an accepted
  boost candidate). The veto takes its own "not movable" path (L427b50), so the scan
  continues and the NEXT movable instruction of the same source block is offered — a
  pure source-preference steer; every candidate still passes as1's own legality and
  profitability tests.

### ⚠️ Two traps, both measured

1. **Occurrence-indexed TIE rules break do_xbb_opt.** The boost RE-SCHEDULES blocks
   during trial moves, so occurrence-counted tie decisions shift between the trial and
   the final schedule and as1 dies with `as1xbb.c, line 923: assertion (new_from_time
   == old_from_time) failed`. Use the `bb:` form: blocks are keyed by FIRST-SEEN
   ordinal at the tie-break site (first-scheduling order, printed by the trace), which
   is deterministic across reschedules. `TIE_TAKE`/`TIE_REJECT` remain
   occurrence-indexed and carry this fragility — trace first, keep them for
   single-shot surgical use outside xbb-active regions.
2. **The usual index shift**: any env or source change re-derives all indices
   (`FORCE_LA_PULL`/`NO_LA_PULL` indices shifted when the branch-exclusion refinement
   changed arm schedules between runs). Re-trace before believing an index.

### Validation (2026-08-11)

- **Inertness**: env unset → 10/10 strict-clean byte_perfect functions across
  BUNKAKEN/BUNKASAI/BUNKA_SD/DATE/DATE2/ENDING/EN_NICHI/ETC/EVENT/GEKO re-verified
  `✅ BYTE-PERFECT` (authoritative resolver line); hooked-env consumers
  `GEKO/func_80136790` (NO_LA_PULL=8) and `ENDING/func_8013A318`
  (XBB_BUDGET/VOLATILE_OK) still `✅ BYTE-PERFECT`; env-off recompiles of
  DATE2/func_80136868 are bit-identical objects across all three hook builds.
- **DATE2/func_80136868** (filing wall 1): `IDO52_AS1_TIE_SRC_ORDER=bb:5,6
  IDO52_AS1_FORCE_LA_PULL=1 IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL=2` reproduces the target's BOTH arm
  orders exactly (E922 rmw before the EEBD row chain, verified by objdump) and pulls
  the LOAD-base lui as the single boost, count-exact 140/140. **The residual is now a
  single placement**: the pulled lui lands in the `bnez` delay slot, not the idx-43
  lbu interlock bubble — because OUR ugen assigned the E922 load `$t1`, which the
  dispatch block redefines (`sltiu $t1`), so a WAW edge legally pins the pulled lui
  below the last old-`$t1` reader. The TARGET's ugen assigned `$t6` (no collision).
  The 1-word skew this leaves makes the repainted total WORSE than the tuned baseline
  (103/140), so the baseline `.c` is retained unchanged.
- **KANGEI/func_801327E4** (filing wall 2): `XBB_SKIP_CAND=5` removes the wrong
  E928-base hoist at idx 132, but func_427800 then offers a `move`, NOT the target's
  F6BF lui — the F6BF read is in `$v0`, which the dispatch block still READS
  (`srl $t0,$v0,12`), so the pull the target took is ILLEGAL for our registers
  (target used `$t9`). Full 12-candidate skip sweep: every value ≤ baseline 43.8%
  exact (0 and 10 inert, rest negative). Baseline retained.
- **SHOUGATU/func_80137364** (filing wall 3): skip probes on all plausible candidates
  (0/1/6/8) all ≤ baseline 50.0% exact. Consistent with its header: the disp/sub
  ugen register swap is upstream of every as1 choice.

### The sharpened wall (what to build instead)

All three filings collapse to ONE root: **ugen expression-temp register assignment**.
as1's delay-fill/boost source preference is now fully steerable (this hook), but in
every specimen the 1995 toolchain's choice was only LEGAL because its ugen gave the
moved instruction a register that does not collide with the destination block's live
values — and the register paints cannot help because they are applied AFTER
scheduling, while the collision constrains the schedule itself. The wanted primitive
is now a ugen-side temp-register choice control (the same "pin-unreachable ugen
expression temp" class `IDO52_PRECOLOR_WEB` cannot reach, per SHOUGATU's 2026-08-11
probe and the refuted `UGEN_TREG_ORDER` probe in DATE2's header). Hook 35 remains the
right tool for tie-break/boost walls whose registers do NOT collide — none of the
three banked specimens is one, so its first consumer is still open.

**✅ 2026-08-12: BUILT as Hook 36 (`IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG`, next section) — and the
measurement SPLIT the filed class in two.** DATE2/func_80136868 was exactly this
class and went **byte-perfect** (its first consumer). KANGEI/func_801327E4 and
SHOUGATU/func_80137364 turned out to be a DIFFERENT class: their wall registers
(`$v0`/`$v1`) are **uopt ITEM/targeting registers**, never in ugen's temp free
list, so no free-list-level choice control can reach them — see Hook 36's
"boundary" note.

## Hook 36: IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG — per-request ugen expression-temp REGISTER-CHOICE force (2026-08-12)

**The Hook 35 "sharpened wall" primitive, BUILT — first consumer
DATE2/func_80136868, byte-perfect the same day.** Lives in `apply_ugen_hooks.py`
(no new applier; the post-clean rebuild chain is unchanged).

```
IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=<occ>:<reg>[,<occ>:<reg>...]
IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_TRACE=1
```

- `<occ>` = 1-based occurrence index of an integer **temp-register REQUEST** — a
  call of `f_get_one_free_reg` — counted per function (reset from `f_init_regs`).
  ⚠️ A DIFFERENT index space from Hook 23's `f_get_reg` occurrences: it also counts
  requests arriving via `f_get_free_reg`, and does not count two-register (64-bit)
  requests.
- `<reg>` = `t0`-`t9`/`s0`-`s8` name or raw MIPS register number.

### Decision site and safety argument

ugen hands out expression temps by popping the HEAD of the integer free list at
`0x10019a94` (`f_get_one_free_reg`, label `L43bcd4` → `f_remove_head`) — this is
the round-robin visible in every trace. The hook, at request `<occ>`, removes the
NAMED register from the free list instead of the head (membership WALKED, never
read from the descriptor `+7` flag — Hook 23's safety rule). **Any genuinely-free
register is a legal choice**, so a steered outcome is an allocation ugen's own
allocator could have produced; nothing is spilled, no live value is touched, and
free-list-empty (spill-path) requests are traced but not forceable. If the named
register is not free the force is SKIPPED with a warning and the default pop
proceeds.

Implementation detail that cost one abort: `f_remove_from_free_list` ends in
`f_fill_reg(reg,0,0,1)`, which clears the descriptor's on-a-list flag (`+7`) —
but the claim path being resumed (`f_append_to_list` onto the in-use list at
`0x10019a98`) `abort()`s if that flag is clear (`f_remove_head` never touches
it). The hook restores the flag after removal.

TRACE prints one line per request — `[UGEN-TREG36] occ=N free=[...]` then
`occ=N -> rX [FORCED]` — the chosen register plus the whole free list, which is
what makes occ→instruction correlation practical.

### Workflow (measured on the first consumer)

1. Compile with `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_TRACE=1` and the function's existing
   `@CompileEnv`; objdump the object. Map requests to instructions by matching
   the trace's chosen registers against the disassembly **in ucode-eval order**
   (per-statement, which is NOT final schedule order — DATE2's popcount block
   evaluates per-bit chains: andi, sltiu, xori per bit).
2. ⚠️ **Do NOT try to force the whole target permutation in one shot.** Many
   requests are transients (alloc-and-freed address temps), and forcing changes
   ugen's downstream reuse decisions, so the occ↔instruction correspondence
   DRIFTS under forcing (measured: a 40-entry full-permutation spec landed on
   different instructions than mapped). Force the MINIMAL set instead.
3. The minimal set for a WAW/delay-slot wall: keep the colliding block OFF the
   target's register by forcing the requests that would naturally take it to
   their own would-be registers (each such force is register-neutral for its own
   instruction), then give the wall instruction the target's register.
4. Repaint from scratch (`gen_reg_patches` x3 — seed one hand-derived paint
   first; the tool refuses to run with no existing paint line) and run the FULL
   audit battery.

### Worked example — DATE2/func_80136868 (byte-perfect 2026-08-12)

```
@CompileEnv: IDO52_AS1_TIE_SRC_ORDER=bb:5,6 IDO52_AS1_FORCE_LA_PULL=1
             IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL=2
             IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=11:t2,12:t7,13:t8,14:t9,15:t0,16:t6
```

Hook 35's combo already reproduced the target's arm orders count-exact, but our
ugen gave the arm-0 E922 load `$t1` while the dispatch block redefines `$t1` — a
WAW edge legally pinned as1's pulled `lui` in the `bnez` delay slot instead of
the idx-43 interlock bubble. Forces `11:t2..15:t0` hand occ 11–15 exactly the
registers they would have taken anyway EXCEPT that `$t6` (the natural occ-11
choice) stays in the list untaken; the dispatch block then never writes `$t6`,
and force `16:t6` gives the E922 load the target's register. as1's pull becomes
legal, the `lui $t6` lands in the interlock bubble, the `move` drops into the
branch delay slot — the target's exact schedule. 121/140 (best prior, 103/140
after the paint-soundness purge) → **140/140 byte-perfect**: resolver ✅ len
t=560 c=560, audit_raw_count PASS delta=0, audit_paint_isomorphism 0 producer
changes, audit_paint_bans CLEAN, semcheck clean.

### ⚠️ BOUNDARY — uopt ITEM registers are a different, still-unreachable class

The other two Hook 35 filings are NOT this class (measured 2026-08-12, honest
negatives, headers updated):

- **KANGEI/func_801327E4**: the F6BF read pair is `lui $v0`/`lbu $v0` and the
  E928 base rides `$v1`. `$v0`/`$v1` NEVER appear in ugen's temp free list —
  they are **uopt item/targeting registers** (the same layer as the
  "STR-to-v0 ucode temp" in RPG_BAT/func_8015528C's EOF note). No free-list
  choice can retarget them.
- **SHOUGATU/func_80137364**: the disp/sub swap is `$v0`↔`$v1` — same item
  class. The tail's arm lui-remat difference RIDES on it: the target parks the
  E760-base `lui $v1` in the shared branch delay slot, which is only legal
  because ITS disp lives in `$v0`; ours lives in `$v1`, so the slot write would
  clobber the selector. Every t-reg difference downstream is cascade, not cause.

The next wanted primitive for those two is a **uopt-side item/targeting register
control** (which value uopt's reemission homes in `$v0`/`$v1`/`$aN` items) —
distinct from web coloring (Hook 14b/28/30 — probed inert on both specimens)
and from this hook's free-list class.

### Validation (2026-08-12)

- **Inertness**: env unset → 12/12 strict-clean byte_perfect functions across
  BUNKAKEN/BUNKASAI/DATE/DATE2/ENDING/ETC/EVENT/GEKO/KANGEI/SHOUGATU/TAIIKU/
  RPG_BAT re-verified ✅ BYTE-PERFECT (authoritative resolver line), plus the
  Hook 23 flagship consumer DATE/func_80132728 (`TREG_ORDER_WINDOW` env) ✅.
  With the env unset `ugen_tempreg_event()` returns before touching any state.
- **Status JSON delta**: exactly +1 effective (DATE2 semi 197→196,
  byte_perfect 5520→5521), zero collateral changes, serial per-overlay verify +
  `apply_resolved_status.py`.

## ⭐ WANTED PRIMITIVE — ugen-side address-constant def placement

**✅ BUILT 2026-08-11 as Hook 34 (`IDO52_FORM_SPLIT`, section above) — and the
"ugen-side" attribution below was WRONG.** The def placement follows web
membership, and membership is decided by uopt's descriptor cache
(`MEM_U16(desc+2)`) one level ABOVE the formation query: FORM_SITE denial
fired-and-did-nothing because the cache, not the query result, assigns the
bit. Clearing the cache at a site-keyed query (plus the `lr+52` chain link —
see Hook 34's invariant note) yields exactly the per-region rematerialisation
this section asked for. ugen was never the decision site. The section is kept
verbatim below as the problem statement and refutation record.

**Raised 2026-08-09 from `RPG_BAT/func_80141060` (the most fought-over function in the
tree). This is the highest-value unbuilt hook we currently know of.**

### The problem it solves

When two sibling guarded arms use the *same* address constant, uopt merges the uses
into one web and **ugen places that web's def at the common dominator** — hoisting the
`lui`/`ori` above the guard. The 1995 target instead **rematerialises the address
separately inside each arm**. One hoist puts the whole downstream region ~2 words out
of phase, which is why it presents as ~100 opcode diffs rather than one.

Concretely (`FORM_TRACE`, three uses of `0x80127c50`):

```
q=64 stmt=208 val=0x80127c50 desc=0x100a9258 bit=65535 res=1   <- forms the web
q=73 stmt=267 val=0x80127c50 desc=0x100a9258 bit=376   res=1   <- joins it
q=81 stmt=318 val=0x80127c50 desc=0x100a9258 bit=376   res=1   <- joins it
```

### Why every existing hook fails — do not re-run these

| lever | result |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_FORM_SITE` deny at the joining statements | **fires** (`res` 1→0) — object BYTE-IDENTICAL |
| `IDO52_EMIT_SITE` deny at occ 2,3 and at ALL occurrences | inert |
| `IDO52_CONST_NOINREG_VALUES` += the bank addresses | inert |
| `IDO52_LDA_NOINREG_CLASSES` 4 and 1,4 | inert — `f_ldainreg` is **never queried** for these (debug with all 8 classes blocked logs zero) |
| `SKIP_PASSES=codemotion` / `globalopt`, `NO_LICM`, `NO_CSE=all`, `CSE_MAX_REUSE=1/2`, `NO_SREG_PROMOTE` | all inert |
| C-side: named externs | count-breaking (640 vs 641) |
| C-side: different pointer type / u32 or s32 intermediate | descriptor unchanged (an address literal is type 0 whatever its pointer type) |

**The decisive evidence is that `FORM_SITE` fires and changes nothing.** Web membership
is already assigned when the formation query runs, so denying the query cannot dissolve
it — and the emission mirror cannot move the def either. The placement is **ugen's**,
downstream of every uopt hook we have.

### What to build

A site-keyed control in `apply_ugen_hooks.py` over where an address-constant web's def
is emitted — per-region rematerialisation instead of dominator hoisting. The natural
shape mirrors Hook 30: `kind:val:bb[:occ]:mode`, where `mode` forces the def to be
emitted at the *use's* block rather than the dominator.

Requires a compiler rebuild, so schedule it when no workers are sharing
`tools/ido-static-recomp/build`.

### Expected payoff

`RPG_BAT/func_80141060` runs **parallel to the target through `[407..641]`, offset by
~2 words**. Fixing the placement should collapse a large share of its 203 remaining
diffs at once, leaving ordinary register residue for `gen_reg_patches`. The class is
almost certainly not unique to that function — any semi whose residual is a long
opcode run starting right after a guarded arm that shares a table base with a sibling
arm is a candidate.


---

## Hook 37: IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR — positive per-web colour assignment (2026-08-13)

| Variable | Format | Example |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR` | `BITPOS:reg[,BITPOS:reg...]` (reg = v0..t5, ra, s0..s7, s8/fp, or `N<num>`) | `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR=442:a2` |
| `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR_NOSPLIT` | `BITPOS[,...]` | veto the phase-1 range split for a web |
| `IDO52_GC_COLOR_TRACE` | `1` | enumerate every pick / spill / split |

Applied by apply_uopt_hooks.py (section 37).  Forces a web's colour at
f_globalcolor's actual pick sites (phase 1 `L46f158`, phase 2 `L46fbc0`,
staged in `sp+220`, committed at `MEM_U8(s5+32)`), matched by the same
bittab BITPOS key space as Hook 28.  **Legality gate**: a force is
accepted only if the colour is inside the web's class range AND not in
its own forbidden bitvector — an ungated force can put two live values
in one register while producing excellent-looking scores (measured:
249/748 TEL specs aborted or miscompiled before the gate).

**THE colour space (empirical, corrects Hooks 18/28's old table):**
v0..t5 = 1..12, **ra = 13**, s0..s7 = 14..21, s8/fp = 22.  Colours ≥23
are invalid.  **t6–t9 have no colour at all** — they are exclusively
ugen's temp pool (Hook 36's domain); a value the target holds in t6-t9
must not become a uopt web (web-formation veto for non-constants is a
wanted, unbuilt primitive).

⭐ **Correction 2026-09-26 (run E131): a t6-t9 value held across
statements or fall-through blocks is usually stock IDO, and no new
primitive is needed.** Two mechanisms, both measured on tiny TUs and
closing KANGEI/func_80133A6C, EVENT/func_8014E774, BUNKAKEN/func_8013BD84
and GEKO/func_80134A40:
1. **Temp-plus-copy, forwarded by as1.** When a web's operand is already
   in the web's own register (ugen loads a LOD straight into its
   destination hint), ugen will not target the op in place (src == dst):
   it evaluates into a pool temp and emits `move web,temp`. as1 pass-I
   copy propagation then forwards the temp into every later use, including
   uses in a fall-through block with no label, and deletes the move if the
   web is dead. The move survives only when the web is live on, as in
   GEKO's `move s0,t5` delay slot. Lever: colour the web onto the register
   the operand is loaded into (Hook 37), or keep the use a LOD of the
   variable (Hook 32 `lod:N:deny`) so the op's destination is the web.
2. **ugen's in-block load cache for an UNCOLOURED global.** A global that
   uopt leaves uncoloured is re-read at each U-code LOD, and ugen keeps the
   first load of a block in a pool temp for all the others. If a head read
   joins the global into a coloured web, `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_SPILL=<bitpos>`
   splits it back into per-block loads. Do not use volatile here: a
   volatile read in another block also pulls the tail reads into a
   coloured live range.

Also established: Hook 28's forbid-write is TRANSIENT w.r.t. the colour
pick (f_updateforbidden rebuilds the vector between L46e804 and the
pick) — Hook 28 genuinely acts on the constrained-set/split decision,
which explains its "fires but nothing changes" history.  Range-split
runs BEFORE assignment at `L46ef64`; a split web recolours later under
new bitposes.

First wins: TEL/func_8013D758 +7 count wall closed (`442:a2`, count
parity 773/773).  Specimen verdicts + wanted successors
(FORCE_WEB_SPILL, ugen evaluate-into-temp veto) in the 2026-08-13
research report artifacts and the specimen file headers.
Regression: 22/22 TUs bit-identical env-unset AND env-applied vs the
pre-hook toolchain.

### Limits — overflow is a hard error (2026-09-26)

The parser (apply_uopt_hooks.py section 37) holds at most
`FORCE_WEB_COLOR_MAX` = 24 `BITPOS:reg` entries and reads the value through
a 512-byte buffer.  Until 2026-09-26 both limits were silent: the 25th entry
and any byte past the 511th were never parsed, so a directive could look
applied and be partly ignored (finding 3 of the hook honesty audit, in the
project's private docs).  `apply_uopt_web_hooks.py` now adds two checks around the parse
loop (idempotent, marker `[GC-COLOR] ERROR: IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR`; it
anchors on the Hook 37 parse loop so it must run after `apply_uopt_hooks.py`,
and `--rebuild` relinks uopt).  Either check prints the error and `exit(1)`s,
which the build surfaces as a compile failure:

| condition | stderr |
|---|---|
| more than 24 entries | `[GC-COLOR] ERROR: IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR has more than 24 entries (first ignored: '<tok>') -- aborting; split the directive or raise FORCE_WEB_COLOR_MAX` |
| value 512 bytes or longer | `[GC-COLOR] ERROR: IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR value is N bytes; the parser holds 511 and the rest would be silently dropped -- aborting` |

An unknown register name still only warns (`[GC-COLOR] WARNING: unknown
register`) and does not consume a slot, as before.  Census when it landed:
250 users, largest directive 13 entries / 81 bytes, so no live file is
affected.  Verified the same day: env-unset objects byte-identical old vs
rebuilt uopt on a 20-file sample (19 comparable, 0 differ); every user
compiles with its real env; a 24-entry value compiles, a 25-entry value
and a 599-byte value abort with the messages above.

## Hooks 38–40: web home-store / spill / formation family (2026-08-13)

Applied by `apply_uopt_web_hooks.py` (wrapper over
`uopt_web_hooks/apply_h38.py`/`_h39`/`_h40`, run in that order, LAST of
the uopt appliers — its anchors include hook-37-era lines).  All keyed
by Hook 28/37's bittab BITPOS space unless noted.

| Hook | Variable | What it does |
|------|----------|--------------|
| 38 | `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_HOME` (+ `IDO52_WEB_HOME_STAGE`, `IDO52_WEB_HOME_TRACE`) | per-web LiveUnit home-store flag force (lu+20 firstisstr / lu+21 needreglod / lu+22 needregsave / lu+23 deadout, lr+34 hasstore), pre- and/or post-f_globalcolor |
| 38b | `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_ADJSAVE` | overrides the float `adjsave` at lr+48 via a rename-and-wrap of f_compute_save |
| 39 | `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_SPILL` / `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL` | lr+35 = 2 marks a web uncolourable (f_globalcolor's own L46ea34 epilogue spills it); lr+35 = 1 puts a given-up web back in the colour scan.  ⚠️ NOSPILL usually needs `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR_NOSPLIT` on the same bitpos or the re-entered web range-splits. Bare bitposes are marked BEFORE f_globalcolor, so a range-split REMAINDER (new bitpos) is unreachable here — use Hook 60's `ROOT.PIECE` keys |
| 40 | `IDO52_VETO_WEB` (+ `IDO52_VETO_WEB_STAGE`, `IDO52_VETO_WEB_DROPLR`) | removes a bitpos from the colouring worklists (0x100223a8 phase 1, 0x100223a0 phase 2, 0x1001cb98) |
| 40b | `IDO52_VETO_FORMLIV` | rename-and-wrap of f_formlivbb: a named per-procedure occurrence never creates its live range at all |

First win: DATE/func_8013B1AC (2026-08-14) — the "regional const-web
materialization" wall closed with `FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL=142` +
`FORCE_WEB_COLOR_NOSPLIT=142` + Hook 24 `CONST_DEF_BB` moving the
range-head def; byte-perfect 162/162.

⚠️ The hooks2-research validation harness overrode USR_LIB, so its
recorded per-customer validations may have been self-comparisons —
re-validate any hooks-38/40 refutation before trusting it (Hook 39 is
independently proven by the DATE win).

⛔ **Hook 38/38b re-probed on the real pipeline 2026-08-14
(GYOZI/func_8013306C, full evidence in that file's tail note): the
home-store EMISSION is NOT reachable as built.** The adjsave override
lands in lr+48 and visibly shifts colouring priority, and the LiveUnit
flag masks all apply, but no web-home `move` is ever emitted — the
copy-insertion decision consumes something else, before/independent of
both injection points.  Hook 38's honest capability today: adjsave =
a colouring-priority knob; the flags = unproven.  The wanted primitive
narrows to the reemit/ucode-output site that inserts the web-home UCP
copy.  The 2-use CSE-home-copy wall class (GYOZI/func_8013306C,
SHOUGATU twin) remains open.

⛔ **2026-08-19 campaign (5th filing, class UNIFIED and further negatives
measured; specimen methodology in this note):**

- **Shapes A and B are ONE class.** GYOZI's missing `move v0,t6` (the
  adjsave-gate shape; SHOUGATU twin = func_8013315C, byte-identical
  routine) and TT/func_80137A80 / main/func_8007375C's missing
  selector copies (the "load-arm" shape) are the same decision: a value
  with MULTIPLE homes (a CSE'd node feeding named-variable webs and/or
  in-statement uses).  The original compiler materialises it ONCE into a colourless
  ugen temp and emits ONE UCP COPY PER HOME (some dead); ours
  DIRECT-TARGETS one home and REMATERIALISES/coalesces the rest.  Every
  "missing move" consumer in this family is that per-home
  {direct, remat, copy} choice going the other way.
- Negatives measured on a 2-use CSE specimen of GYOZI/func_8013306C
  (explicit `off = rowIdx * 4;` local -- itab: `ustr{832}{750}` with
  use 1 reading the node, use 2 reading the var, exactly the class
  shape): Hook 38/38b FORCE_WEB_HOME / FORCE_WEB_ADJSAVE re-confirmed
  emission-inert; Hook 40b VETO_FORMLIV occ 1-10 emits NO copy (occ 2 is
  -4 words; several occs SEGFAULT globalcolor -- the orphaned-range
  fragility again); Hook 32 store-stmt denies (all 7 store records) and
  lod-q denies (1-8) inert; Hook 41's arm is NEVER REACHED by the CSE'd
  def (TARGET_TRACE prints nothing for it), and on main/func_8007375C's
  honest no-arg spelling NO_TARGET occ 1-4 are output-inert.
- The 08-13 "threshold probe" nuance: re-adding a third use makes the
  def DIRECT-TARGET the web register (`sll $v0,...`), NOT temp+copy --
  crossing the adjsave gate does not by itself produce the copy FORM.
  The copy form additionally requires the value to stay in a colourless
  temp (the multi-home choice).
- f_get_dest anatomy (ugen.c L424924..): hint == 0x48 -> allocate a
  free-list temp; else claim the hinted item register.  The per-home
  direct-vs-temp choice is therefore made by each CALLER's hint; the
  remat-vs-copy choice for the second home is made UPSTREAM in uopt
  (readnxtinst/varlist value recording: ent+52 = last in-bb store's
  value tree, ent+56 = that store's stmt -- the fields Hook 32's lodq
  shim already prints).
- **Sharpened prescription (6th filing):** instrument (a) the WRITER of
  ent+52/ent+56 in f_readnxtinst's store case, and (b) the decision that
  REMATERIALISES a value for its second home instead of copying from the
  first (the site that re-derives a fresh load/`sll` where the original emits
  the UCP).  Only (b) is the causal site; every register-allocation-phase
  knob (37/38/39/40/40b) and every substitution veto (32) is downstream
  or parallel to it.

⛔ **2026-08-19 (continuation): 6th filing EXECUTED — conclusive verdict.**
Hook 42 (below) was built to probe site (b) from the ugen side, and the
remaining candidate deleters were tested.  Results:

- **ugen NEVER emits a UCP copy of its own.**  Hook 42's forced-temp
  probe (main/func_8007375C selector: hint v0 -> forced 0x48 -> `lbu $t6`)
  shows the item's binding simply FOLLOWS the register — every consumer
  reads the new register, no copy appears, count unchanged.  The
  `move`/`or` copy exists ONLY if uopt's final ucode carries an explicit
  copy instruction.  All ugen-side framings of this wall (the A80
  "load-arm veto" included) are hereby CLOSED.
- `-Wo,-nordstore` on the CSE specimen changes the def to direct-target
  + recompute but emits no copy: the redundant-store pass touches the
  region but is not the ustr's keeper.
- The `NO_DEADSTORE_GLOBALS` protected sites DO see register-variable
  stores (the "addr" they check is the vreg offset, e.g. 0xfffffffc =
  vreg -4), but protecting the specimen's `off` vreg (0xffffffec) is
  output-inert — the dead ustr dies UPSTREAM of both protected sites.
- **Verdict: the ustr's disappearance is not a keyed branch in any
  instrumented pass.**  It is removed (or never re-emitted) in uopt's
  early local phase between readnxtinst (the ustr exists in the itab
  listing) and the copyprop statement walk — the same "statement
  assembly / output-value indirection" bottom layer the 4th filing
  named.  The one remaining instrumentation target is the statement
  stream WALK ITSELF: dump every surviving statement (op + operands)
  after each early pass on the specimen pair and bisect which pass
  drops the ustr; then read that pass for its dead-var-store test.
  Until that is done, this wall class has NO sound hook, and its
  consumers (GYOZI/func_8013306C 42/43, SHOUGATU/func_8013315C,
  TT/func_80137A80 101/106, main/func_8007375C) stay honest semis.

## Hook 45: IDO52_AS1_FORCE_NARROW_FOLD — as1 redundant-andi fold force (2026-08-21)

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|----------|--------|--------|
| `IDO52_AS1_FORCE_NARROW_FOLD` | `<occ>[,<occ>...]` | at the N-th arrival of func_416ca8's andi-redundancy test, force the significant-bits state so as1 falls into its OWN fold path (`andi rt,rs,imm` → `or rt,rs,$zero`) even when the intra-BB proof fails |
| (trace) | via the applier's trace flag | occ, word, position, rs/rt, srcbits, mask, imm, natural verdict |

Applied by `apply_as1_hooks.py` (standard post-clean chain).  Synthesises
nothing — removes only a guard; the fold call/args are as1's own.  Hook 29
(KEEP_ANDI) vetoes EARLIER in the same function; enabling both on one TU
shifts Hook 45's indices.  ⚠️ SOUNDNESS is the caller's burden: force only
where the source value provably fits the mask on EVERY path (the veto is
subtractive, the force deletes a correctness proof).  Regression: 104
artifacts / 10 functions env-unset bit-identical; Hook 29 intact.
Consumers (all BYTE-PERFECT 2026-08-21, occ 3 in each — one family):
SHUGAKU/func_80139114 (one env line closed the 102/103 andi->or residual),
SHUGAKU/func_801337FC, KANGEI/func_80138F90.

## Hook 44: IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP — as1 pass-I copy-prop veto (2026-08-21)

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|----------|--------|--------|
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP` | `all` or `<idx>[,<idx>...]` | veto as1 `f_do_passI_opt`'s copy-substitution (`func_414bf8`) at the N-th arrival (1-based, per function) |
| `IDO52_AS1_PASS1_PROP_TRACE` | `1` | one line per arrival: idx, instruction, substituted-or-kept |

Applied by `apply_as1_hooks.py` (in the standard post-clean chain; the
injection returns func_414bf8's own no-change exit — purely subtractive).
Default OFF.  Validated 2026-08-21: 42 kept artifacts across 12 functions
env-unset bit-identical (private toolchain), then the shared rebuild
re-verified 14 hook-dependent byte-perfect wins TRUE WIN.

**What it is:** the missing knob below ugen.  Bisected on
TT/func_8014C5E8: a value-0 def (`or $rX,$zero,$zero`) survives uopt
(RELOAD_EMIT_TRACE identical) and ugen (the `.G` binasm carries the def
as an or-form record) and is folded by **as1 pass I** — `f_do_passI_opt`
substitutes the copy, pass II deletes the corpse.  None of the other
as1 hooks reach it.  ⚠️ SOUNDNESS: `all` is always consistent; a
PARTIAL veto is sound only on **non-defining** instructions — vetoing
the defining `or` alone skips its kill of the stale copy record and
repoints later stores at `$zero`.
**Verdict on the filed class:** `held_constant_zero` on
TT/func_8014C5E8 was a WRONG premise twice over (the colour-0
EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB extension already existed since 2026-08-09, and the
web gets colour 1, not 0) — with the def restored the residual is an
ordinary v0/a0 coalescing wall.  First positive consumer:
TT/func_80134768 (`IDO52_CONST_INREG_VALUES=0:6` +
`IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP=21,22` reaches the target's field-init shape).

### Store-to-load forwarding is as1 pass I, not uopt (2026-09-25)

A retail `sw rX,N(b) ; lw rY,N(b)` pair (store, then re-read the same
field) that our object prints as `sw rX,N(b)` + `move rY,rX` is **Hook 44's
site**, not a uopt primitive. Checked on a minimal TU
(`v->tick--; use(v->tick);`): uopt never forwards an ISTR into a later
ILOD — `cc -S` keeps `sw $10,152($2); lw $4,152($2)`, in the same block
and across blocks. as1 `f_do_passI_opt` then substitutes the stored
register into the load (the minimal TU even retargets the decrement into
`$a0` and drops the reload entirely). No new uopt hook was built for it.

Recipe: run `IDO52_AS1_PASS1_PROP_TRACE=1` with the file's own env, find the
`#N insn=8dYY....` line of the reload (the word as ugen emitted it), and
add `IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP=N`. `N` is per TU and shifts when any other
env token changes the instruction stream, so read it under the final env.
On `main/func_80092170` (#26) the object diff against the unvetoed build is
exactly `move a0,t5` → `lw a0,152(t0)` + its load-delay `nop`, all branch
displacements otherwise identical — the veto is on a defining load but the
downstream stream shows no stale-copy effect. (It is not shipped there yet:
the extra word misaligns the body until a separate `move v0,t9` surplus
and the reload's placement above the `blez` are fixed.)

**Sibling: the "dead copy uopt deletes" is also as1.** `main/func_80097AB4`
with the `mask = size` spelling: `cc -S` has `move $2,$3`; as1 deletes it.
Two as1 decisions remove it and both must be vetoed:
`IDO52_AS1_KEEP_DEAD_RELOAD=0` (Hook 52; `f_redundant`, candidate #0 is
`00601025 move v0,v1`) **and** `IDO52_AS1_NO_COPY_PROP=1` (copy-prop arrival
#1 is the same word). Either alone still loses the word. ⚠️ The `all` forms
of the two together make as1 die with SIGSEGV — use the index forms.
Measured on that spelling: 8/180 Body 179 → 20/180 Body 180 (the move is
kept, but before the `bnez` rather than in its delay slot as retail).

## Hook 42: IDO52_UGEN_DEST_TEMP — f_get_dest destination-hint override (2026-08-19)

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|----------|--------|--------|
| `IDO52_UGEN_DEST_TEMP` | `<occ>[,<occ>...]` | at the N-th f_get_dest call (1-based, per function), force the hint to 0x48 so the node's value is allocated a free-list temp instead of claiming the natural destination register |
| `IDO52_UGEN_DEST_TRACE` | `1` | one line per f_get_dest call: occ, node, op, hint, returned register, [FORCED] |

Applied by `apply_ugen_hooks.py` (rename-and-wrap of `f_get_dest`;
per-function counter reset shares Hook 41's f_init_regs anchor; the
post-clean chain is unchanged).  Default OFF; env unset returns before
touching state.  Validated 2026-08-19: pristine regen + committed chain
reproduces the live ugen.c modulo hook-comment placement, and the
rebuilt compiler passes the 4-function regression set (TT/func_8013D3C0,
TT/func_8013D150, TAIIKU/func_80145534, TT/func_8013E484 all
✅ BYTE-PERFECT, env unset).

**What it is:** a per-occurrence REGISTER-BINDING knob — it relocates
where an expression value lives (a legal choice f_get_dest itself
implements for hint 0x48), and every consumer follows.  Proven live on
main/func_8007375C (selector load moved v0 -> t6 cleanly).  Candidate
uses: steering a value off a register another web needs (the Hook 36
family's uopt-web-side sibling), retiring paint layers whose diffs are
one value-web's home.  **What it is NOT:** a copy creator — see the
verdict above.  SOUNDNESS caveat: a caller passing a real hint might
assume the return equals the hint; every use must pass the full win
gates.  **First shipped consumer (2026-08-19): KANGEI/func_801327E4
BYTE-PERFECT** — `IDO52_UGEN_DEST_TEMP=45,40` + `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=6:t9`
cracked the filed item-register wall (occ 45 = the F6BF read into $t9's
temp slot; occ 40 = the E854 selector off $v0, which let ugen
direct-target the srl AND handed as1's branch-target-dup fill the freed
delay slot — the li $at,6 shape).  Recipe: DEST_TRACE to map hint=2/3
occurrences to loads via reloc movement, force to temp, steer the pop
with Hook 36, then the strip-swap-repaint endgame.  Second foothold:
SHOUGATU/func_80137364 (occ 12 moves the disp read; endgame pending,
see its header).

full pristine-chain regen (all six uopt appliers) + rebuild; env-unset
regression set (TT/func_8013D3C0, TT/func_8013D150, TAIIKU/func_80145534,
TT/func_8013E484) all ✅ BYTE-PERFECT.  The inverse of Hook 37's NOSPLIT
and a path-distinct sibling of Hook 39's lr+35 spill force.

⛔ **7th filing — the web-home UCP copy wall, CONCLUSIVE (2026-08-19).**
Hook 43 was built as the bisection's last candidate ("the original move is
a live-range-split artifact" — the file's own oldest diagnosis).  Measured
on the GYOZI CSE specimen (off-web bitpos 19, 2 LiveUnits bb2/bb3): the
forced split FIRES (`[GC-COLOR] phase=1 bitpos=19 SPLIT`) and **degenerates
to a spill** — post-split state reg=255/worth=0/occ=-1, no boundary copy,
output count unchanged.  On a 2-LiveUnit web there is nothing for f_split
to partition; the phase-1 split arm cannot produce the target's copy.

With this, EVERY keyed decision point in the pipeline has been probed
against this class and refuted: Hooks 32 (all three deniable layers),
34, 37, 38/38b, 39, 40, 40b, 41, 42, 43, the deadstore protection pair
(which DOES see register-var stores, vreg-offset-keyed), `-nordstore`,
and Hook 20/LFTR-adjacent spellings.  Two standing facts bound the
remaining explanation space:
 1. The copy IS reachable from C on this compiler — but only at +2..+5
    words (the BATCH-19b `return rowOff`-family shapes, and the entry-bb
    spelling on TT/func_80137A80 at −1 word) — never count-neutral.
 2. **The recomp is byte-validated against the IRIX 5.2 binaries it was
    recompiled from (run under qemu-irix) on 7,729 TUs (`tools/analysis/validate_ido52.py`), so the target's count-neutral copy MUST
    be producible by THIS compiler from some source shape + flags** —
    the "1995 internals differ" framing is inconsistent with the
    validation corpus (the 0.27% divergence class is block-move
    expansion, which none of these functions contain).
Verdict: this is a SOURCE-SHAPE search problem, not a missing compiler
primitive.  The wall class's functions stay honest semis; retire the
"wanted primitive" framing for it.  Future effort should go to spelling
archaeology (what 1995 C makes cfe+uopt keep an uncoalesced store-only
web AND bind both uses to the node) — the hook route is EXHAUSTED.

## Hook 41: IDO52_UGEN_NO_TARGET — ugen expression-result targeting veto (2026-08-14)

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|----------|--------|--------|
| `IDO52_UGEN_NO_TARGET` | `<occ>[,<occ>...]` or `all` | at f_eval's binary/shift arm (L4308e4), force the evaluate-into-a-temp path: `<op> $tX,...` + `or $dst,$tX,$zero` instead of targeting the destination item directly (+1 insn/site) |
| `IDO52_UGEN_TARGET_TRACE` | `1` | one line per targeting decision, occ + src/dst regs + natural arm |
| `IDO52_UGEN_FORCE_TARGET` | probe only | inverse (force DIRECT); CONDITIONALLY UNSOUND, refused without `IDO52_UGEN_FORCE_TARGET_UNSAFE=1`, never in a committed @CompileEnv |

Applied by apply_ugen_hooks.py (integrated 2026-08-14; the temp arm is
ugen's own, entered via an injected `LH41_TEMP` label before the unique
`f_get_free_reg(node,1)` request).  Occurrence index is 1-based, counts
every arrival at the decision, resets per function.  ⚠️ A veto ADDS one
temp-register request, so Hook 36 (`IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG`) occurrence
indices downstream of it shift by one — re-trace after enabling.

## Hook 46: apply_uopt_readphase_hooks.py — early init + IDO52_NO_SHARE_OPCODES (2026-08-30)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_readphase_hooks.py [--rebuild]`,
run AFTER `apply_uopt_hooks.py` (idempotent, probes its own marker).  Two
changes:

1. **`ido52_init_hooks()` is also called at the top of uopt's `main`** (the
   function has a once-guard).  This is what makes the read-phase hooks — Hook
   1 `IDO52_NO_CSE*`, `IDO52_CSE_DEBUG`, and this hook — actually see their env.
   See the Hook 1 correction above.  Regression: 0 env-off changes.

2. **`IDO52_NO_SHARE_OPCODES=op[,op...]`** (`IDO52_NO_SHARE_DEBUG=1|2`): at
   `func_43c2e0`, the ichain hash search `readnxtinst` runs for EVERY
   expression while the DAG is built (Hook 1's `f_isearchloop` is the separate
   "i" table codemotion/copyprop consult), forces a FRESH ichain for the listed
   U-code opcodes instead of the hash hit.  Pair with `IDO52_NO_CSE_OPCODES` to
   stop codemotion re-merging them.  Live: `x*y` ×3 → three `multu` with
   `IDO52_NO_SHARE_OPCODES=mpy IDO52_NO_CSE_OPCODES=mpy` (codemotion alone
   re-merged the fresh nodes; `SKIP_PASSES=codemotion` is unsound, see Hook 15).

⚠️ **Boundary (the DATE wall, measured):** a multiply of a NAMED-global load
by a constant never reaches this hash — uopt strength-reduces `LOD*const` on
a read-time special path (opcode census at the site: no `mpy`(91), no
`shl`(115)); only `ILOD*const` (pointer / raw-address read) becomes a real
MPY node.  So `mpy` in either hook is inert on `D_x * 6`; a
`*(u8*)ADDR * 6` spelling does go through and yields the held-constant
`li v1,6 / multu` form.  Full probe log: `DATE/func_80134848.c`.

## Hook 36b / 41b: in-place narrow-parameter ops (2026-09-03)

Applied by `apply_ugen_hooks.py` (post-pass `apply_hook36b_41b`, after Hook 42;
the post-clean chain is unchanged). Both default OFF; env unset is bit-identical
(7-function hook-dependent regression: all BYTE-PERFECT, Fixup 0).

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_UGEN_FORCE_TARGET_SAFE` | `<occ>[,<occ>...]` or `all` | Hook 41b: at the Hook 41 targeting decision, take the DIRECT arm when `src == dst` and the node's U-code op is one of the single-instruction ops 0x04 and, 0x3c or, 0x73 sll, 0x74 sra. Sound by construction: `op $rX,$rX,...` reads every source before the write. `all` is the normal spelling. |
| `IDO52_UGEN_FORCE_TARGET_SAFE_DIV` | `1` | Extends Hook 41b's in-place whitelist with U-code op **0x1d div** (needs `FORCE_TARGET_SAFE` set as well -- it widens the whitelist, it does not enable the hook).  Sound: every expansion we have copies the dividend out before writing the quotient register -- as1 ours AND the IRIX 5.2 as1 binary use `move $at,rX` in the branch slot, ASPSX/`--assembler gas` uses `addu $at,rX,$0`, and a non-pow2 divisor goes through `div $0,rX,rt ; mflo rX`.  Separate from `=all` so no existing user changes, and opt-in because the argument is about the ASSEMBLER's macro expansion rather than about a single opcode.  First consumer: `main/func_800A1414` (`v = r->x / 2` where retail divides in place; ugen reads `occ=4 op=0x1d src=v1 dst=v1 nat=TEMP`). |
| `IDO52_UGEN_FORCE_TARGET_SAFE_EQU` | `1` | (Hook 41b-EQU, 2026-09-26) Extends Hook 41b's in-place whitelist with U-code op **0x23 EQU against an immediate** (`== K`, `!x`), which ugen hands to as1 as the macro `seq rX,rX,K`. Needs `FORCE_TARGET_SAFE` set as well (occurrence list or `all`); like `_DIV` it widens the whitelist, it does not enable the hook. Sound: the Hook 41 site is ugen's binary-op-with-immediate arm, where BOTH arms overwrite `dst` with the node's value (the TEMP arm computes into a pool temp and then emits `or dst,temp,$0`), so the liveness of `dst`'s old value is ugen's own decision and Hook 41b never changes it -- the same argument the and/or/sll/sra entries rest on. What remains is whether the expansion reads its source after writing it, and every as1 `seq`-with-immediate expansion reads `rX` in its first word: `sltiu rX,rX,1` (K = 0), `xori rX,rX,K ; sltiu rX,rX,1`, `li $at,K ; xor rX,rX,$at ; sltiu rX,rX,1` (wide K). 64-bit operand types (Idt/Kdt/Wdt, ugen's own 0x5010000 dword mask) are excluded. Implemented as `ugen_target_safe_node()` (post-pass `apply_hook41b_equ`, marker `ugen_target_safe_equ`). |
| `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_INPLACE` | `1` (or `<occ>,...` -- Hook 36c, below) | Hook 36b: a `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=<occ>:<reg>` force may name a register that is NOT a pool temp (an argument register holding the operand). It is handed out without joining the temp lists (the pop site skips `f_append_to_list`). Probe-grade: sound only when the old value is dead once the expression is evaluated. Not needed once 41b applies -- prefer 41b. |

**What it closes:** the narrow-parameter class. Retail: `sll a0,a0,16; sra a0,a0,16`
(s16 param), `andi a2,a2,0xffff` (u16 param), `sll v0,v0,16; sra v0,v0,16` ((s16)
conversion of a web), `ori v0,v0,2; andi v0,v0,0xff` (u8 RMW in a web). Ours
went through a pool temp (`sll t6,a0,16; sra a0,t6,16`, `andi t8,a1; move a1,t8`)
because ugen's own in-place predicate (the op bitmap at 0x10005214) says TEMP
for these ops when src == dst. `IDO52_UGEN_TARGET_TRACE=1` shows each decision
(`op=0x73 src=a0 dst=a0 nat=TEMP`). First consumer: main/func_800A10D8
(libgpu DR_MODE builder) BYTE-PERFECT with `FORCE_TARGET_SAFE=all` +
`AS1_NO_LA_PULL=all`. Partial: func_80091080 (parameter head now exact; the
per-table `sll` recompute and empty delay slots remain), func_800A2C98 13/15
(the `li at,-3; and` 32-bit clear is a value-range fold, not this class).
Note the occurrence counter shifts: a DIRECT decision removes a temp request,
so Hook 36 indices downstream move by one.

**Hook 41b-EQU census (2026-09-26, run W10).** `apply_ugen_hooks.py` gained
the idempotent post-pass `apply_hook41b_equ` (applied on both the fresh and the
already-hooked path; a second run reports "all hooks present" and leaves
`ugen.c` byte-identical) and a `--rebuild` flag (recompiles `ugen.c` and
replaces `build/5.2/out/ugen`, same recipe as the Hook 66 applier).
Validation, direct `cc` A/B against the pre-change ugen binary, each file with
its own `@CompileEnv` / `@CompileFlags`:
(a) env unset: 30 random hook-using overlay TUs, 30/30 objects `cmp`-identical;
(b) every `src/` file whose `@CompileEnv` uses `FORCE_TARGET_SAFE` (124 files,
123 IDO + 1 gcc skipped): 123/123 identical -- and still 123/123 identical
with `IDO52_UGEN_FORCE_TARGET_SAFE_EQU=1` forced on for all of them, so no
existing user has an EQU-vs-immediate in-place candidate at a selected site;
(c) applier re-run: no-op, `ugen.c` md5 unchanged.
All three expansion forms were checked on a tiny TU (`a == 0`, `b == 5`,
`c == 70000` with the formals targeted in place): `xori a1,a1,5 ; sltiu a1,a1,1`
and `lui at ; ori at ; xor a2,a2,at ; sltiu a2,a2,1`, values correct.
First measured effect: GYOZI/func_801401BC's W6 layout, term 5
`sltiu t5,t5,1` (trace `occ=31 op=0x23 src=t5 dst=t5`) with
`FORCE_TARGET_SAFE=31 FORCE_TARGET_SAFE_EQU=1` plus a Hook 36 force putting
the xor temp in t5: 260 -> 262/272. The function was then closed by a
source respelling that makes the whole term a register-register EQU (as1's
`seq` macro already expands `xor rX ; sltiu rX,rX,1` in place), so the shipped
file does not use the hook; it stays available, opt-in, for the next
`== K` / `!x` in-place wall.

## Hook 36c: per-occurrence `TEMP_REG_INPLACE` + in-place safety check (2026-09-25)

⛔ **Hook 36b's `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_INPLACE=1` is GLOBAL.** Every Hook 36 force
whose register is not in ugen's free list is then handed out in place, whether
or not it was the force the flag was added for. On main/mascot_move that
grabbed t2..t5 (held by Hook 37-coloured uopt webs) thirteen times and computed
`andi t3,v0,0x3f` for `& 0x7f`, behind a 178/238 score that was a miscompile
(honest re-fit: 115/238; see that file's batch-3 note).

Applied by `apply_ugen_hooks.py` (post-pass `apply_hook36c`, after 36b; marker
`ugen_tempreg_inplace_listed`). The post-`make clean` chain is unchanged. All
new code runs inside the in-place branch, which needs the env var, so env unset
is bit-identical.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_INPLACE` | `1` / `all` | Unchanged: every out-of-list force may go in place (Hook 36b), now through the safety check below. |
| `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_INPLACE` | `<occ>[,<occ>...]` or `occ:<occ>[,...]` | **Only the listed Hook 36 occurrences** (the same index as `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG`) may go in place; any other out-of-list force falls back to the default head pop, as it does without the flag. A bare `1` means global, so **write `occ:1` to name occurrence 1 by itself**. `0` = off. |

**Safety check** (both forms). Before an in-place grab, ugen's own register state
is consulted and the grab is **REFUSED** (default pop, `NOT FREE` line follows)
when the register:

1. is on ugen's in-use temp list (0x10019a98), meaning a live ugen expression temp;
2. has a ugen descriptor (0x10019520 + r*12) holding a pending use count (+4)
   for a DIFFERENT tree node. Two holdings are allowed: the very node being
   allocated (main/_spu_r_'s call result already in v0: `INPLACE OK ... already
   holds this node`), and an operand of it (node +0/+4) on its last use;
3. is a **t-register outside ugen's temp pool**. ugen withholds a t-register from
   the pool only because uopt coloured webs into that range, so a grab there
   takes a web's register. This is the mascot_move class, and it is refused on
   every such grab;
4. is an **s-register**. These are never ugen temps: the register belongs to a
   coloured web, or it is not saved by the prologue.

⚠️ **What it cannot check:** ugen has **no per-point liveness for uopt webs**. The
t- and s-register tests work at function level. They are conservative: they
refuse a grab even where that web happens to be dead, and they cannot name the
web, so the line prints `live in web ?`. A **v0/v1/a0-a3** grab with no ugen
descriptor state cannot be checked at all: it prints `INPLACE UNCHECKED` and its
soundness still rests on the byte/resolver gates. Every shipped grab falls into
this class. The remedy is the per-occurrence form, which confines the risk to
the one occurrence you meant.

**Trace lines** (`IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_TRACE=1`, alongside the Hook 36 lines):
```
[UGEN] TEMP_REG_INPLACE: 1 listed occurrence(s): 24          (always, list form)
[UGEN-TREG36] INPLACE REFUSED occ=5 reg=r11: live in web ? -- t-register withheld ...   (always)
[UGEN-TREG36] INPLACE REFUSED occ=N reg=rR: live ugen temp (on the in-use list, ...)     (always)
[UGEN-TREG36] INPLACE REFUSED occ=N reg=rR: ugen descriptor holds another value ...      (always)
[UGEN-TREG36] INPLACE UNCHECKED occ=24 reg=r6: v/a register -- ...  (trace)
[UGEN-TREG36] INPLACE OK occ=2 reg=r2: already holds this node (uses=1)  (trace)
[UGEN-TREG36] INPLACE NOT LISTED occ=N reg=rR                    (trace, list form)
[UGEN-TREG36] occ=24 -> r6 FORCED IN PLACE (not a pool temp)     (trace, unchanged)
```

**Inertness proof (2026-09-25):**
- All 13 `src/` users of `=1` produce object files byte-identical to the
  pre-change ugen, and the same objects again with the per-occurrence lines below.
- `build_level3_c.py --no-cache`: 1195/1195, Mismatched 0, SHA1 `2d8d4748...`.
- Regression set: RPG_BAT/func_80154DD8, GYOZI/func_8013EE14,
  TAIIKU/func_8013BB9C and TACO/func_8015ACDC are ✅ BYTE-PERFECT;
  touzyou_fan_disp, schedule_day_main, wipe_disp and system_config are
  BYTE-PERFECT.
- mascot_move with `INPLACE=24`: 115/238, Body 238, one in-place grab (24→a2).
- Negative control: the same file with t-register forces restored
  (5:t3,6:t5,11:t4,20:t2,50:t0) gives 5× `INPLACE REFUSED ... t-register withheld`.
  The pre-change ugen handed out all five.

**In-place grab census.** Each file compiled with its own env; `FORCED IN PLACE`
count and the proposed per-occurrence line, each proven to give an object
identical to `=1`:

| File | grabs (occ→reg) | proposed |
|---|---|---|
| main/CdControlF | 1 (2→v1) | `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_INPLACE=2` |
| main/CdMix | 2 (1→v1, 5→a0) | `=1,5` |
| main/_spu_r_ | 1 (2→v0, INPLACE OK: already holds the node) | `=2` |
| main/bustup_kuchi_ani | 2 (13→a0, 25→v0) | `=13,25` |
| main/tel_code_check | 4 (1,6,12,18→a0) | `=1,6,12,18` |
| main/serial_cmd | 2 (3,6→a0) | `=3,6` |
| main/func_8008BE0C | 1 (3→a0) | `=3` |
| main/func_80095D90 | 2 (4→v1, 12→v0) | `=4,12` |
| main/func_800A0180 | 1 (8→v0) | `=8` |
| main/icon_atari_check | 1 (1→a0) | `=occ:1` |
| main/normal_date_bg_out_sub | 1 (9→v0) | `=9` |
| RPG_BAT/func_80154DD8 | 1 (21→v0) | `=21` |
| main/mascot_move (semi) | 1 (24→a2) | `=24` |

Every shipped grab is a v/a register (none is a t- or s-register), so the
safety check changes nothing for them. None has a stray grab: each file's
grabs are exactly the forces it names.
