## Hook 47: IDO52_NO_BINFOLD_OPCODES — uopt binary constant-fold veto (2026-09-17)

⚠️ **BUILT, SOUND, AND NOT IN THE POST-`make clean` CHAIN.** `uopt.c` is
pristine without it. Apply it deliberately when you want it:

```bash
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_constfold_hooks.py \
        tools/ido-static-recomp/build/5.2/uopt.c --rebuild
```

**Site.** uopt's expression-simplification walker `func_414394` (reached from
`f_copypropagate`) calls `f_binaryfold` once both operands of a binary U-code
node have resolved to literal constants; `f_binaryfold` replaces the node with
the computed constant, and the later CONST_INREG pass serves that constant out
of whatever register already holds it. For `x & 0xFF` where propagation has
proved `x == 255` the visible result is a bare `move`. The hook declines the
fold for selected opcodes by taking the function's OWN pre-existing bail-out —
the branch already used when two constant operands have differing machine
types — so the node is simply left unfolded.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_NO_BINFOLD_OPCODES` | `OPC[:FROM[:TO]][,...]` | Decline the constant fold of those U-code opcodes (`_opc_names` mnemonic — `and`=4, `ior`=60, `xor`=141, `add`=1, `shl`=115 … — or a raw number). `FROM`/`TO` are a 1-based occurrence window over that opcode's *eligible* folds in the uopt run (default all). Counter is global per run: one function per TU when windowing. |
| `IDO52_NO_BINFOLD_DEBUG` | `1` \| `2` | `1` prints each denied fold; `2` prints every constant-constant fold the site sees (opcode + both operand values) — that is how you read off the occurrence index. |

**Soundness.** The hook only ever DECLINES an optimisation, on a path the
function already takes, so it cannot delete, move or invent code. Guarded on
both operands being literal-constant leaves of the same node class. With the
env unset the inserted code is one false `if`, so output is bit-identical to
the unhooked compiler (verified on the reduced case and on
`main/pad_type_check`).

⛔ **What it does NOT do, and why it exists.** It was built to serve the
`held_constant_mask_fold` blocker's stated "wanted primitive" (a uopt veto for
the and-with-mask simplification, sibling of Hook 29) and it **REFUTES that
request**. On `main/pad_type_check` word 33 it turns retail-mismatching
`move v1,a1` into `li v1,255` — still not retail's `andi v1,a1,0xff` — because
**ugen** then folds `AND(const,const)` itself (`f_fold_constant`, opcode case
4). Additionally vetoing ugen's fold (tried as a temporary patch) gives a
three-word `li $3,255; and $8,$3,255; move $3,$8`, strictly worse. So no pair
of fold vetoes reaches that word.

The positive finding from the same investigation, worth more than the hook:
**IDO emits `andi rD,rS,0xff` only when the AND's operand has a NON-CONSTANT
REACHING DEFINITION at that use.** Constant propagation here is per-use
reaching-defs, not web-wide — remove the non-constant arm from the witness
below and the identical source folds to `move`:

```c
if (x == 0) { n = 1; limit = K; } else { limit = 255; }
if (x == limit) { if (H) goto c; }
n = limit & 0xFF;            /* -> li $3,255 ; and $2,$3,255 */
```

Also established on that pass: the **IRIX IDO 5.2 binaries under qemu-irix
fold identically**, so a surviving redundant `andi` on a provably-constant
operand is never a recomp artifact — go look for the non-constant reaching
def in the source instead of reaching for a hook.

---

## Hooks 48 / 49: IDO52_AS1_KEEP_SEXT / IDO52_AS1_NO_BRANCH_FOLD — as1 pass-II fold vetoes (2026-09-22)

Applied by `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_hooks.py` (already in the
post-`make clean` chain; no new applier step). Both default OFF; with the env
unset the helper returns before counting, so as1 is behaviour-identical
(verified 2026-09-22: `build_level3_c.py` 1089/1089, retail SHA1
`2d8d4748…`; 16 registered main files using other `IDO52_AS1_*` hooks and 5
overlay files all still BYTE-PERFECT).

**Where they live.** `func_419268` (the per-BB pass-II peephole driven by
`f_do_passII_opt`) dispatches each instruction on its major opcode:

| instruction | handler | hook |
|---|---|---|
| `sra` (SPECIAL funct 3) | `func_416528` | **48 KEEP_SEXT** |
| `beq`/`bne` (4, 5) | `func_4179f0` | **49 NO_BRANCH_FOLD** |
| `andi`/`ori` (12, 13) | `func_416ca8` | 29 KEEP_ANDI / 45 FORCE_NARROW_FOLD |
| `sll` (funct 0) | `func_41673c` | **50 NO_SHIFT_COMBINE** |
| `and` (funct 0x24) | `func_416ac4` | — |
| `addiu` (9) | `func_4171dc` | — |

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_SEXT` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` (0-based; a bare number is an index) | Veto the sign-extension-pair fold: `func_416528` turns `sll rX,rY,N ; sra rX,rX,N` into a move (both halves via `f_change_bb`) when the destination's demanded bits fit in 32-N — e.g. an s16 extension whose only consumers are `sh` and `andi 0xffff`. |
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_SEXT_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-KSEXT] #N word=… rd rt sa [-> VETOED]` per sra reaching the test |
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_BRANCH_FOLD` | same | Veto the branch/def fold: `func_4179f0` looks up the compared register's reaching def and, dispatching on its internal opcode, rewrites the branch and lets the def die — `xori v0,v0,1 ; beqz v0` → `bnez v0` (def_op 0x59); single-bit `and ; beqz` → `sll ; bgez` (def_op 0x18, incl. the sign bit). |
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_BRANCH_FOLD_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-NBFOLD] #N branch=… def=… def_op=0x.. [-> VETOED]` |

Both vetoes take the handler's own `return 0` ("no change") exit — the same
control flow as an unfoldable candidate — so they are subtractive and sound
by construction. Indices are per translation unit in arrival order and shift
when the stream changes; pass II iterates, so re-derive with the trace.

**Consumers (2026-09-22):**
- `main/func_8008C7A0` **BYTE-PERFECT 32/32** (was 12/28): KEEP_SEXT=all plus
  retail's temp-then-move shape — `FORCE_TARGET_SAFE=1,2,4`,
  `UGEN_NO_TARGET=5,7`, `UGEN_TEMP_REG=1:t2,2:t2,3:t3,4:t3`,
  `AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP=26,27,28,29`, `AS1_NO_COPY_PROP=all` (every token
  proven load-bearing).
- `main/SpuVmDamperOff` **BYTE-PERFECT 54/54** (was 52/54):
  `NO_BRANCH_FOLD=0` — its "as1 turns `and`+`beqz` into `sll`+`bgez`"
  wall was this fold.
- `main/ContRpn1` 6/19 (Body 18) → 15/19 count-exact: the "sign-test
  fusion" wall was this fold, NOT an ASPSX tell.  Later the same day
  **BYTE-PERFECT 19/19**: the ugen `$25` indirect-call register is only
  f_get_dest's hint (`IDO52_UGEN_DEST_TRACE`: `occ=2 ... hint=25`), and
  Hook 42 `IDO52_UGEN_DEST_TEMP=2` turns it into a plain free-list temp
  (`lw t7,56(t6) ; jalr t7`, the AND then pops t8 — retail exactly).
- `main/_spu_r_` 35/49 → 44/49: `NO_BRANCH_FOLD=1` with the Hook 36b
  `TEMP_REG=2:v0` INPLACE force keeps retail's in-place `xori`; the
  remaining 5 words are an as1 bb-2 list-scheduler order that
  start-time (not tie) comparisons decide.

A blind `=all` sweep of every IDO open semi found no other improver (col2mono,
MouseState, mascot_move, seq_start, swap_env, vload_font … regress), so
use occurrence lists, not `all`, unless the trace shows one candidate.

## Hook 50: IDO52_AS1_NO_SHIFT_COMBINE — as1 pass-II sll-of-sll combine veto (2026-09-22)

Applied by `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_hooks.py` (same applier, same
post-`make clean` chain; its helper `NSCOMB_HELPER` is inserted before
`func_41673c` and reuses Hook 48's `as1_occ_sel`). Default OFF; with the env
unset the helper returns before counting.

**The transform.** `func_41673c` (the `sll` handler of `func_419268`) looks up
the source register's reaching def (`func_416304`); if it is itself an `sll`
(internal op 2, +36) with no pending state and the summed shift amount is
< 32, it `f_change_bb()`s the def into a move and rewrites this `sll` to shift
the def's source by the sum: `sll t8,s0,3 ; sll t9,t8,16` →
`move t8,s0 ; sll t9,s0,19`, and copy-prop then deletes the move (one word
fewer). The hook sits at `L41687c`, after every legality test and immediately
before the `f_change_bb`; a veto takes the handler's own `v0 = 0` /
`L416954` "no change" exit — subtractive, sound by construction.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_SHIFT_COMBINE` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` (0-based) | Veto the sll-of-sll combine at candidate N (arrival order per TU). |
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_SHIFT_COMBINE_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-NSCOMB] #N sll=<outer word> def=<def word> sum=<M+N> [-> VETOED]` |

**Validation (2026-09-22):** env unset → `f4.o` bit-identical to the pre-hook
as1 on the consumer TU; `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` 1103/1103, 0
mismatched, retail SHA1 `2d8d4748…`; a sample of overlay files using other
`IDO52_AS1_*` hooks unchanged.

**Consumer:** `main/func_8008F8A4` **BYTE-PERFECT 75/75** (was 29/75, Body 74,
Fixup 2): `NO_SHIFT_COMBINE=all` (one candidate: `sll=0018cc00
def=0010c0c0 sum=19`, i.e. `(s16)(i*8)` = `sll t8,s0,3 ; sll t9,t8,16 ; sra`)
restores the missing word; `u16 *` for the SPU record (retail `ori s7,zero,
0x80ff`, not `addiu -32513`) removed the Fixup; the loop block then fitted
with `TIE_SRC_ORDER=bb:2` + re-fitted TIE_TAKE/REJECT lists.

## Hook 51: IDO52_AS1_NO_JUMP_INVERT — as1 jump-over-branch inversion veto (2026-09-22)

Applied by `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_hooks.py` (same applier, same
post-`make clean` chain; its helper `NJINV_HELPER` is inserted before
`func_41b5e8` and reuses Hook 48's `as1_occ_sel`). Default OFF; with the env
unset the helper returns before counting.

**The transform.** `func_41b5e8` is a per-procedure branch clean-up that
`f_traverse_bb` calls before pass I — it is NOT `f_do_branch_opt`,
`f_do_forward_branches`, `f_do_xbb_opt`, `f_gen_br_patches` or
`f_do_handle_branchop` (all previously eliminated). For each block it
classifies the terminating branch (`func_41aa60`); class 2 is "unconditional
`b L` whose target block is a lone conditional branch". If that conditional's
target (`func_41b444`) is the jumping block's own fall-through successor,
`func_41b22c` copies the conditional into the jump's slot with the condition
inverted (`func_41b12c`) and retargets it past `L`:

    b    L        ; entry            bnez a0, L+8
    sw   ra,20(sp)             ->    sw   ra,20(sp)
    C0:  ...      ; fall-through     C0:  ...
    L:   beqz a0,C0                  L:   beqz a0,C0   (now unreachable)

Retail (ASPSX-era library code) keeps the `b`. The hook sits at `L41ba98`
after the fall-through test and before `func_41b22c`; a veto jumps to
`L41bbb0`, the path `func_41b5e8` takes when `func_41b22c` declines —
subtractive, sound by construction. Class 1 (jump-to-jump threading) is not
touched.

**How it was found:** a private as1 with every call in `f_traverse_bb`
env-gated, then every call in `func_41b5e8`; only `func_41aa60` /
`func_41b444` / `func_41b22c` changed the entry word. The same method
(generate a gated copy of `build/5.2/as1.c` with a regex, link against
`version_info.o` + `libc_impl_52.o`, run from a copied `out/` dir) is the
fastest way to localise any future as1 transform.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_JUMP_INVERT` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` (0-based) | Veto the inversion at candidate N (candidates that passed the fall-through test, arrival order per TU). |
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_JUMP_INVERT_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-NJINV] #N jump=<b word> target_branch=<bcc word> [-> VETOED]` |

**Validation (2026-09-22):** env unset → 16 TUs (two matched libgs emitters,
both consumers, 12 overlay files carrying other `IDO52_AS1_*` hooks)
bit-identical to the pre-hook as1; `build_level3_c.py` 1114/1114, 0
mismatched, retail SHA1 `2d8d4748…`.

**Consumers:**
- `main/CD_readsync` **BYTE-PERFECT 68/68** (was 67/68): one candidate
  (`jump=10000000 target_branch=10800000`), `NO_JUMP_INVERT=all` on top of
  `XBB_SKIP_CAND=all NO_FALLTHRU_FILL=all`.
- `main/func_80098650` **BYTE-PERFECT 28/28** (was 24/28): with the `b` kept,
  the beqz slot fill is a pure boost-candidate choice —
  `IDO52_AS1_XBB_PICK_TRACE` lists #4 = the next macro's `li at,1` (retail's
  fill) and #5 = `li t7,1` for the beq slot (retail leaves it empty), so
  `XBB_SKIP_CAND=0,1,2,3,5`. `NO_BRANCH_OPT=1` was no longer load-bearing.

## NOT LANDED: `hasstore` force / back-edge reload for a register parameter (2026-09-22)

Proposed after func_800ABFD8 / func_800AC364 (retail: `move v1,a1`, no
preheader store, reload of the parameter home on the loop BACK EDGE).
The gate is f_globalcolor's rlods/rstrs pass: for a block that reloads a
web, has no store of it (`store_count == 0`, `!firstisstr`) and needs
its home valid at exit (`needregsave`), a live range with `hasstore`
set clears `phi_s1` (recomp label `L4705b0`, `s1 = zero`), which vetoes
the reload-to-predecessor move (f_somepremember / f_allpreppout /
f_put_in_jump_bb -- the jump-edge insert also needs `-Wo,-createbb`).
A register parameter's range always has `hasstore` (entry LiveUnit is
`firstisstr`).

**Forcing `hasstore = 0` there (or keeping `phi_s1`) is UNSOUND.**  After
the move, the rstr-sinking second pass drops the preheader's store
(head is `unkDA` and in `livebbs`), so the home is only valid again if
EVERY successor of the head stores before any reload.  On func_800ABFD8
arm B's entry block is not in the parameter's split piece (f_split's
addadjacents heur-A/B admission keeps it out), stores nothing, and the
back-edge `lw v1,204(sp)` reads an uninitialised home on the first
arm-B iteration -- a real miscompile, one word short of retail (the
missing word is retail's arm-B store).  A guarded variant (abort unless
every head successor keeps the web in the same register, does not
reload and stores) is sound by construction but refuses both target
functions, so nothing was landed; the prototype applier is kept out of
tree.  Retail's shape is sound -- its arm-B entry IS in the piece -- so
the open question is the split admission, not `hasstore`.

`-Wo,-createbb` alone: blind flag sweep over 208 compiling IDO semis
(≤400 words) -- LIVE on 12, net-positive on 1 (func_80093A88, +3 on a
body 140 words short of target: noise), negative on the rest.

## Hook 52: IDO52_AS1_KEEP_DEAD_RELOAD — as1 dead-instruction removal veto (2026-09-22)

Applied by `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_hooks.py` (same applier, same
post-`make clean` chain). The veto sits inside `f_redundant`; its helper
`as1_kdrel_veto` is PROTOTYPED before `f_redundant` (0x415988, which precedes
Hook 48's `as1_occ_sel`) and DEFINED before `func_419268`. Default OFF; with
the env unset the helper returns before counting.

**The transform.** `func_419268` (the per-BB pass-II peephole) calls
`f_redundant` at `L41936c` for every instruction record not marked keep
(+33). `f_redundant` asks `func_414570` which register the instruction
defines and `func_413624` whether that register is dead at this point
(overwritten before any use; both halves for a hi/lo pair), and if so calls
`f_change_bb(idx, 0, 5, 0)` — deletes the instruction — and returns 1.
That is how a reload uopt emits after a call, but which is re-read after the
next call before any use, disappears. Retail keeps it: the libgs emitter
family 0x800AA060.. has a dead `lw v1,<work home>` right after its first
`jal`. Located by run O with a private as1 that skipped the call (the
deletion was the entire count residual of six functions).

The hook sits at `L415a8c`, after every legality test and immediately before
`f_change_bb`; a veto takes `f_redundant`'s own `v0 = 0` exit (`L415aec`) —
exactly the path for an instruction whose destination is live, after which
`func_419268` tries copy-prop on it as usual. Subtractive: it only keeps a
word the compiler emitted, never creates one.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_DEAD_RELOAD` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` (0-based) | Keep the instruction at candidate N (f_redundant's positive verdicts, arrival order per TU). |
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_DEAD_RELOAD_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-KDREL] #N rec=<record> word=<insn> dead_reg=<r> [-> KEPT]` |

**Validation (2026-09-22):** env unset → `build_level3_c.py` full rebuild
(0/1118 cache hits after the toolchain change) SHA1 `2d8d4748…`, 0
mismatched, 0 non-reloc fixups (covers the 104 registered main files carrying
`IDO52_AS1_*` hooks); all 92 byte-perfect overlay files carrying an
`IDO52_AS1_*` hook still resolve BYTE-PERFECT.

**Consumers (all BYTE-PERFECT, raw body == target, no swaps, no paints):**
`main/func_800AA060` 84/84, `func_800AB630` 101/101, `func_800AC6F0`
110/110, `func_800AD2AC` 122/122, `func_800ADFA4` 129/129, `func_800AEC94`
138/138, `func_800AFAC8` 155/155 — each has exactly one candidate (#0, the
`lw v1` after the first call). The rest of the recipe on top of run O's
colours: an unused top scalar + pad sized to retail's locals, Hook 36 forces
mapped request-by-request against `cc -S` (each unaligned `ulw` copy burns
one request on its `$at` half, so the numbering skips), `IDO52_AS1_TIE_TAKE`
chains that put the three spill stores before the local-address
computations ahead of each call, and a residual `remap_stack_slots` for ra
and the spill block. The "no-callee-saved caller-spill allocator" blocker
class is void: the naive per-call save/restore strategy is ordinary IDO 5.2
output once the webs are coloured off the s-registers and this one deletion
is vetoed.

## Hook 53: IDO52_SPLIT_ADMIT / IDO52_SPLIT_REJECT — f_split piece admission (2026-09-22)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_splitadmit_hooks.py [uopt.c] [--rebuild]`.
⚠️ **Add it to the post-`make clean` chain** (after `apply_uopt_hooks.py`,
whose Hook 43 comment block it uses as its helper anchor; it is independent
of the web/readphase appliers). Idempotent (marker `ido52_split_admit_init`).
Default OFF: with the env unset every inserted line is an untaken branch.

**The decision.** `f_split(lr, &rest, class, flag)` (recomp `L46cd98`) turns
the live range being coloured into a PIECE and moves everything else into a
fresh remainder range (new bitpos; the piece KEEPS the original bitpos). It
picks a start LiveUnit (first one with `firstisstr` in a block that still
has a free colour, else any with occurrences), then grows the piece by BFS
over **successor** edges only (graphnode+24), skipping expansion from blocks
where the value is dead-out (lu+23), `is_cup` blocks (flag arm) and blocks
ending in an indirect jump (op 0x31). `f_addadjacents` (`L46b6ac`) is the
per-candidate admission test. It computes `shared = f_marksharedintf(...)`
(interferences the block adds), `before = piece.colorsleft (lr+33)`, applies
`f_updateforbidden`/`f_updatecolorsleft`, and then:

| mode | admit iff |
|---|---|
| heuristic ON (`0x1001eab0 != 0`, the default) | `shared < before && 2*colorsleft_after >= piece.numintf(lr+36) + shared` |
| heuristic OFF (`-Wo,-noheurAB` clears the flag) | `colorsleft_after != 0` |

Admit (`L46b858`) and reject (`L46b988`, restores lr+33/+40/+44) share all
other bookkeeping, and f_split afterwards rebuilds both interference lists
from the `+9 -> +8` marks, re-files both ranges on the constrained /
unconstrained worklists and leaves colouring, spilling and the rlods/rstrs
boundary store/reload placement to the unmodified allocator.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_SPLIT_ADMIT` | `BITPOS:BB[,...]` (`BB` = bb number or `*`) | while growing the piece whose bitpos is BITPOS, decide candidate block BB by the heuristic-OFF rule. Never admits a block that would leave the piece with no colour. |
| `IDO52_SPLIT_REJECT` | `BITPOS:BB[,...]` | keep block BB out of that piece (the natural reject path). |
| `IDO52_SPLIT_TRACE` | `1` | `[SPLIT] start / admit? (shared, colorsleft before->after, numintf, heurON, heurOFF, natural verdict) / expand / piece=N blocks: ...` |

**Soundness (by construction).** A REJECT is the path the BFS takes for any
failing candidate — stopping the piece anywhere is always legal. An ADMIT
is exactly the choice uopt makes for every block under its own
`-noheurAB` option (colours left must stay > 0). Neither touches the
LiveUnit flags, `hasstore`, or the store/reload placement — the class of
change that made the 2026-09-22 `hasstore` force unsound (section above).
Keys: BITPOS is the Hook 28/37 bittab key of the piece at split time (the
remainder's bitpos is freshly allocated and is only stable while earlier
decisions are unchanged); BB is graphnode+8, stable per compile.

**Workflow.** `IDO52_SPLIT_TRACE=1` lists every decision; flip one at a time
(REJECT on a natural ADMIT, ADMIT on a heuristic-only REJECT) and score.
`tools/analysis/sweep_split_admit.py FUNC 0xADDR SIZE src.c [--no-colour]`
does it greedily (split and, unless `--no-colour`, Hook 37 colour candidates
in one search) and prints the directive.

**Validation (2026-09-22):** env unset → `build_level3_c.py` full rebuild
(0/1137 cache hits) SHA1 `2d8d4748…`, 0 mismatched, 0 non-reloc fixups;
raw-object identity with the pre-hook uopt on 7,504 IDO TUs (998 registered
main + 6,506 overlay/semi sources: every object byte-identical).

**Consumers (libgs emitter family, 2026-09-22):**

| function | before | with Hook 53 | shipped |
|---|---|---|---|
| `func_800AD4A0` | 14/191, body 192 | 70/191, body 191 (count-exact) | `REJECT=16:2,12:1,29:1` + Hook 36 → 77/191 |
| `func_800AD79C` | 18/192, body 192 | 62/194, body 194 (count-exact) | `REJECT=8:1,132:2` + Hook 36 + `AS1_NO_COPY_PROP=all` → 92/194 |
| `func_800AF1F8` | 72/208 | 92/208 (`REJECT=19:1`) | **BYTE-PERFECT 208/208 — without Hook 53**, see below |
| `func_800ABFD8` / `func_800AC364` | 35/227 | ≤ 36 | nothing: the vtx wall is the register-parameter back-edge reload (`hasstore`), not piece admission; admitting arm B (`ADMIT=24:9` / `24:10`) makes the piece uncolourable (every caller-saved colour 1e20, s-registers all taken) and it is re-split |
| `func_800AEEC0` | 5/204 | 7/204 | not shipped (no colour levers fitted yet) |

In every useful case the lever is a REJECT: our allocator puts the
loop-invariant slot pointers' entry block, preheader and loop head in ONE
piece, which is then priced out of every caller-saved colour and spilled
whole, where retail cuts at the preheader (entry piece in v1/t0 stored in
the preheader, a separate head piece reloaded at the loop head).

**What the split exposed — the route that closed func_800AF1F8.**
Once the pieces are retail's, `cc -S` (ugen output) is retail's shape —
`lw $3,148($sp)` at the head, `move $7,$3` for the argument, `move $8,$5`
for the vertex base — and **as1's copy propagation** is what folds the
copies away (`lw a3,...` / `sw a1,228(sp)`). `IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP=all`
restores `move t0,a1`; `IDO52_AS1_NO_COPY_PROP=<n>` (the `or a3,v1,zero`
candidate from `IDO52_AS1_COPY_PROP_TRACE=1`) restores `move a3,v1`.
Two uopt facts learned on the way, both for the "precolour" family of
walls: (1) `lu+19` is the argument/parameter register a LiveUnit is passed
in (set by f_makelivranges/f_localcolor via `f_passedinreg`, gated by
`0x1001eaa8`, which `-Wo,-noprecolor` clears); (2) f_cupcosts prices any
range containing such a LiveUnit at 1e20 for colours in `0x1001e608`
(v0, v1, ra), and the "generic" caller-saved cost it reuses for the other
t-registers is computed on v1 — so a precoloured range can only take its own
argument register or an s-register naturally. `-Wo,-noprecolor` lifts that
(the head piece then takes v1 on its own).  On func_800AF1F8,
`-Wo,-noprecolor` alone produces retail's pieces (the split override
became redundant and was ablated out), and the closing env is
`-noprecolor` + `IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP=all` + `IDO52_AS1_NO_COPY_PROP=2`
+ 8 Hook 36 forces + one `IDO52_AS1_TIE_TAKE`, with remap/swaps for the
save slots and the prologue/preheader order (file header has the
ablation).  **Why Hook 53 cracked the case anyway:** its trace is what
showed the pieces were wrong, and the cost dump behind them pointed at
lu+19.  Try `-Wo,-noprecolor` first on any sibling whose loop-head
reload lands in an argument register; reach for SPLIT_ADMIT/REJECT when
the pieces are still wrong after that.

## Hook 54: IDO52_AS1_FTF_BACKWARD — fall-through fill after a backward branch (2026-09-22)

Applied by `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_delay_hooks.py` (already in the
post-`make clean` chain; no new applier step). Default OFF; with the env
unset the helper returns before counting (as1 object-identical, verified).

**The decision.** as1's fall-through fill (`func_41d7b4`, Hook 27's
`NO_FALLTHRU_FILL` site) drops the next block's first word into the previous
block's `branch ; nop` slot. Whether a block is a candidate is recorded when
it is emitted, in `bb+70` bit `0x02`, as: slot is a nop AND the branch is not
`beq x,x` AND **its displacement field is >= 0** — i.e. a FORWARD branch
(label still unresolved, field 0). The hook lifts only that last test, at the
`L41e588` computation. Every legality test still runs on the donated word:
`f_eligible`, `f_cannot_trap`, `func_41d22c` (`f_defuse`: must not define
anything live at the branch TARGET — `f_find_branch_target` resolves backward
targets like forward ones — and must not load from memory), and the
pc-contiguity check before the rewind. `func_41d22c` has no test against the
branch's own operands for forward branches either: `beq v0,at,L ; li at,2` is
legal MIPS I (the branch reads its operands before the slot executes) and as1
emits it natively after a forward branch. The rewind does not move the
backward branch, whose displacement is already encoded.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_FTF_BACKWARD` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` (0-based; ⚠️ a bare `1` means `all` in this parser — write `occ:1`) | mark the N-th backward conditional branch (that failed only the direction test) fall-through fillable |
| `IDO52_AS1_FTF_BACKWARD_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-FTFB] #N bb=<block> branch=<word> [-> PERMITTED]` |

**Consumer:** `main/func_8009FF08` **BYTE-PERFECT 90/90** (was 82/90, Body
91): `IDO52_AS1_FTF_BACKWARD=occ:0` — the goto dispatch's `beq v0,at,case_1`
(backward) takes the next `li at,2`; candidate #1 (the `case_2` beq) is then
refused by as1 itself because `ra` is live at case_2 — retail's nop. The old
header's "XBB boost refuses the write-after-read" diagnosis was wrong: the
retail fill is the fall-through fill.

**ASPSX rule observed in retail (for gccwrap, not as1):** in library code
assembled by ASPSX, a branch slot takes the NEXT word when that word writes
only `$at` — even when the branch reads `$at` (`beq v0,at ; li at,2`,
`beqz at ; lui at,..`, `beqz v1 ; li at,1`) — and is copy-and-bumped when the
next word writes the branch's operand register (`beqz v0 ; li v0,60` is not
filled from below). `--ido-copy-and-bump` models only the second half
(func_8008B93C, func_800A3320).

## Hook 55: IDO52_NO_JUMP_MERGE — uopt jump straightening veto (2026-09-22)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_jumpmerge_hooks.py [uopt.c] [--rebuild]`.
⚠️ **Add it to the post-`make clean` chain** (independent of the other uopt
appliers; its helper anchors on `f_loopunroll`'s definition). Idempotent
(marker `ido52_jmerge_veto`). Default OFF.

**The transform.** Besides unrolling, `f_loopunroll` straightens jumps: a
block B ending in UJP (stmt opcode 0x88) whose target T has exactly one
predecessor and a jump/fall-through tail (opcode in the bitset at
0x100013bc — a block ending in a CALL fails it) gets T's statements spliced
into B (`f_change_adj_node` / `f_post_loopblock` / `f_oneloopblockstmt` /
`f_codeimage`), and the test repeats on the merged block. Runs even with
`-Wo,-loopunroll,0` (only `IDO52_SKIP_PASSES=loopunroll` stopped it). This is
what pulls every CALL-FREE case body of a goto dispatch
(`goto dispatch; case_N: ...; goto done; dispatch: if (x == N) goto case_N;`)
back into the compare chain at -O2 — cfe lowers `if (c) goto L` as a
conditional jump over a UJP stub whose target has one predecessor. The hook
sits at `L477650`, after every test; a veto takes `L477ca4` (advance to the
next block), the path for a target with two predecessors.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_NO_JUMP_MERGE` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` \| `bb:T,...` (a bare number is an INDEX) | veto candidate N / merges into target block T |
| `IDO52_NO_JUMP_MERGE_TRACE` | presence | `[JMERGE] #N from_bb=B to_bb=T tail_op=0x.. [-> VETOED]` |

Sound by construction (declines an optional transform; nothing created).
**Consumer:** `main/func_800A3320` 11/55 → 16/55 (`=all`, 3 candidates):
retail's `b dispatch ; bodies ; beqz/beq/beq` layout is reproduced; the formal
webs renumber with the graph (32/24/2). Residual is ASPSX assembly (entry
schedule, the `$at` slot fill above, frame).

**Where uopt does NOT reorder:** `f_controlflow` builds a DFS block list
(`func_42faa8`, prefers the bb+1 successor), but `f_reemit` emits the
statement CHAIN, which stays in source order through every later pass —
so a layout change that survives into `cc -S` either is a statement-chain
splice (this hook) or happens in ugen (Hook 56).

## Hook 56: IDO52_UGEN_NO_REGION_MOVE — ugen f_labelopt region-move veto (2026-09-22)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_ugen_layout_hooks.py [ugen.c] [--rebuild]`.
⚠️ **Add it to the post-`make clean` chain** (independent of
`apply_ugen_hooks.py`; its helper anchors on `func_435fb0`'s definition).
Idempotent (marker `ugen_rmove_veto`). Default OFF.

**The transform.** ugen's `f_labelopt` (-O1 and up) runs, per procedure,
`func_4351b8` / `func_435908` (jump chains, jump-over-jump), then
`func_435fb0`: walking the tree list region by region (a region starts at a
label, `func_435e94`), when a region ends in UJP (tree opcode 0x88) and the
target region passes `func_435f44` / `func_435e50` / `func_435eec` (cannot be
fallen into, does not fall out), it unlinks the TARGET region and splices it
right after the jump (one move per call); the jump-to-next then disappears.
`case_1: D = 60; goto test; c1_0: ...; return; test: if (x == 0) goto c1_0;
default...` becomes `D = 60 ; test ; default ; c1_0` — retail keeps source
order (`b test ; c1_0 ; test ; default`). Located by gating each call in
`f_labelopt` in a private ugen: only `func_435fb0` changed the layout
(`cfe -> ugen -O0` and uopt's output both keep it).

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_UGEN_NO_REGION_MOVE` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` \| `lab:L,...` (L = ugen's INTERNAL label number, tree +36, as traced — not the `$N` of `cc -S`) | veto the move at candidate N / for target label L |
| `IDO52_UGEN_NO_REGION_MOVE_TRACE` | presence | `[UGEN-RMOVE] #N label=L [-> VETOED]` |

The veto takes `func_435fb0`'s own not-movable path (`L436134`), i.e. the
layout ugen emits at -O0. Because the pass moves at most one region per call,
vetoing N can expose a later region as the next candidate — re-trace.

**Consumer:** `main/func_8008B93C` 28/115 → 52/115 (`=all`, one candidate):
every block of the bodies-first nested dispatch lines up with retail (case 5
and case 0 bodies written BEFORE their `case` label, so the jump table lands
on the test block). The +2 body words are the ASPSX `$at` slot fill (Hook 54
note) and retail's `move v1,v0` selector copy.

**Validation (Hooks 54–56, 2026-09-22):** env unset → as1 / ugen objects
identical to the pre-hook tools on the consumer TUs; `build_level3_c.py
--no-cache` 1150/1150, 0 mismatched, 0 non-reloc fixups, retail SHA1
`2d8d4748…`; a random 40-file sample of overlay files carrying as1/uopt/ugen
hooks unchanged (33 byte-perfect, 3 semis at their recorded %, 4 asm_only).

## Hook 57: IDO52_FORM_MERGE — site-keyed constant web MERGE; the `held_constant_zero` recipe (2026-09-24)

Applied to the live `build/5.2/uopt.c` on 2026-09-24, but **not yet listed in
the post-`make clean` chain** (`tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_all_hooks.py`) — add it there before
any file ships `IDO52_FORM_MERGE`. Apply after Hook 34 (`apply_uopt_websplit_hooks.py`):

```bash
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_formmerge_hooks.py [build/5.2/uopt.c] --rebuild
```

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_FORM_MERGE` | `const:VAL:STMT[-STMTTO][,...]` | At the walker's const formation query (`func_4623c4` L462438, Hook 30's `stmt=` key) for VAL whose descriptor has **no web yet** and a positive candidacy result, re-point that use's itab node (`node+20`) at the first EARLIER descriptor of the same value that already owns a web. The walker's own join path then adds the use; later uses of the node follow. |
| `IDO52_FORM_TRACE=1` | (Hook 30) | `[FORM-MERGE] stmt=.. val=.. desc=.. -> joins bit N of desc D` or `: no donor web` |

**Why a merge, and why it re-points the NODE, not `desc+2`.** cfe emits one
constant descriptor per (value, dtype): a literal 0 used as an int (Jdt,
`type=6`, listing `{0|0}`) and a 0 stored through a u8 lvalue (Ldt, `type=8`,
`{0|1}`) are two descriptors, hence two webs. Copying the donor's web bit into
`desc+2` (the Hook 34 mirror image) was built first and is **refuted**: the web
forms and is coloured, but the use still emits `move $4,$0`, because emission
asks `f_inreg(ichain, bb)` — the per-bb register→ichain map at `bb+64` — and
the register holds the DONOR ichain. Re-pointing the node makes every consumer
(walker, globalcolor, `f_inreg`) see the donor. Only same-VALUE descriptors can
donate, so the value the register holds is identical by construction.

### The class diagnosis — `held_constant_zero` is a LOCAL VARIABLE, not a constant

Measured on GYOZI/func_8013EE14 and SHOUGATU/func_8013E5DC (`-l` listing,
`IDO52_UOPT_TRACE=5`): retail's `move a0,a1` is a **variable web being assigned
from the zero web** (`x = 0` with x coloured a0, the const web coloured a1),
not a const copy. Evidence chain:
- In our build `SEL` (the u8 union member) is itself a register variable
  (`isvar S 10 3887`, bit 15, colour a0) — so `SEL = 0; f(SEL, 0)` stores
  from the VARIABLE's register. Feeding it any register value makes
  `f_genrop` (op 0x7b to a size-1 register var) emit `CVTL` → the
  `and $4,$x,255` every "u8 x" spelling produced. Retail has no truncation,
  so retail's a0 was never SEL's register: the source passed a separate
  **int local** initialised to 0.
- That local's load is folded by the bb-local LOD substitution (Hook 32
  Family 2). `IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE=lod:Q:deny` at the local's arrival (`off=`
  = its frame offset, `vkind=2` = constant value) keeps it a web (listing:
  `>>>active<<<{1024|0} 15 3` = `-8vreg` in a0).
- The u8 store's `{0|1}` const is its own web → `sb $zero`. `IDO52_FORM_MERGE`
  at the store's stmt puts it in the int zero web → `sb a1`.
- Post-call uses of 0 extend the zero web across the jal (a caller-saved
  colour plus a reload): deny them with `IDO52_FORM_SITE=const:0:S:0`.
- If the colour lands wrong, `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR=BIT:reg` (Hook 37) —
  legal once the web no longer spans the call.

### Measured (resolver-independent `test_overlay_match`, scratch copies; tree files restored byte-for-byte, md5 checked)

| Specimen | Before | After | Recipe |
|---|---|---|---|
| GYOZI/func_8013EE14 | 49/97, `sb zero`/`move a0,zero` wrong | **50/97**, both held-zero words `[22] sb a1` / `[24] move a0,a1` MATCH; 6 diff lines left, count exact | `int x;` + `SEL = 0; x = 0; f(x, 0)`; `IDO52_CONST_INREG_VALUES=0 IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE=lod:1:deny IDO52_FORM_MERGE=const:0:13 IDO52_FORM_SITE=const:0:22:0,const:0:36:0,const:0:51:0` |
| SHOUGATU/func_8013E5DC | 117/118 words (resolver 29/118) | **118/118 count-exact, 70/118 exact**; `[28] move a2,zero`, `[44] sb a2`, `[46] move a0,a2` MATCH; frame 88 exact | `s32 x;` in place of the unused `s32 n;`, K&R `extern s32 func_80051E88();`, `SEL = 0; x = 0; r = func_80051E88(x);`; `IDO52_CONST_INREG_VALUES=0 IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE=lod:1:deny IDO52_FORM_MERGE=const:0:19 IDO52_FORM_SITE=const:0:27:0,const:0:41:0,const:0:59:0 IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR=83:a2` |
| main/touzyou_fan_disp | 1172/1173 | **unchanged** — different sub-class, see below | — |

What is left on BOTH overlay specimens is the same, and not this class:
`andi t4/t5,v0,0x7f` one expression temp low (`[25..27]` / `[47..49]`) and the
`lui a1/a2,0x800f; lbu a0,..(aN)` base register before the second call
(`[40..43]` / `[62..65]`, ours bases on a0). Next levers: Hook 36 temp force
for the andi run; the lbu base is the ugen address-temp choice.
Stmt/q keys shift on any source edit — re-derive with `IDO52_FORM_TRACE=1`
and `IDO52_COPYPROP_TRACE=1`.

The notes that follow are kept as the record of where the previous pass
stopped; their one error is corrected there (the `[CPM copyrep]` skip was
inert because the leaf's image had ALREADY been committed at L414778, and
because as1 folds the surviving add a second time).

`idx = 0; if (pos == year) { ...; idx++; }` → `li v1,1`. Cross-bb, so no
Family 2 lod arrival fires (all 20 swept singly: inert) and Family 1
`IDO52_COPYPROP_SITE=*` is inert. The fold is in `func_414394`
(f_copypropagate's image builder), var/ilod-leaf branch: `func_412d78`

## Hook 58: IDO52_COPYPROP_VAR — variable-keyed veto of the CROSS-BLOCK reaching-value replacement (2026-09-24)

Applied to the live `build/5.2/uopt.c` on 2026-09-24. **Add it to the
post-`make clean` chain** (`apply_all_hooks.py`) right after
`apply_uopt_copyprop_hooks.py` (Hook 32 — it reuses Hook 32's statement
ordinal and trace switch):

```bash
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_copyprop_var_hooks.py [build/5.2/uopt.c] --rebuild
```

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_COPYPROP_VAR` | `OFF[@BLOCK][:OCC[-OCCTO]][,...]` | In `f_copypropagate`'s image builder `func_414394`, var-leaf case: for the `isvar` leaf whose frame offset is OFF (and block BLOCK, if given), at its OCC-th arrival with a non-empty copy-candidate chain (1-based, per procedure; omitted = every arrival), empty the candidate chain. The leaf keeps its own `LOD var` image. |
| `IDO52_COPYPROP_TRACE=1` | (Hook 32's switch) | `[CPV var] off=OFF@BLOCK occ=N stmt=S item=.. bit=.. loc=.. size=..` per keyed arrival, and `[CPV commit] stmt=S item=.. off=OFF@BLOCK src=.. skind=K` for every arrival that actually REPLACED the leaf (skind 2 = constant). |

OFF/BLOCK are exactly what the `-Wo,-l` listing prints on the variable's
`isvar` line: `{ 960|0} 103 isvar M 8 -12` → `-12@8` (item+40 is the
offset, `item+44 >> 16` the block). All locals sit in the procedure's
block, so `@BLOCK` is only needed for statics (several sit at offset 0).

**Mechanism.** `func_414394` L414444 (var/ilod leaf) looks the leaf up in the
copy-candidate hash chain (`0x100135e0[item+8]`) at L414524 and walks it
(L414544..L414d54). A candidate whose copy statement is available in this
block (`f_bvectin0` on the block's copy vector) and survives the
expinalter / has_ilod / is_incr / countvars screens is **committed at
L414778**: `*result = source tree`, then `func_412d78` + `f_copycoderep`
rewrite the item, and the parent folds (`0 + 1` → `1`, `f_binaryfold` —
Hook 47's site). The hook sets the chain head to 0 at L414524, which is the
function's own "no candidate" path (`goto L4164f0`, result left 0). It
never creates a candidate and touches no other leaf: it can only DECLINE
one propagation. This is the cross-block propagation neither Hook 32 family
reaches (Family 1 keys statement images, Family 2 is the bb-local LOD gate
in `f_readnxtinst`).

**Why the previous probe was inert.** Skipping `f_copycoderep` alone does
nothing: the leaf's image was already written at L414778, before the
rewrite. The veto has to be at the chain lookup.

### ⚠️ The add is folded TWICE — pair with Hook 44

With the uopt replacement vetoed, ugen emits `addu $3,$3,1` (`cc -S`), and
**as1 pass I folds it again** to `li $3,1`: it knows `$3 == 0` from the
`move v1,zero` across the fall-through edge into the `if` body. Veto that
single arrival with Hook 44 (`IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP=<n>`; read `n` off
`IDO52_AS1_PASS1_PROP_TRACE=1`, the `#n insn=24630001` line at the site —
**the counter is 0-based and per TU**, whatever Hook 44's table says).
Either half alone is inert: uopt veto alone → as1 folds; as1 veto alone →
ugen already emits `li`. Hook 44's soundness caveat (a partial veto on a
DEFINING instruction skips the substitution but `func_418fb8` still records
the def) is satisfied here by construction — the object equals retail — but
check any other use of it the same way.

### Measured (scratch copy of the tree file; live toolchain after install)

| Specimen | Before | After | Recipe |
|---|---|---|---|
| main/touzyou_fan_disp | 1172/1173, word 0x8006DA08 `li v1,1` | **1173/1173 BYTE-PERFECT**, Body 1173 == target | `@CompileEnv: IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=90:t5 IDO52_COPYPROP_VAR=-12:54 IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP=93` (`-12` = `idx`; `-12@8` and bare `-12` give the same object — `idx` has exactly ONE committed replacement in the whole function, `[CPV commit] stmt=824 ... off=-12@8 src=.. skind=2`) |
| GYOZI/func_8013EE14, SHOUGATU/func_8013E5DC | 50/97 / 70/118 (Hook 57) | unchanged — **no simpler form** | Both functions have ZERO `[CPV commit]` lines: their `x = 0` fold is the bb-local LOD gate (Hook 32 `lod:1:deny`), not this pass. Hook 58 has nothing to veto there. |

Diagnosis evidence (touzyou_fan_disp): `-l` listing — local-opt table
`112 uadd{960|0}{1|1}` / `113 ustr{960|0}{26|0}` (`idx = idx + 1`); after
copy propagation baseline adds `630 ustr{960|0}{1|1}` (`idx = 1`), which the
veto removes; the `-K` `.s` then flips `li $3, 1` → `addu $3, $3, 1`, and
the final object changes in exactly one word (objdump diff) once Hook 44 #93
is added.

## Hook 59: IDO52_NO_BLOCK_SPLIT / IDO52_BLOCK_SPLIT_LIMIT — uopt basic-block CAPACITY split (2026-09-24)

Applied to the live `build/5.2/uopt.c` on 2026-09-24. **Add it to the
post-`make clean` chain** (independent of the other uopt appliers; any
position):

```bash
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_blocksplit_hooks.py [build/5.2/uopt.c] --rebuild
```

⭐ **Try the NATIVE flag first.** The limit this hook overrides is uopt's
own original `-varref N` option (default 20). `@CompileFlags: -Wo,-varref,N`
is a real IDO flag, so it ranks above any hook on the preference ladder. The
env forms are here for sweeping (`LIMIT`) and for a single block (`NO_BLOCK_SPLIT`,
which has no native equivalent).

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_NO_BLOCK_SPLIT` | `BB[,BB...]` | Veto the capacity split for uopt block `BB`, the number the `-Wo,-l` flow-graph listing prints (`MEM_U16(curgraphnode+8)`). The block keeps growing until a natural end (label/branch/call). Later blocks renumber one lower. Applies in every procedure of the TU. |
| `IDO52_BLOCK_SPLIT_LIMIT` | `N` | Replaces the per-block limit for every block. Same as `-Wo,-varref,N`. |
| `IDO52_BLOCK_SPLIT_TRACE=1` | presence | `[BSPLIT] bb=BB cnt=C lim=L SPLIT\|VETO` for every capacity split that fires or is vetoed. **Run this first**: it names the block to key. |

**Decision site (f_getop L43ab50).** uopt reads ucode one basic block at a
time. The per-block reader is `func_459944`, which `f_oneproc` calls. It
loops `f_readnxtinst`/`f_getop` until the end-of-block flag `0x100135d4` is
set, then runs `f_constarith` + `f_codeimage`. Natural block enders are op
bitsets (L43aa2c, L459dc0). The **capacity** ender is:

```
if (0x1001e628 == 0 && 0x1001c444 == 0x1001c448)        /* statement boundary */
    endflag = !(varref_count(0x1001e668) < varref_limit(0x1001e674));
```

- `f_readnxtinst` bumps the counter for each variable reference it reads
  (L43e8c4 / L43eaa0). A `p->c = code` store counts 2, and so does a
  `p->uv = hdr->uv0.word` copy.
- The counter is reset to 0 at every block start (L459d08). The limit is
  copied from `0x1001e670` at the same place.
- `0x1001e670` is set by `f_processargs` from `-varref` (L43726c). Its
  default is `0x14` (L437280).

The hook ANDs a veto into `endflag` (or recomputes it against the
forced limit). It acts only inside a procedure's block reader (`lim != 0`,
`curgraphnode != 0`); before the first block the flag is computed against
lim 0 and the hook leaves it alone. It can only make a block LONGER: it
never adds a split.

### Measured: `func_800AF538` 176/177 → **177/177 BYTE-PERFECT**

Trace: `[BSPLIT] bb=2 cnt=20` and `bb=5 cnt=20`. bb5 is the call2→call3 region
(tag/code/clut/tpage + four `p->c` stores; the four `p->uv` stores and call3
spill into bb6). With the region as one block:

- `cc -S` loses exactly one line: the surplus `sw $3,<home>($sp)` before
  call3 (word 155).
- as1 then deletes the reload after call2, because `v1` is now dead until the
  post-call3 reload (`[AS1-KDREL] #0 word=8fa300ac`). Retail keeps it
  (0x800AF720). With the veto alone the body is 176 and the rest of the
  function shifts by one word.
- Hook 52 at that single candidate restores it:

```
@CompileFlags: -Wc,-pic1 -Wo,-do_opt_saved_regs -Wo,-varref,29
@CompileEnv:   IDO52_AS1_KEEP_DEAD_RELOAD=0 <existing tokens>
```

Hook 59 alone does the same (`IDO52_NO_BLOCK_SPLIT=5 IDO52_AS1_KEEP_DEAD_RELOAD=0`,
no `-varref`). Either spelling gives Body 177 == target, Exact 177/177,
Fixup 0, `audit_reloc_positions` OK. `-varref` values 29..100 all give the
same `.s`; 21..28 only move the split later inside the same region, and the
store stays.

### Blast radius (limit raised, 2026-09-24)

- 20 random registered main functions (≥200 B) at `IDO52_BLOCK_SPLIT_LIMIT=29`
  and `=1000`: **19 unchanged**. `bg_set` breaks (157/157 → body 155,
  0/156 and 5/156). A TU-global `-varref` is therefore NOT a region default:
  set it per file.
- All `src/main/semi-matched/func_800A*`/`func_800B*` semis at `LIMIT=1000`:
  only `func_800AF538` moves.

### Inertness (2026-09-24)

- `build_level3_c.py --no-cache`: `Mismatched: 0`, SHA1
  `2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`.
- Regression set all BYTE-PERFECT: DATE/func_8013B1AC, TT/func_8013D3C0,
  TT/func_8013D150, TAIIKU/func_80145534, TT/func_8013E484,
  GYOZI/func_8013EE14, SHOUGATU/func_8013E5DC, RPG_BAT/func_80154DD8,
  touzyou_fan_disp, menu_bar_color, func_8004F238.
- `func_800AF538` `cc -S` is byte-identical to the pre-hook compiler both
  unset and with `IDO52_BLOCK_SPLIT_TRACE=1`.
- With none of the three variables set, the shim returns the original flag
  after one cached getenv.

## Hook 60: IDO52_FORCE_WEB_SPILL / NOSPILL = ROOT.PIECE — Hook 39 for range-split pieces (2026-09-25)

Applied to the live `build/5.2/uopt.c` on 2026-09-25. **Add it to the
post-`make clean` chain AFTER `apply_uopt_web_hooks.py`** (it patches Hook
39's env parse and anchors on Hook 39's pre-globalcolor call, and on Hook
37's NOSPLIT site):

```bash
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_splitpiece_hooks.py [build/5.2/uopt.c] --rebuild
```

**The gap it closes.** Hook 39 writes lr+35 (the "worth colouring" byte)
BEFORE `f_globalcolor`. Inside it, the phase-1 cost test (L46ef64, `a3=1`)
and the neighbour re-split loop (L46f4fc, `a3=0`) call `f_split(lr, &rest,
colour, flag)`: the range being coloured is cut to a PIECE that KEEPS its
bitpos, everything else moves into a REMAINDER with a brand-new bitpos
(`f_newbit` grows numwebs `0x1001cad8`), and `f_split` zeroes lr+35 on
both halves. So the remainder did not exist when Hook 39 ran — `[WEB-SPILL]
bitpos 171 out of range (numwebs 167) -- SKIPPED`. (Hooks 37 COLOR/NOSPLIT,
38b ADJSAVE and 43 already resolve a bitpos at the moment they fire, so a
bare post-split bitpos works for them; only Hook 39 was blind.)

**Decision site.** `f_split` is renamed `f_split_h60orig` and wrapped. After
each split the wrapper keys the new range `ROOT.PIECE` — ROOT = the
pre-globalcolor bitpos the chain started from, PIECE = 1, 2, … in creation
order under that root (a remainder of a remainder is ROOT.2) — and applies
the keyed forces to it, and re-applies them to the parent when the parent
is itself a keyed piece (the split just zeroed its lr+35). Keys are stable
under unrelated env changes in a way bare post-split bitposes are not
(adding one force renumbered 200→201 on mascot_move).

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_SPILL` | `R.P` (mixable with bare bitposes) | lr+35 = 2 on the piece: phase 1 takes f_globalcolor's own L46ea34 uncolourable epilogue, phase 2 skips anything with lr+35 != 1. |
| `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL` | `R.P` | `f_compute_save` (the allocator's own adjsave/occ), then lr+35 = 1 if it came out 2. |
| | `R.P:A` | as above, and when the piece is cut by its OWN next split, set its adjsave (lr+48) to A so phase 2's `cost < adjsave*occ` accepts the cut piece. Not at creation: there the piece is still the whole remainder and a forced adjsave colours all of it (measured, 239 words). |
| | `R.P/A` | adjsave A at creation too. |
| `IDO52_WEB_PIECE_TRACE=1` | presence | `[WEB-PIECE] split parent=B (key R.p) -> bit=N key=R.P (own cost test \| neighbour re-split)` for every split, plus one line per applied force. **Run this first**: it names the key. |

Bare tokens keep Hook 39's exact old meaning. A dotted token used to be read
by `atoi` as the bare ROOT; it is now excluded from Hook 39's list (no
shipped `@CompileEnv` contained one). PIECE 0 is rejected (use the bare
bitpos).

⚠️ A piece whose own cost-test split FAILS (no start block; f_split
L46d3b0) is marked spilled (lr+32 = 0xff) together with its remainder, and
no later lr+35 write can recover it. `NOSPILL` on such a piece is inert —
the trace shows it re-applied but the WEB-HOME dump still says reg=255.

### Also fixed: Hook 37 `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR_NOSPLIT` abort

`IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR_NOSPLIT=34` on TACO/func_8015ACDC (with Hook 28
excluding every s-register from web 34) aborted uopt: `Internal:
func_8015ACDC: code: 5042`, signal 6. Cause: at L46ef64 `sp+220` is the
best colour the scan found, initialised to -1 at L46eb88. With NO legal
colour the natural cost test always splits (f20 is still the sentinel).
Vetoing that split sent the web to the pick at L46f158 with colour -1, which
was committed. The veto now requires `sp+220 >= 0`. Otherwise it prints
`[GC-COLOR] bitpos=N NOSPLIT DECLINED: no legal colour` and the natural
split runs. A web with a legal colour behaves as before. To keep such a web
whole, spill it whole with `FORCE_WEB_SPILL=N`.

### Measured

| Specimen | Before | After | Recipe |
|---|---|---|---|
| TACO/func_8015ACDC | 79/315 resolver-exact, raw PASS (two cancelling errors) | hook alone: 69/315, raw **+1**. Kind is now reloaded from 112 as in retail and base stays in s8 (`sw s8,108` + reloads gone). The +1 is `base += 2` sitting after `func_80143A14(slot)`, so its jal delay slot stays a `nop`. With that ONE source swap (scratch copy, `base += 2;` before the call): **227/315, raw PASS delta 0**. The swap alone, without the hook, gives 61/315. | `IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB=34:s0+…+s8 IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR=41:s8 IDO52_FORCE_WEB_SPILL=34.1` (34.1 = bit 171, kind's post-call half, which was coloured s8) |
| main/mascot_move | 125/238, Body 238, Fixup 0 | **128/238**, Body 238. Words 14/15 (`lui/lh a3` post-call reload of D_800BC108) now exact. | add `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL=0.1:1` and `200:a3` to its `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR` (0.1 = bit 200, the remainder of web 0; its own split cuts it to {bb6}, which then takes a3) |

mascot_move, where it stops: retail also keeps the THIRD D_800BC108 load
(0x80067af4) in a3, which is the next piece, 0.2 = {bb15}. Its own
cost-test split fails (the ⚠️ above), so it is spilled before any re-apply
can reach it. `0.2/A` (forced at creation) colours the whole
remainder-of-remainder instead (38/238, body 239). The next
primitive would be keyed `SPLIT_REJECT`/adjsave ordering on the 0.2 cut. The
first remaining mismatch at 128 is word 16: `lui t0` vs retail `lui t6`
(the D_800EF6E8 temp, Hook 36's domain). The file's own greedy Hook 36 fit
must be redone on top.

TACO/func_8015ACDC's first remaining mismatch at 227 is word 74: `addu
a1,t2,s5` vs retail `addu a1,s5,t2` (operand order). Then come register-field
diffs in the per-record stamp stores and a store/decrement order pair at
words 162/164.

## Hook 60b: IDO52_WEB_PIECE_NOREAPPLY / IDO52_WEB_PIECE_RESCUE — post-cut control for keyed pieces (2026-09-25)

Same applier as Hook 60 (`apply_uopt_splitpiece_hooks.py`). Run on a live
Hook-60 `uopt.c` it swaps the helper block in place ("Hook 60 upgraded to
60b"); run on a pre-Hook-60 `uopt.c` it applies both at once. The two
routes give byte-identical `uopt.c`, and a second run is a no-op. Chain
position is unchanged: after `apply_uopt_web_hooks.py`.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_WEB_PIECE_NOREAPPLY` | `R.P[,R.P]` | After key R.P is cut by its own next split, do NOT re-apply its `FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL` to it as the parent. The piece's post-cut worth and adjsave stay natural, so f_globalcolor recomputes them (`f_compute_save`) at the next pick. The `/A` creation-time adjsave still applies. |
| `IDO52_WEB_PIECE_RESCUE` | `R.P[,R.P]` | When key R.P's OWN cost-test cut leaves an EMPTY remainder, re-queue the piece instead of spilling it. Fires at most once per key per procedure. |
| `IDO52_WEB_PIECE_TRACE=1` | presence | Now also prints, at every phase-1 cost test (L46ef64), `cost test bit=N: best colour=C cost=X vs adjsave*occ=... -> SPLIT/colour forbid=...` plus `neighbours of bit=N (colour): b(c) ...` (the interference list, lr+56, with each neighbour's lr+32). After every split it prints `after cut: piece ... lr+32 forbid ...; remainder ... EMPTY/has blocks`. **This is the tool for asking "why is colour X illegal here?"** |

**Decision sites (evidence).**
- *Phase-1 cost test, L46ef64.* The colour scan leaves `f20` = the cheapest
  legal colour's cost and `sp+220` = that colour, or -1 with cost 1e20 when
  no colour is legal. The web is cut iff `adjsave * occ <= f20`. After the
  cut, both paths go to L46f56c. The piece stays in the queue (0x100223a8)
  with lr+35 zeroed and is re-picked on the next iteration, where
  `f_compute_save` decides its worth again. So without Hook 60's parent
  re-apply, a cut piece gets its natural worth.
- *f_split's failed-split path (L46d344 -> L46d3b0).* The cut piece may
  absorb every block, so the remainder has no livbb (lr+8 == 0) and an
  empty bvect. In that case f_split marks BOTH halves spilled (lr+32 =
  0xff), resets both queue bits and decrements every neighbour's lr+36
  over the remainder's list, which is the parent's old list. It also
  skips the L46d618 partition loop, so the piece's own interference list
  (lr+56) is left EMPTY. Its forbidden set (lr+40/44) has already been
  recomputed precisely over its blocks.
- *RESCUE undoes this for the piece only.* It sets lr+32 = 0 and
  re-sets the queue bit. It re-increments the neighbour counts and moves
  the remainder's list back to the piece. That list is a superset of the
  piece's real interferences, so the colour the piece takes is forbidden
  to every web that could overlap it. That makes it sound and
  conservative. lr+36 is set to the list's live-entry count, because
  f_split's consistency checks 0x13b1/0x13b2 (`Internal: code: 5041/5042`)
  compare that count against the list.

**Measured.**

| Specimen | Before | After | Recipe |
|---|---|---|---|
| TACO/func_8015ACDC | 291/315 resolver-exact (file env: `FORCE_WEB_SPILL=34.1`) | **293/315**, len t=c=1260 | `IDO52_EXCLUDE_REGS_WEB=34:s0+…+s8 IDO52_FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL=34.1/1 IDO52_WEB_PIECE_NOREAPPLY=34.1 IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=23:t0,25:t2,27:t4,30:t9,32:t8,33:t0,35:t1,40:t9,42:t0,43:t8,51:t8,52:t7,63:t0`. The Hook 36 set is a fresh resolver-scored greedy fit (9 rounds). Trace: bit 171's cost test SPLITs it and the post-cut re-pick finds natural worth 2, so it is `SPILLED` as retail. 34.2 (bit 179) is coloured 2 = `$v1`. Word 127 `lw v1,112(sp)` is exact and words 0..133 are all exact. Without NOREAPPLY the same env gives 144/315. First remaining mismatch: word 134, `sll t9,s2,2` vs retail `sll t5,s2,2` (a t-register rotation over words 134..139 that Hook 36 cannot reach). |
| main/mascot_move | 161/238, Body 238 | **178/238**, Body 238 | see below |

**mascot_move: what the rescue found.** The third D_800BC108 load is key
0.2 (bit 208, {bb15}) with `NOSPILL=0.2`. The Hook 60 header's reading was
that its own cut "comes back marked spilled". That is the failed-split path
above: 0.2 IS {bb15}, so the cut empties the remainder. With
`IDO52_WEB_PIECE_RESCUE=0.2` plus `NOSPILL=0.2:5`, bit 208 is re-picked and
coloured (t5). The new trace named every web that forbids a3 in bb15:
`91(a1) 75(a0) 73 71(v1) 69 67(a3) 61 57 16(a2) 14`, with forbid 7ff00000,
meaning colours 1..11 are all taken. **a3 was illegal only because of web 67.**
Once 67 is off a3, `208:a3` is LEGAL in 100 of 336 enumerated
75/16/91 permutations. The Hook 60 header's "moving 67 does not make 208
legal" was measured with 208 never reaching the pick.

Following that chain, 67 cannot take a0 while 75 or 91 hold it. The
winning permutation is `75:t5,16:t3,91:a1`. With it, 67 and 14 fall
NATURALLY into retail's a0 and a2 (`67:a0` and `14:a2` measured inert), and
web 0 passes its cost test WHOLE (colour a3, the existing `0:a3` force). So
it is never split and the Hook 60/60b keys become inert and are dropped. The
third load is then `lui a3 / lh a3,-16120(a3)` at words 81/85, exact.
Final env:
`IDO52_UGEN_FORCE_TARGET_SAFE=10 IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_INPLACE=1
IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR=61:t2,0:a3,29:t4,21:t3,75:t5,16:t3,91:a1
IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=24:a2,5:t3,11:t4,53:t5,29:t8,58:t4,57:t3,25:t5,39:t5,31:t6,18:t3,6:t5,46:t5,20:t2,13:t9,52:t6,50:t0`.
The Hook 36 set is the file's old set extended by `sweep_temp_reg_greedy.py
--seed <old set>` (6 rounds). Seeding from `24:a2` alone converges lower
(151). Colour ablation (each removed): 61:t2 -> 61 (body 240), 0:a3 -> 159,
29:t4 -> 34 (239), 21:t3 -> 177, 75:t5 -> 111, 16:t3 -> 166, 91:a1 -> 170.
First remaining mismatch: word 34, `andi a0,t3,0x7f` vs retail
`andi a0,v0,0x7f`. So RESCUE is the diagnostic that opened this recipe, not
part of it.

## Hook 60c: `R.0` — the piece that KEEPS the original bitpos, and `R.P/A:B` (2026-09-25)

Same applier (`apply_uopt_splitpiece_hooks.py`); run on a live 60b `uopt.c`
it swaps the helper block in place and patches Hook 43's `ido52_gc_rsplit`
("Hook 60b upgraded to 60c"); a second run is a no-op. Chain position
unchanged (after `apply_uopt_web_hooks.py`).

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL` | `R.0`, `R.0:A`, `R.0/A` | Key `R.0` = the piece that keeps bitpos R after R is cut. Applied **only** at the wrapper's parent re-apply after each of R's own cuts (before any cut, R is Hook 39's bare bitpos and `R.0` does nothing). `:A` and `/A` both set its adjsave at that point. |
| `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_SPILL`, `IDO52_WEB_PIECE_RESCUE`, `IDO52_WEB_PIECE_NOREAPPLY` | `R.0` | accepted with the same meaning as for `R.P` |
| `IDO52_FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL` | `R.P/A:B` | adjsave A at creation, B at every re-apply after the piece's own cut. `/-1:5` makes the new range fail its first cost test (`adjsave*occ = -occ <= cost`), so it is cut. Then the cut piece gets adjsave 5 and is coloured. The new remainder takes its natural path. |
| Hook 43 interaction | implicit | When an `R.0` force is configured and R has already been cut in this procedure, `FORCE_WEB_RANGE_SPLIT=R` no longer re-forces the re-picked R.0 piece. The trace prints `[WEB-PIECE] bit=R key=R.0: already cut -- Hook 43 re-force DECLINED`. The piece goes to the natural cost test. |

**Decision site.** The wrapper's parent re-apply
(`ido52_h60_after_split` -> `ido52_h60_apply(olr, ob, oroot, opiece,
"parent")`) already ran for a root parent with `opiece = 0`, but
`ido52_h60_apply` returned on `piece < 1`. It now accepts piece 0 on the
`"parent"` call only. Bare `NOSPILL=R` is unchanged: it is still Hook 39's
pre-globalcolor write, and the cut still zeroes it.

The second wall was measured on VALEN. With `NOSPILL=R.0` the piece was made
colourable, but `FORCE_WEB_RANGE_SPLIT` is keyed by bitpos, so the re-picked
piece was force-split AGAIN. That cut always emptied the remainder
(`after cut: ... EMPTY (split failed: both spilled)`), because the piece
held every block left. So Hook 43's re-force is declined for a root with an
`R.0` key. Also, `R.0`'s natural adjsave after the cut is negative (-14.5
on VALEN). It then fails the natural cost test and is cut again, so give
it `:A` (5 works).

**Measured: VALEN/func_8013283C, natural -cckr rewrite** (the header's
recorded rewrite, webs 37/40/43 = best2/best1/best0). Resolver, len
t=c=1240:

| Env (on the rewrite, nothing else) | Resolver-exact |
|---|---|
| none | 21/310 |
| `RANGE_SPLIT=37,40,43 NOSPILL=37.1,40.1,43.1` (60/60b) | 24/310, body 308 |
| `RANGE_SPLIT=37,40,43 NOSPILL=37.1/-1:5,40.1/-1:5,43.1/-1:5` (no `.0`) | 24/310, body 304 |
| **`RANGE_SPLIT=37,40,43 NOSPILL=37.0:5,40.0:5,43.0:5,37.1/-1:5,40.1/-1:5,43.1/-1:5`** | **70/310**, count-exact |

Trace of the winning env: all three `.0` init pieces are coloured (37 ->
colour 1, 40 -> 2, 43 -> 4). Each `.1` loop remainder is cut once at its own
cost test (40.1 and 37.1 -> {bb8}, 43.1 -> {bb7..12}) and the cut piece is
coloured (2/3/4). The `.2` remainders spill as retail's memory homes. The
register halves come back (`move a0,t9` + `sh t9` pairs in each arm).

**Where it stops.** The shipped file is still 92/310 on its own tuned env
(Hooks 21/36/18), and this route has not yet beaten it on that body. The
same keys on the shipped source (webs 37/43/46) give 20/310 with its hooks
and 26/310 without. The remaining structural differences on the rewrite:
1. **The `.0` pieces are {bb3 4 5 13}, not retail's init-only {3 4 5}.** They
   reach into the loop header and latch. We emit the boundary stores
   `sh v0,86 / sh v1,88 / sh a1,90` in the loop header and reload all three
   at the latch (`lh v0,86; lh v1,88; lh a1,90`). Retail stores once, before
   the loop (`sh t4,80 / sh zero,82 / sh zero,84`). The first cut is Hook
   43's, and f_split grows the piece from its start block.
2. **Home slots are 86/88/90 (best2/best1/best0), where retail has
   80/82/84 (best0/best1/best2).**
3. **`43.1`'s cut piece is {bb7..12}.** Retail's best0 pieces are per-block
   (the compare block, then arm 1).

Pre-existing head differences from the rewrite (words 20..45: `li` order and
the 255 chain) come before all of these. The next primitive would be
control of the FIRST cut's block set (a keyed start block or block
exclusion for Hook 43), so that `R.0` = the init blocks only.

## Hook 1b: IDO52_NO_CSE_SITE — per-site veto of ONE f_isearchloop CSE reuse (2026-09-25)

Applied to the live `build/5.2/uopt.c` on 2026-09-25. **Add it to the
post-`make clean` chain** after `apply_uopt_hooks.py` and
`apply_uopt_readphase_hooks.py` (it extends Hook 1's site and uses its
`_opc_names` table; it parses its own env lazily, so it does not depend on
Hook 46's early init):

```bash
python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_nocse_site_hooks.py [build/5.2/uopt.c] --rebuild
```

(`--rebuild` links `out/uopt.new` and installs it with an atomic rename.)

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_NO_CSE_SITE` | `OPC:OCC[-OCCTO][,...]` | At `f_isearchloop`'s result (L447124), when the search FOUND an existing entry, count a hit for the expression's opcode (`MEM_U8(expr+32)`, types 4/5/6), per procedure, 1-based. A keyed hit is turned into a miss (`t0 = s0 = 0`); the function's own miss path (`f_appendichain`) makes a fresh entry. `OPC` = a Hook 1 opcode name (`mpy`, `ixa`, `add`, ...), a number, or `*` (count all hits). |
| `IDO52_NO_CSE_SITE_TRACE=1` | presence | `[CSE-SITE] hit#N opc=O(name) occ=K any=A type=T bb=B entry=0x.. [VETO]` for every hit. **Run it first** — `occ` is the key; `bb` is `MEM_U16(curgraphnode+8)` at query time (-1 = outside the block reader). |

This is Hook 1 (`IDO52_NO_CSE_OPCODES`) restricted to one occurrence: Hook 1
empties the bucket at the TOP of the search for every expression of an
opcode; Hook 1b acts on one HIT. Same decision point as
`IDO52_CSE_MAX_REUSE`, which it precedes.

⚠️ **Scope: `f_isearchloop` is the CROSS-BLOCK table** (codemotion /
copyprop). Two uses of one address inside ONE basic block are merged by the
read-phase local CSE (the `readnxtinst` ichain hash, `numlcse` in the `-l`
listing) and never produce a hit here — `IDO52_NO_CSE*` and this hook are
all inert on them. To make such a pair reachable the uses must sit in
different blocks (a `goto L; L:` between the statements).

### Measured: `BUNKAKEN/func_80145548` 186/189 → **189/189 BYTE-PERFECT**

Retail's tail computes `&D_800EE760[D_8012B384]` twice (`li t1,56` held,
2× `multu/mflo/addu v1`). Trace with the tail split by `goto L; L:` (plain
index, no `| 0`):

```
[CSE-SITE] hit#6 opc=91(mpy) occ=1 ... bb=21 entry=0x1005b190   <- n < 11 arm (must stay CSE'd, a0 across the switch)
[CSE-SITE] hit#7 opc=65(ixa) occ=1 ... bb=21
[CSE-SITE] hit#8 opc=91(mpy) occ=2 ... bb=24 entry=0x100577c0   <- tail, 2nd statement
[CSE-SITE] hit#9 opc=65(ixa) occ=2 ... bb=24
```

| Tail spelling | Env | Result |
|---|---|---|
| plain, one block | `NO_CSE_OPCODES=mpy` | 188 words, tail still ONE multu (local CSE); arm split +3, frame 216 |
| `goto L; L:` split | none | 184 (address CSE'd across the blocks, lowered to shifts) |
| `goto L; L:` split | `NO_CSE_OPCODES=mpy` | tail exact, arm split (frame 216) |
| `goto L; L:` split | `IDO52_NO_CSE_SITE=mpy:2` | **189/189, len t=756 c=756, 0 unresolved, audit_raw_count PASS delta 0**, zero PostProc |

`ixa:2` alone does not do it (the mpy is still reused, one multu); `mpy:2`
alone is enough (`mpy:2,ixa:2` gives the same object). The earlier
per-file claim that the plain one-block tail reproduces retail under
`NO_CSE_OPCODES=mpy` does not hold on the current toolchain.

  BUNKAKEN + DATE/func_8013B*) bit-identical objects.

  after one static-int check; the per-procedure reset returns early.

---

## Hook 61: IDO52_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT / IDO52_AS1_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT / IDO52_AS1_NO_JUMP_CHAIN — keep a jump to the next label; veto as1 jump chaining (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_jumpnext_hooks.py [--ugen PATH] [--as1 PATH] [--rebuild]`
(patches BOTH `build/5.2/ugen.c` and `build/5.2/as1.c`).
⚠️ **Add it to the post-`make clean` chain** (independent of every other
applier; its helpers anchor on `func_4351b8`'s / `func_41b5e8`'s own
definitions). Idempotent (markers `ugen_jnext_veto`, `as1_jnext_veto`).
Default OFF.

**Two deletions, one per pass — a retail `b next ; nop` needs BOTH vetoes.**
This is why every earlier probe (`IDO52_NO_JUMP_MERGE=all`,
`UGEN_NO_REGION_MOVE=all`, `AS1_NO_BRANCH_FOLD`, `NO_JUMP_INVERT`,
`NO_BOPT`/`FORCE_BOPT`, `NO_BRANCH_OPT=1`, a `goto` to a shared tail label)
was inert: each pass independently deletes the jump, so fixing either one
alone changes nothing in the object.

1. **ugen, `f_labelopt` → `func_4351b8` (`L435424`).** Not uopt: the jump is
   present in uopt's output. `f_labelopt` (-O1 and up) calls `func_4351b8`
   twice per procedure (before and after Hook 56's `func_435fb0`). For each
   jump statement (tree opcode `0x88` UJP; `0x7f` / `0x26` conditional) it
   follows jump chains, scans forward from the jump over label-like nodes
   (bitset `0x10005264`; a label with a nonzero `+34` stops the scan) and,
   when it reaches the jump's own target label (`s0 == s1`),
   `f_delete_statement`s the jump. Veto → `L4355bc`, the path taken when the
   next label is not the target (stamp the label, advance).
   Evidence: with `IDO52_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT=lab:13`, `cc -S` of func_8009F99C
   gains exactly one line, `b $45` after case 1-3's `jal $25`.
2. **as1, `func_41b5e8` (`L41b92c`), the -O2 CFG pass before pass I/II.**
   For a block ending in a branch whose target block (`func_41b444`) is its
   own fall-through successor (`s2+4`), it zeroes the slot count (`s2+62`),
   makes the branch a no-op via `f_change_bb(.., 5, ..)` and clears its
   target (`s5+20`). Located by skip-bisecting the reorg driver's calls in a
   private as1 (only skipping `func_41b5e8` kept the `b`); as1 `-O1`/`-O0`
   keep it natively. Veto → `L41bbf8` (free the scratch list, add the CFG
   edge to the target; an unconditional branch gets no fall-through edge) —
   the state of any ordinary unconditional branch.
3. **Hook 61b, the other arm of the same as1 test.** When the target is NOT
   the fall-through, as1 goes on to `L41b99c`: follow the target's jump
   chain (`func_41aa60`, up to 6 hops) and retarget, copying the chain
   block's leading word into the slot when that block is `insn ; b far`
   (`b join` → `b end ; move v0,a0`). Veto → the same `L41bbf8` exit, so the
   branch keeps its original target and slot.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` \| `lab:L,...` (a bare number is an INDEX) | ugen: keep deletion candidate N (0-based, TU arrival order over BOTH `func_4351b8` calls) or every candidate whose target is ugen's INTERNAL label L (tree `+36`, as traced; not the `$N` of `cc -S`). **Prefer `lab:`** — a kept jump is re-offered by the second call, so indices shift. |
| `IDO52_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT_TRACE` | presence | `[UGEN-JNEXT] #N op=0x88 label=L [-> KEPT]` |
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` | as1: keep branch-to-fall-through candidate N (0-based, TU arrival order) |
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-JNEXT] #N word=0x10000000 [-> KEPT]` |
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_JUMP_CHAIN` | same | as1: leave candidate N off the jump-chain retarget. EVERY branch-ending block that reaches the test is a candidate (chain or not; vetoing a non-chain branch is a no-op) — find the index by tracing the branch word and sweeping. |
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_JUMP_CHAIN_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-JCHAIN] #N word=... [-> VETOED]` |

All three are subtractive and sound by construction: each veto takes the
pass's own "nothing to do" exit; no instruction is created, no opcode or
operand rewritten (a `b` to the next word is a no-op jump; an un-chained
branch still reaches the same code).

**Consumer: `main/func_8009F99C` 118/132 → BYTE-PERFECT 132/132**, Body 132,
Fixup 0 (scratch copy; the file is not edited by this investigation):
```
@CompileEnv: IDO52_NO_JUMP_MERGE=1 IDO52_UGEN_NO_REGION_MOVE=all
  IDO52_AS1_NO_JUMP_INVERT=0 IDO52_AS1_TIE_REJECT=39,46,82,91
  IDO52_UGEN_DEST_TEMP=4,15,31,37 IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_INPLACE=3,4,5
  IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=3:v0,4:v0,5:v0 IDO52_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT=lab:13
  IDO52_AS1_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT=0 IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL=0,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
  IDO52_AS1_NO_FALLTHRU_FILL=0,1 IDO52_AS1_FTF_BACKWARD=occ:0
```
(one line in the file). Keeping the jump makes the old env Body 134 — the
old count-exactness was COMPENSATION (the missing `b ; nop` against the
ladder's surplus `nop` + BOPT `lw t4` copy). Re-fitting the as1 fills once
the `b` exists: allow LA_PULL #1 (`li at,1` into the ladder `beqz`), allow
the fall-through fill (veto only #0/#1, the arms), `FTF_BACKWARD=occ:0`
(the backward `beq v1,1` takes `li at,3`, Hook 54), drop `NO_BOPT=2` (the
last `beq` copy-and-bumps `lw t9,32(sp)` as retail does), and
`DEST_TEMP` occ 37 (case 1-3's `lw t0,52(t9) ; jalr t0` — previously
recorded inert; it is live on this layout). Every one of the 12 tokens
ablated: removing any costs 8-81 words or the count.

**Checked on `main/func_800A26AC` (native-as1 alternative, 34/116, Body
115):** the ternary wall "as1 chains `b $42` into `b $44`" IS this site —
`IDO52_AS1_NO_JUMP_CHAIN=16` (the 5th `0x10000000` candidate) reproduces
retail's `beqz a0 ; li v1,1 ; b join ; move v1,a0 ; li v1,1 ; b end ;
move v0,v1` shape exactly, but the positional score is unchanged (34/116,
Body 115) because the whole tail sits one word early. That word is the
other named residual, the pre-header's `beqz ; nop ; b body ; lui s0` s0
def on the jump edge: ugen's input already has `bne $10,0,$35 ; li $16,K`
with the def as the `$35` range head, i.e. uopt def placement (Hook 24
territory), NOT a deleted jump — its only JNEXT candidate (`lab:43`, kept
with `AS1_KEEP_JUMP_NEXT`) adds a `b` retail does not have (Body 116, no
gain). So: the jump chain is Hook 61b's business; the jump-edge def is not.

**Inertness (2026-09-25):** env unset each helper returns before counting.
- `build_level3_c.py --no-cache`: 1197 matched, `Mismatched: 0`, 0 non-reloc
  fixups, SHA1 `2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`.
- Regression set all BYTE-PERFECT: DATE/func_8013B1AC, TT/func_8013D3C0,
  TT/func_8013D150, TAIIKU/func_80145534, TT/func_8013E484,
  GYOZI/func_8013EE14, RPG_BAT/func_80154DD8, TACO/func_8015ACDC,
