## Hook 62: IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO — ASPSX-order div/divu/rem/remu macro expansion (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_divmacro_hooks.py [--as1 PATH] [--rebuild]`
(patches `build/5.2/as1.c`; `--rebuild` compiles to `out/as1.new` and renames
it over `out/as1`). ⚠️ **Add it to the post-`make clean` chain** (independent
of every other applier; anchors are `f_parsestmt`'s / `f_gendiv`'s own recomp
labels). Idempotent (marker `as1_divm_pre_record`). Default OFF.

**The wall it closes.** Retail's library-region IDO code was assembled by
ASPSX (`@Compiler: ido52-gas`, CLAUDE.md "Compilers"). Native as1 + the cross-block vetoes
already reproduces its *schedule* (func_800A24FC, batch BZ), but for a
register-divisor `div rd,rs,rt` as1's `f_gendiv` (L4646d8) emits the whole
macro at once — `div $zero,rs,rt ; bnez rt ; nop ; break 7 ; [li at,-1 ; bne
rt,at ; lui at,0x8000 ; bne rs,at ; nop ; break 6] ; mflo rd` — while ASPSX
emits the `div` and **defers** both the check and the `mflo`/`mfhi`. No other
hook reaches a macro expansion.

**The model** is maspsx's `defer_div_macros` (tools/maspsx/maspsx/__init__.py):
- the CHECK is flushed before the first later instruction that writes rs/rt or
  uses `$at` (a symbolic store's `lui at` counts), and at any block end;
- the MFLO/MFHI before the first later instruction that reads or writes rd or
  touches HI/LO, and at any block end — check first when rd is rs or rt;
- at a block end the check goes first, except an immediate-compare branch
  macro that reads rd (`beq rd,0,L`: mflo first — retail 0x80096B24).

**Sites (build/5.2/as1.c).**
1. `f_gendiv` `L464ac4` (right after `f_emitalu3` emitted `div $zero,rs,rt`):
   register divisor (`sp+147 == 0`), rd != 0, hook on for this occurrence →
   record the pending check (signedness from the `0x1000426c` bitset,
   L464b0c) and mflo/mfhi (selector from L464d48) and `goto L464da0`.
2. `f_parsestmt` `L4563b0` (before every binasm record): classify the record
   (asmtype `+5 & 0x3f`; opcode `(u16(+6) >> 1) & 0x1ff`; format table
   `0x1002ffd0[op]`; reg1/reg2/form/reg3 from the word at `+8`; immediate
   `+12`) and flush what ASPSX would flush — by calling **the same emitters
   f_gendiv calls, with the same arguments, in the same order**
   (`f_emitbene 0x4c` / `f_emitnop 1` / `f_emitspec 0xb,7|6` /
   `f_loadimmed -1|0x80000000` / `f_mark_unmovable` / `f_emitreg2 0xe|0xc`,
   plus the `+34` byte clears).
3. `f_parsestmt` exit `L457fc4`: flush anything still pending (as1 hands a
   chunk back to `f_main` after every block end; nothing crosses it).

Binasm facts established here (dumped records vs `cc -S`): asmtype 0 label,
21 `.text`, 22 `.word`, 23 instruction, 24 `.end`, 26 `.rdata`, 27 `.ent`,
28 `.loc`, 32 `.set` (option word `+8`: 11 volatile, 12 novolatile), 38
`.mask`, 43 `.frame`, 47 `.option`, 53 `.livereg`. Opcodes: addu 2, and 3,
b 4, bge 14, bgeu 15, bne 26, div 32, divu 33, j 34, jal 35, la 36, lb 37,
lbu 38, lh 39, lhu 40, li 41, lw 42, move 49, mul 56, or 64, rem 65, remu 66,
sb 70, seq 71, sh 76, sll 79, slt 80, sltu 81, sra 83, srl 84, subu 86, sw 87,
xor 89, not 90. Register 72 = none. afrrr forms: 3 `rd,rs,rt`, 4 `rd,rs,imm`,
2 `rd,imm`; afra forms: 0 `off(base)`, 1 symbolic; afrrl form 12 = compare
with an immediate.

**Soundness.** Nothing is created or dropped — the words are as1's own
expansion, emitted later in the stream. The classifier only lets a record
pass without a flush if it is on a whitelist whose register effects are
fully known (addu and or xor slt sltu sll sra srl subu — immediates only in
their one-word range —, li, move, not, la lb lbu lh lhu lw sb sh sw); every
other record (unknown op, any branch/jump, label, directive other than
`.loc`/`.livereg`/`.set volatile`) flushes everything first, and any other
`.set` stops deferral for the rest of the procedure. **Safety net:** while
anything is pending, every emitted machine word is decoded (MIPS I) and a
write to rs/rt, a read/write of rd, a HI/LO op or any control transfer
aborts the compile (`exit 1`) instead of miscompiling. Stress: 212 TUs with
real divides (47 registered main + 165 byte-perfect overlay), hook ON in both
forms — 0 aborts; non-nop instruction multisets equal except where as1's own
block-local copy-propagation / load-forwarding reacts to the moved block
boundaries (13 TUs, `move`/`li`/`addiu` counts).

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO` | `aspsx` \| `aspsx:N[,N...]` \| `N[,N...]` | defer every register-divisor div/divu/rem/remu, or only occurrences N (0-based, TU arrival order at f_gendiv; rd=$zero and immediate divisors are not counted) |
| | `aspsx-mffirst[:N,...]` | as `aspsx`, but when ONE record triggers both flushes the mflo/mfhi goes first (still check-first when rd is rs/rt). Retail evidence: 0x8008F148 / 0x8008EC58 `div ; mflo t2 ; <check> ; lui at ; sb t2,sym` (maspsx puts the check first there). Also consistent with 0x80091344 (`mfhi t6 ; <check>` ahead of a `div x,2` macro), which the hook does NOT model: an unlisted record always flushes check-first. |
| `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-DIVM] #N op=32 rd=5 rs=15 rt=3 -> DEFERRED`, then each flush with the record that caused it |

Out of scope (untouched): immediate divisors (as1's shift / `li at` forms —
retail's `div x,2^k` `bgez ; move at ; addiu at ; sra` is ASPSX's own form),
`mul x,2^k-1`, 64-bit divides (`f_genddiv`).

**Gates (2026-09-25).** Unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` Mismatched 0,
SHA1 2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b; all 47 registered IDO main
functions whose retail window carries `break 7` identical before/after
(`test_single_match`); 19 overlay functions with divides (one per overlay)
`BYTE-PERFECT (name-derived link, strict compare)`.

**Consumers.**
- **`main/func_8008BB24` 145/170 → BYTE-PERFECT 170/170**, Body 170, no Fixup,
  `audit_reloc_positions` OK. The whole residual (25 words, both divides) was
  the expansion order; with the macro deferred as1's own list scheduler gives
  retail's `div ; lui t8 ; lw t8 ; nop ; addiu t9 ; mflo a1 ; andi a1 ;
  <check> ; lui at ; b ; sw t9`. Every other token and both PostProc steps
  ablated load-bearing. `aspsx` and `aspsx-mffirst` give identical bytes.
- `main/func_80096A18` 24 → 27/112 (retail's `divu ; mfhi t9 ; bnez v0 ; nop ;
  break 7 ; beqz t9` at 0x80096B20; registers still one temp high).
- `main/func_8008F054`: score-neutral on the draft body (one word short
  before the first divide), but `aspsx-mffirst` reproduces the 0x8008F144
  region's shape and `aspsx` the second region's.

## Hook 62b: IDO52_AS1_DIV_POW2 — ASPSX's signed div/rem by an immediate power of two (2026-09-25)

Same applier as Hook 62 (`tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_divmacro_hooks.py
[--rebuild]`; independent marker `as1_dp2_site`, so it applies on top of an
as1.c that already carries Hook 62). Default OFF.

**Where the site is.** `cc -S` shows ugen emits `div $14,$4,4` / `rem $2,$4,8`
with an IMMEDIATE — the expansion is as1's, not ugen's. `f_gendiv` (L4647e4)
routes a power-of-two immediate to `func_463eb8`, which dispatches div (L4640ac),
divu (L463f54), rem (L4643fc), remu (L463fb4).

**The retail form** (all 16 library-region instances in 0x80086000-0x800BB3C0,
none in the game region — the game region is as1-assembled and uses as1's form):

    [nop]* ; bgez rs,1f ; move at,rs ; addiu at,at,2^k-1 ; 1: sra rd,at,k

as1's signed div is `bgez rs,1f ; sra rd,rs,k ; addiu at,rs,2^k-1 ; sra rd,at,k`
when rd != rs, and `bgez ; move at,rs ; addiu at,RS,K ; sra` when rd == rs.
Retail uses the `move at` form ALWAYS, with the addiu reading `$at`. The rem
form (`bgez ; andi rd,rs,K ; beqz rd ; nop ; addiu rd,rd,-2^k`) is already
as1's own.

**The residue nop (both div and rem).** Exactly one idle nop precedes the
`bgez` when rs's most recent definition IN THE SAME BLOCK is a load — however
far above it the scheduler put the load — plus as1's own load-delay nop when
the load is adjacent. All 16 instances fit, no counterexample:

| rs defined by | retail | instances |
|---|---|---|
| load in the same block, not adjacent | 1 nop | 0x8008D5AC (`lb a1` 5 words up), 0x800A149C (`lh t3` 5 up) |
| load in the same block, adjacent | 2 nops | 0x800A14C4, 0x800A1CA4, 0x800A335C, 0x800A3380 (+ rem 0x800A1C60) |
| ALU / earlier block / parameter | 0 | 0x8008C564, 0x8008CF84, 0x8008D000, 0x8009136C, 0x80091760, 0x800A18E8, 0x800A19E0, 0x800A1B3C, 0x800A1FEC (+ rem 0x8008C540, 0x800A19A8) |

(gccwrap's `--ido-div-macro-residue` models only "adjacent load"; the
non-adjacent rows are why the rule is block-scoped here.)

**Sites (build/5.2/as1.c, func_463eb8).**
1. `L464138` (signed div, |imm| < 0x10000): when the hook fires, force
   `v0 = 0` — as1's own rd==rs shape (`move at,rs` in the slot, branch
   offset 2) — and remember it;
2. `L4641b0`: the `addiu` takes `$at` (reg 1) as its source instead of rs;
3. `L464578` (signed rem, |imm| < 0x8000): residue nop only.
The residue nop is `f_emitnop(…,1)` emitted FIRST, inside the range the
routine's own `f_mark_unmovable(start, last)` pins (start is recorded at
entry), so the scheduler keeps it glued to the macro. Rule check: scan the
records already emitted (as1's record array holds the current block only —
verified by trace; it restarts at 0 after a label) backwards for rs's last
writer, stopping at any branch/jump; a load (lb/lh/lw/lbu/lhu) → nop.
Eligible: imm > 1, rs/rd not `$at`. Negative divisors, |imm| ≥ 0x10000,
divu/remu: stock.

**Soundness.** `bgez rs` over `move at,rs ; addiu at,at,2^k-1` then
`sra rd,at,k`: at = rs (+2^k-1 iff rs < 0) — round toward zero, the same
value as1's form computes; the nop is a nop. No word is dropped.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_DIV_POW2` | `aspsx` \| `aspsx:N[,N...]` \| `N[,N...]` | ASPSX form + residue nop at every eligible site, or only at occurrences N (0-based, TU arrival order; div and rem share the counter) |
| | `aspsx-nonop[:N,...]` | the div form only, never the residue nop |
| `IDO52_AS1_DIV_POW2_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-DP2] #N div\|rem rd rs imm -> ASPSX\|stock [+residue-nop]` (trace alone changes nothing) |

**Gates (2026-09-25), variable unset.** `build_level3_c.py --no-cache`:
Mismatched 0, SHA1 2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b. 33 registered
IDO main functions from `src/main/matched/` with `/2^k` / `%2^k` in the
source compiled with the pre-62b as1 and the new one: 33/33 identical
objects, all BYTE-PERFECT; 23 of those TUs reach the site (trace), 17 of
them change with the hook ON (the game-region ones — expected, they are
as1-assembled). Overlays with the as1 pow2 form in their asm
(TACO/func_801393F4, RPG_BAT/func_8014CB14, TEL/func_80133D5C,
TT/func_801356C8, DATE/func_80152684, EN_NICHI/func_80134998, DATE2/
func_801385CC, ETC/func_80145A94, NAME_ENT/func_80148634): all
`BYTE-PERFECT (name-derived link)` unset; the ones tried with it ON change —
so the site is reached and the default is inert.

**Consumers (2026-09-25).** Every library-region pow2 site in these now
reproduces retail word-for-word; none closes, the residuals are elsewhere:
- `main/func_800A3320` 22/55 F3 → **26/55 Body 55 F1** (NO_JUMP_MERGE and
  three as1 vetoes ablated out with it).
- `main/func_800A17FC` 27 → 28/149 (Body 146, F8).
- `main/func_800A1A50` 38/178 unchanged, Body 179 → 181 (the two residue
  nops are retail-true; the old count was compensation).
- `main/func_8008D580`: site verified, token NOT applied (1/54 on the
  current transcription vs 3/54).
- `main/func_8008D08C`, `main/func_80094660`: NO pow2 divide in retail — their
  `move at,rs ; sll rd,at,k ; addu/subu rd,rd,at` is ASPSX's `mul rd,rs,IMM`
  expansion (as1: `sll rd,rs,k ; addu rd,rd,rs`, no `$at`) — a different
  site (the as1 `mul` expander). **Built the same day as Hook 62c — and the
  parenthesis is wrong: as1 emits the same `move at` form whenever ugen hands
  it rd == rs; the difference is as1's own copy-propagation (see 62c).**
- `main/func_80091160`, `main/func_80094660` are `@Compiler: ido52-gas` —
  every as1 hook is inert there; gccwrap's `ido_expand_pow2_macros` already
  emits this form.

## Hook 62c: IDO52_AS1_MUL_CONST — keep the `mul` macro's `$at` copy out of as1's optimisers (2026-09-25)

Same applier as Hooks 62/62b (`tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_divmacro_hooks.py
[--rebuild]`; independent marker `as1_mulc_accept`, applies on top of an as1.c
that already carries 62/62b). Default OFF.

**Where the multiply is expanded.** `cc -S` shows ugen emits `x * K` (K not
register-held; `IDO52_CONST_NOINREG_VALUES` decides that) as the MACRO
`mul rd,rs,K`. as1's `f_genmul` (L462834) expands it through `f_smul`
(L4626c8), a recursive shift/add chain: odd K with K%4==3 → `smul(K+1)` then
`subu rd,rd,src`; K%4==1 → `smul(K-1)` then `addu`; even → `smul(K>>s)` then
`sll rd,rd,s`. That is the same signed-digit chain gccwrap's
`_mul_const_shift_add` (the ASPSX model) produces: 3 = 4-1, 60 = (16-1)<<2,
600 = ((((4+1)<<2)-1)<<2)-1)<<3 — verified by compiling K = 3,5,7,10,24,60,
168,600,16383,123457: identical words. **Before emitting, `f_genmul` itself
copies the operand to `$at` (`move at,rs`, L462b34..L462b80) when rd == rs and
|K| has ≥ 2 set bits (`negu at,rs` when K < 0), and every term reads `$at`.**
So as1's expansion IS ASPSX's, including the `$at` form. Then the chain is
budget-checked (L462d24: kept when shorter than the `li ; mult ; mflo`
fallback, else `f_backup_pinst`).

**What differs** is what as1 does next: the macro's words are ordinary
records (+34 "optimisable" byte set), so
- pass II copy-propagation (`func_419268`, the Hook 6 `NO_COPY_PROP` site)
  deletes `move at,rs` by renaming its producer: `lhu at,2(a0) ; nop ;
  sll v0,at,14 ; subu v0,v0,at`;
- with that vetoed, pass I forward-propagation (`f_do_passI_opt`, the Hook 44
  `NO_PASS1_PROP` site) rewrites `sll v0,at,14` into `sll v0,v0,14`.
`IDO52_AS1_NO_COPY_PROP=all IDO52_AS1_NO_PASS1_PROP=all` together reproduce
retail — proof of the diagnosis — but veto every copy in the TU. ASPSX never
optimises inside a macro.

**Retail census** (whole image; `move at,rX` + a `$at`-reading chain):

| site | K | form |
|---|---|---|
| 0x8008D0AC, 0x8008D0C0 (func_8008D08C) | 16383 = 2^14-1 | `move at,a1 ; sll a1,at,14 ; subu a1,a1,at` (and a3) |
| 0x800914FC (func_80091160), 0x8009195C (func_80091570), 0x800947F0 (func_80094660) | 60 = 15<<2 | `lw v0 ; nop ; move at,v0 ; sll v0,at,4 ; subu v0,v0,at ; sll v0,v0,2` |
| 0x800947CC (func_80094660) | 10 = 5<<1 | `mflo v1 ; move at,v1 ; sll v1,at,2 ; addu v1,v1,at ; sll v1,v1,1` |
| 0x800A0730 | 10 | `move at,t1 ; sll t1,at,2 ; lbu a0,18(a1) ; addu t1,t1,at ; sll t1,t1,1` (a load scheduled INTO the chain) |

7 instances, all library region, **all rd == rs**, all 2^k±1 × 2^s (no
3-term constant has an aliased instance; 168/600 occur only with rd != rs,
where as1 and ASPSX already agree word-for-word). **Counterexamples: 0** —
no library `sll rd,at,k ; addu|subu rd,rd,at` without the `move at` (the
folded as1 shape), and the game region (as1-assembled) has no `move at` and
no `$at`-reading chain at all. No residue-nop rule: the only nop
(0x800914FC etc.) is the ordinary load delay before `move at`.

**Site.** `f_genmul`'s accept branch (L462d24, chain within budget): for
`mul` (op 0x38) whose first emitted record is `addu at,rs,zero`, clear byte
+34 on that record and on every chain record that reads `$at` — the byte
`f_gendiv` clears on its own check sequence and both optimisers test
(pass II: `+34 == 0` → reset the copy table and skip; pass I: `+34 == 0` →
`func_412b40`, skip). Nothing is emitted, removed, moved or re-encoded, so
the product is as1's own; `negu` (K < 0) and rd != rs chains are stock.
The occurrence counter counts `$at`-copy sites only.

**It needs rd == rs from ugen.** ugen normally gives `mul` a fresh temp and
copies back (`mul $12,$5,16383 ; move $5,$12`), and as1 then folds the move —
no `$at` anywhere. Getting retail's in-place `mul $5,$5,16383` is a Hook 36c
force on the mul's temp request: `IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG=<occ>:<rs>` +
`IDO52_UGEN_TEMP_REG_INPLACE=occ:<occ>` (trace must say `INPLACE OK ...
already holds this node`; an `INPLACE UNCHECKED` v/a force to a DIFFERENT
register gives the stock chain plus moves).

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_MUL_CONST` | `aspsx` \| `aspsx:N[,N...]` \| `N[,N...]` | keep every `$at`-copy chain, or only occurrences N (0-based, TU order, `$at`-copy sites only) |
| `IDO52_AS1_MUL_CONST_TRACE` | presence | `[AS1-MULC] #N mul K=.. rd=.. rs=.. words=.. -> ASPSX\|stock (n kept)`, and `mul K=.. : no $at copy -> stock` for every other constant mul (trace alone changes nothing) |

**Gates (2026-09-25), variable unset.** `build_level3_c.py --no-cache`:
Matched 1213, Mismatched 0, SHA1 2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b —
and the SAME with `IDO52_AS1_MUL_CONST=aspsx` exported globally (no
registered function has an rd == rs constant multiply). 41 registered IDO
main functions with `* 3/5/…/60/168/600` in the source (nibble_decimal_convert,
_debug_number_show, system_config_pad_mode, spot_light, … — 40 constant-mul sites
traced, none `$at`-copy): 41/41 BYTE-PERFECT unset and identical with the
variable set. Overlays (resolver): BUNKASAI func_80151A18/80142BF4/80146A80/
80150B84, DATE func_8013F1B0/801399B4/8013A2D8/801324D8/80134A30/80136410,
BUNKA_SD func_80132FF8 — 11/11 `BYTE-PERFECT (name-derived link)`, equal
lengths, unset and set.

**Consumers (2026-09-25).**
- `main/func_8008D08C` 23/233 Body 224 → **33/233, Body 226, Fixup 0**
  (INPLACE 4:a1,5:a1,6:a2,7:a2 + MUL_CONST=aspsx + re-swept Hook 36
  29:t5,17:t7,60:t9). Both sites load-bearing (:0 alone 18, :1 alone 32).
  Words 8-10 now `move at,a1 ; sll a1,at,14 ; subu a1,a1,at`. Residual: rv
  in a2 (retail a3), second chain scheduled after `mflo a1` (retail before
  the global loads), 7 words short.
- `main/func_80094660` (native as1 screen; file kept on ido52-gas): with
  INPLACE 24:a0,28:v0 both chains emit retail's form (0x800947F0 word-exact)
  but the score is 8/245 Body 218 Fixup 3 with or without 62c — the head /

## Hook 62d: three more ASPSX flush rules for the deferred div/rem macro (2026-09-26)

Same applier as Hooks 62/62b/62c (`tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_divmacro_hooks.py
[--rebuild]`). It **upgrades an as1.c patched by the old applier in place**:
it swaps the Hook 62 helper block (right before `f_parsestmt`) and adds two
sites (marker `Hook 62d: IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO_SPLIT_CMPBR site`). Default OFF.
Each rule acts only on top of `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO` and only while a check
is pending.

**The wall.** `func_80094660` was 217/245, Body 245, Fixup 0: every word
already had retail's opcode and registers. The 28-word residual was where
two divide checks sit. E105 measured all 128 value-form combinations of
62/62c and proved that no record order could reach it: the Hook 62
classifier flushed at every non-whitelisted record. Three of those
records are ones ASPSX lets through. All three rules come from this
function's retail words:

| retail | what ASPSX did | old Hook 62 |
|---|---|---|
| 0x800946D8..0x80094738 | `div zero,t3,t9` (signed 60000000/x); `mflo v1` at its use; `multu t0,v1 ; mflo t4` (reg\*reg `mul`), both `*60`/`*10` shift chains (rd != rs, no `$at`), **then** the 9-word signed check, then `sltu at ; beqz at` | flushed the check at the `mul` record (`op=56 fmt=3 form=3`) |
| 0x800947DC..0x80094828 | divu #2 (`v1/t8`) → `mflo t9 ; sh`; the in-place `*60` `$at` chain `move at,v0 ; sll ; subu ; sll`; `divu zero,v1,v0` (remu #3); `srl t5,v0,1` (the `/2`); `mfhi t4 ; sltu at,t5,t4 ; bnez t8.. ; bnez v0.. ; beqz at` | flushed #2's check at the `mul`, then at the `/2` record, then #3's check before the `bgeu` |

| Variable | Format | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO_MUL_PASS` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N...]` | A `mul rd,rs,rt` / `mul rd,rs,K` record (op 56) passes pending checks. It flushes a pending mflo/mfhi first, because the multiply overwrites HI/LO. It flushes the checks if rd is a check's rs/rt. It also flushes them when a **signed** check is pending and f_genmul will use `$at`, because that check uses `$at`. f_genmul uses `$at` when it copies the operand: K<0 (`negu at`), or rd == rs with ≥2 set bits in \|K\| (`move at`, predicted exactly as f_genmul L462a6c..L462afc decides). The over-budget `li at,K ; multu ; mflo` fallback is caught at f_genmul L462dd0 (new site), before the `li at`. |
| `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO_QUEUE` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N...]` | A register-divisor div/divu/rem/remu that will itself be deferred passes the pending checks: it writes no GPR, and a pending mflo/mfhi is flushed first. So does an unsigned `divu rd,rs,2^k` / `remu rd,rs,2^k` (func_463eb8 expands it to one `srl` / `andi`). Checks queue (≤8) and flush oldest first. |
| `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO_SPLIT_CMPBR` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N...]` | A register compare-branch macro (fmt 6, form 8, ops 14/15/17/18/20/21/23/24 = the `slt[u] at ; beqz\|bnez at` family, both registers ≠ $zero/$at) flushes a pending mflo/mfhi it reads, emits its `slt`, and **then** flushes the checks (new site f_parseafrrl `L44f3ac`, between the slt and the branch), then the branch. It is not split while a signed check is pending, because the slt would clobber the `$at` that check uses. The tempo arm is this case: the check flushes before the `sltu`, which is what retail does. |

Occurrences are 0-based and **per rule**. Each counts the records that reach
its decision while a check is pending (TRACE prints `62d MUL_PASS #N`,
`62d QUEUE #N` and `62d SPLIT_CMPBR #N`, with PASS / flush / SPLIT and the
reason). The applier deliberately rejects a bare number such as `=1`, with
a warning: a bare number reads as an index list, not as "on".

**Soundness.** Nothing is created, dropped or re-encoded. The flushes call
the same emitters f_gendiv calls, only later in the stream, and as1's own
scheduler orders the block. The safety net now tracks every queued check.
It gains one rule: while a signed check is pending, any emitted word that
writes `$at` aborts the compile (`exit 1`). With the variables unset,
behaviour is unchanged. With the 62d variables unset, the upgraded as1
gives byte-identical final bytes to the pre-62d as1 on all 1,132
registered IDO main TUs. That holds both with each file's own env and with
`IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO` forced on (the file's value, else `aspsx`), so the
queue refactor of the Hook 62 state is inert.

**Gates (2026-09-26), variables unset.** `build_level3_c.py --no-cache`:
Mismatched 0, Non-reloc fixups 0, SHA1 2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b.
`verify_overlay_bytes.py DATE` 670/670 and `TT` 298/298: per-function
statuses identical before and after the rebuild.

**Census, forms forced ON** (`MUL_PASS=all QUEUE=all SPLIT_CMPBR=all`) on
top of the file's own `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO` (`aspsx` where the file has
none). Compared with the same build without 62d, over the 1,132 registered
IDO main TUs, 0 aborted. 26 objects change:
- 24 are game region (0x80041000-0x80085FFF; as1-assembled in retail, so
  forcing Hook 62 there is already the wrong model): vcount_meter_disp,
  keta_n, _sprite_set_light_effect1, clut_gray_set, addr_init_main_rom, keta_n,
  show_number_hex, get_fnt, save_file_select_init, mascot_sel_init, biorhythm_sf_set,
  icon_disp_switch, func_8007837C, bustup_addr_set3, _debug_number_show,
  cdq_command_enqueue, func_80074890, swap_env, _pre_opening_exit, mouse_pad_set,
  date_sprite_init, SD_CDLevelAvg, _sprite_set_box_shade, srn_set;
- 2 are library shippers of `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO` (of the 17 registered
  files that ship `DIV_MACRO`/`DIV_POW2`; the other 15 are unchanged):
  `func_80091160` (aspsx:0,3,5) and `func_80091570` (aspsx:3,4). Both are
  sister SEQ-open functions carrying the SAME `*60 ; divu ; srl ; mfhi ;
  sltu ; check ; check ; beqz` tail (retail 0x800914FC..0x80091534 and
  0x8009195C..), and both are BYTE-PERFECT today only through
  swap_instructions bridges that move those check words (28 and 27 swaps;
  E55's note asks for exactly this primitive). With their swaps removed,
  62d takes func_80091160 228 → 231/260 and func_80091570 255 → 261/283.
  The first bridged region (a signed `div x,2^k` pow2 macro, `mfhi` before
  the check) is untouched: Hook 62b's pow2 expansion uses `$at` and a
  branch, which is a different rule. **Next consumers:** re-fit these two
  with 62d plus their own occurrence lists, and retire the swap bridges.
  Not done here: they ship as-is, and the forms are off in their env.

**Consumer.**
- **`main/func_80094660` 217/245 → BYTE-PERFECT 245/245**, Body 245, Fixup
  0, `audit_main_relocs` OK, `audit_reloc_positions` OK. Env =
  E75's + the three forms `=all` (each load-bearing: without MUL_PASS
  183/245 Body 246; without QUEUE 211 Body 246; without SPLIT_CMPBR 237).
  The only other change: `TIE_TAKE` 239,242 → 231,234, the same two bb-9
  ties with the occurrence numbering shifted (each still load-bearing,
  243 without either).

## Hook 63: IDO52_SPLIT_BLOCKS — exact block set of an f_split piece (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_splitblocks_hooks.py [uopt.c] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/uopt.new` and installs it with a rename).
**Add it to the post-`make clean` chain after BOTH `apply_uopt_splitadmit_hooks.py`
(Hook 53) and `apply_uopt_splitpiece_hooks.py` (Hook 60)**: it anchors on Hook 53's
helper block, its f_split trace calls and its f_addadjacents block, and on Hook 60's
`ido52_h60_reset` / `ido52_h60_active`. Idempotent (marker `ido52_splitblk_init`).
Default OFF.

**The decision.** `f_split` (recomp `L46cd98`) copies the range into a fresh
remainder (new bitpos) that owns EVERY LiveUnit, picks a START LiveUnit (`L46cf24`:
first LU with `firstisstr` lu+20 whose block has the colour free, or passed in a
register lu+19; fallback `L46cfc0`: first LU with occurrences), arrives at `L46d0a8`
with `s0` = that LU, moves it into the piece (which keeps the bitpos) and grows the
piece by BFS over SUCCESSOR edges (`L46d208`), calling `f_addadjacents` (`L46b6ac`)
for every expanded block that is not dead-out / `is_cup` / ijp-tailed; its `L46b7ec`
test admits (`L46b858`) or rejects (`L46b988`) each successor still in the remainder.
Everything downstream (interference rebuild, forbidden sets, colouring, rlods/rstrs
reload/store placement) runs unmodified on whatever partition comes out. So a piece's
block set is decided at exactly two points — the start LU and the per-candidate
admission — and Hook 43's "first cut is the wrong blocks" was the START: the
natural start is the first `firstisstr` LU (the store / the init block), so the
piece always grows from there. Hook 53 already controlled admission but could not
move the start.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_SPLIT_BLOCKS` | `KEY:BLOCKS[,KEY:BLOCKS...]` | for a matching split: START = the first listed block (list order) that has a LiveUnit in the range (else the natural start, reported); every candidate successor is admitted iff it is listed **and** the piece keeps a colour (Hook 53's guard; a refused block is reported), every unlisted candidate is rejected (the natural reject path). Overrides Hook 53 for that split. |
| | `KEY` = `B`, `B#N`, `R.P`, `R.P#N` | `B` = bitpos of the range being split (the piece keeps it, so `B#2` is the re-pick of the piece that kept B); `R.P` = Hook 60's ROOT.PIECE key of a remainder (a dotted key switches Hook 60's key tracking on by itself); `#N` = only the N-th split of that key in the procedure (1-based). |
| | `BLOCKS` = `bb[+bb...]`, `lo-hi` ranges | uopt block numbers (`MEM_U16(graphnode+8)`, as the Hook 53 trace and the `-Wo,-l` flow-graph listing print them) |
| `IDO52_SPLIT_BLOCKS_TRACE` | `1` | `[SPLITBLK]` for EVERY split: `split bit=B [key=R.P] cut#N rest=R natural start bb=X; candidates (bb:firstisstr/inreg/deadout/occ): ...` (the range's LiveUnit set), the forced start, each forced ADMIT/REJECT, and `piece bit=B blocks: ... (colorsleft C)` plus any listed block the BFS never reached. |

Limits (by construction, not by the hook): blocks are still reached only through
successor edges from admitted, non-dead-out blocks; only blocks IN the range can be
admitted (a block where the variable is not live — e.g. the block holding the
killing store of a global — cannot be put in any piece); the start override runs
only when uopt found a start at all (the no-start failure path is untouched). A
piece is colourable/spilled by the usual cost test — pair it with Hook 60
`FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL=B.0:A` (the piece that kept B) / `R.P/-1:A` (make a remainder
fail its cost test so it is cut, then colour the cut piece) and Hook 37 colours.

**Workflow.** Run `IDO52_SPLIT_BLOCKS_TRACE=1` (with the Hook 43 force that makes
the split happen) to read the range's blocks; get the block graph from
`IDO52_UOPT_TRACE=5 -Wo,-l,<file>` (`flow graph` section: node, `pre::::` /
`suc::::` lines); then give each cut its block list. The block numbers are stable per
compile; the remainder bitposes are not (use `R.P` keys).

**Inertness (2026-09-25).** `build_level3_c.py --no-cache`: Matched 1212,
`Mismatched: 0`, SHA1 `2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`, 0 non-reloc
fixups. Every shipped user of FORCE_WEB_RANGE_SPLIT / FORM_SPLIT /
FORCE_WEB_SPILL/NOSPILL / WEB_PIECE_* / SPLIT_ADMIT/REJECT (37 files: 26 main, 11
overlay) re-verified BYTE-PERFECT (main: Exact N/N with Body == target; overlays:
resolver `*** BYTE-PERFECT ***`, len t == c). Direct A/B against the pre-hook uopt
binary on 298 TUs (those 37 + 150 other `@CompileEnv` files + 120 random overlay
files, each with its own env/flags): 295 byte-identical objects, 3 fail on both
(`func_8008B93C`, `func_8008E7D0`, `func_800A3104`, GCC-pipeline files the plain
`cc -c` harness does not drive). Regeneration: the applier on the saved pre-hook
`uopt.c` reproduces the live one byte-for-byte; a second run is a no-op.

**Measured on the two specimens that asked for it.**

*SHOUGATU/func_8013EB6C* (file: 179/228, `s`-local shape, Hook 36/37 fitted).
On the LEAD shape (SEL read directly in the loop tests, 94/228 unhooked) SEL is bit
21: LiveUnits in blocks {5,7,10,13,21,22,23,29}, live-through 8/9/11/12/14; the loop
header 4 is NOT in the range (the store in 4 kills; the BFS from 13 is offered 14 but
never 4). Natural Hook 43 cut = {21,22} (post-loop).
`IDO52_FORCE_WEB_RANGE_SPLIT=21 IDO52_SPLIT_BLOCKS=21#1:7-12
IDO52_FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL=21.0:5 IDO52_FORCE_WEB_COLOR=21:t4` gives exactly
retail's loop shape up to the last clause: first test on the stored temp (`andi
t9,t8`), `lbu t4,SEL` at the head of the `== 5` clause and every later clause on
t4 — **173/228, raw count 228 (exact)**; one greedy round of every Hook 37 colour
x every Hook 36 force (1,000+ candidates) buys nothing. `21#1:7-13` (last clause on
t4 too) is 225 words: retail's back-edge block `lui t4; lbu t4,SEL; b top` is a
reload INTO block 4, and block 4 is not in SEL's range in any spelling measured, so
no piece can end there — that reload is not a split-placement question. The file
stays on its 179 env.

*VALEN/func_8013283C* (file: 92/310). On the natural -cckr rewrite (webs 43/40/37 =
best0/best1/best2; blocks 3 = init, 4 header, 5 call, 7/9/11 the compares, 8/10/12
the arms, 13 latch) the env
`IDO52_FORCE_WEB_RANGE_SPLIT=37,40,43
IDO52_SPLIT_BLOCKS=43#1:3,40#1:3,37#1:3,43.1:7+8,40.1:8,37.1:8,40.2:9+10,37.2:10,37.3:11+12
IDO52_FORCE_WEB_NOSPILL=37.0:5,40.0:5,43.0:5,37.1/-1:5,40.1/-1:5,43.1/-1:5,40.2/-1:5,37.2/-1:5,37.3/-1:5`
produces retail's per-block partition exactly as traced (init pieces {3}, best0 {7,8},
best1 {8} and {9,10}, best2 {8},{10},{11,12}, the `.N` remainders spilled): the
3 `sh`+3 `lh` call spill is gone, every compare reloads from the home, and the arms
carry the `sll/sra` + `move` register copies. It scores low (26-39/310, body 304-316)
because the rewrite's source differs elsewhere (retail recomputes `A+B` in arms 1/2
where our body CSEs it; home slots 86/88/90 vs 80/82/84; head `li`/255 order). The
Hook 60c note's "next primitive" is thus delivered; what remains on VALEN is source
shape, not piece placement.

## Hook 64: IDO52_AS1_KEEP_WIDE_AND — keep `li at,-N ; and` unnarrowed (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_wideimm_hooks.py [--as1 PATH] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/as1.new` and installs it with a rename). Independent
anchor (func_416ac4's own recomp label `L416c00`), idempotent (marker
`as1_wand_veto`); run after the other as1 appliers. Default OFF.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_WIDE_AND` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` \| `occ:N[,N..]` — 0-based narrowing sites, one counter per as1 run (= per TU) |
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_WIDE_AND_TRACE` | `1` — `[AS1-WIDEAND] #N word=<and> rd= rs= imm=<K> (<dec>) -> andi 0xKKKK [-> VETOED]` |

**The decision.** ugen emits `x & ~0x20` as the macro `and rd,rs,-33` (`cc -S`);
as1 expands it to `li at,-33 ; and rd,rs,at`. as1's pass-I peephole **func_416ac4**
(dispatched from `f_do_passI_opt` for SPECIAL funct `0x24` == `and` ONLY — `or`/`xor`
have no counterpart routine, so the hook covers `and` alone) then rewrites the pair
into one `andi rd,rs,K&0xffff` when (a) the demanded-bits count of `rd` in the
per-register table at `0x100221b0` is ≤ 16 (an `sb`/`sh` consumer) and (b) the other
source is defined by a constant load (`func_416304` finds the def, `func_416964`
extracts it). The word therefore reaches the andi simplifier func_416ca8 already
as an `andi`, which is why Hook 29 (`KEEP_ANDI`, the inverse direction, at
func_416ca8) cannot touch it. The hook sits at `L416c00`, after both proofs have
succeeded and immediately before the rewrite, so only real narrowings are counted;
a veto returns 0, func_416ac4's own "no change" exit — exactly the code as1 emits
when its proof fails. The `and` is the same `and`: semantics unchanged.

**Retail evidence (per-occurrence, NOT a blanket rule).** Game region
0x80041000–0x80086000 of SLPS_000.65: **91** `andi rd,rs,0x8000..0xfffe` words
feed an `sb` (the narrowed shape) against **4** `li at,-N ; and` pairs feeding an
`sb` — all four inside `sprite_slot_disp` (0x8004841C −33, 0x800484A0 −4, 0x800484E8
−129, 0x80048510 −17). Every overlay narrows (≈180 sb-fed andi, 0 sb-fed wide).
Retail's as1 narrowed; something about these four sites defeated the proof in the
original compile. Use an index list, never `all`, unless every site in the TU is
a retail wide site.

**Validation.** Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` Mismatched 0, SHA1
2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b; 34/34 registered byte-perfect main
functions whose C has `& ~N`/`&= ~N` re-verified identical (`test_single_match`);
12/12 overlay functions with `& ~0x..` masks identical (resolver strict compare).
Micro TU `s->f2 &= ~0x20` (u8 field): unset → `andi t7,t6,0xffdf`; `=0` →
`and t7,t6,at`.

**First use: `sprite_slot_disp`** (semi). Trace sites #0 −33, #1 −4, #2 −129 (the
−17 site is already wide in our compile). `KEEP_WIDE_AND=0,1,2` restores the three
words (Body 816 → 819). The fourth missing word was the `bne` delay slot at
0x8004840C, which retail leaves a nop: our as1 boosts `addiu t3,v0,1` into it via
xbb (`XBB_PICK_TRACE` #40), and after `XBB_SKIP_CAND=40` the fall-through fill
(`NO_FALLTHRU_FILL` occurrence 28, re-derived under the skip) takes it instead —
both are needed. Result Body 820 == target, 311/816 → 412/820, Fixup 6 → 1.
With the four words restored the function was count-exact for the first time,
and the Hook 36 temp-register sweep its header recorded as never run
(`sweep_temp_reg_greedy.py --staged`, then seeded staged + seeded greedy, both
converged) took it 412/820 → **709/820**, Fixup 1, Body 820 (13 forces after greedy
ablation). Still SEMI: the upstream `lw a1,144(sp)` hoist / `move s3,v1`, and the
two vetoed arms schedule `sb ; b ; sh zero` where retail has `sh zero ; b ; sb`.

## Hook 65: IDO52_AS1_FTF_FORCE — fall-through fill of an `$at`-only writer past a `break`-held live-in (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_ftfforce_hooks.py [--as1 PATH] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/as1.new` and installs it with a rename). Idempotent
(marker `as1_ftff_force`). **Requires Hook 27** (`apply_as1_delay_hooks.py`): it
reuses that hook's fall-through-fill counter `as1_ftf_seq` and its `as1_df_parse`
selector, so `FTF_FORCE=occ:N` indexes exactly the `[AS1-FTF] #N` candidates that
`IDO52_AS1_FTF_TRACE` prints and `IDO52_AS1_NO_FALLTHRU_FILL` vetoes. Run it after
`apply_as1_wideimm_hooks.py` (last in the chain). Default OFF: with the env unset
the hook returns before reading anything.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_FTF_FORCE` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` \| `occ:N[,N..]` — Hook 27 fall-through-fill occurrence indices |
| `IDO52_AS1_FTF_FORCE_TRACE` | `1` — one line per candidate refused by the live-at-target test: `[AS1-FTF] #N cand=<word> target=<bb>@<pc> live_at=<0/1> at_only=<0/1> first_at=<word>[ (after break)] DEF-FIRST\|USE-FIRST\|UNDECIDED\|NOT-SCANNED -> FORCED\|REFUSED` |

**The decision.** `func_41d7b4` (per-block emit) drops the next block's first word
into the previous block's `branch ; nop` slot after `f_eligible` (Hook 27's
candidate site, `L41daa4`), `f_cannot_trap`, and **`func_41d22c`**:

1. `f_defuse(word)` → def mask (sp+52/56/60) and use mask (sp+40..). Bit
   `1<<(31-r)` per GPR, so `$at` is `0x40000000`. A def outside the GPR word refuses.
2. If the optimisation flag byte `0x1002f8b0` is CLEAR, as1 accepts only a def mask
   of exactly `0x40000000` — its own "an `$at`-only writer may always take the slot"
   rule (the ASPSX behaviour, see Hook 54).
3. If it is SET (every -O2 compile here): refuse a load (`f_loads_from_memory`), find
   the branch's TARGET block (`f_find_branch_target`), and refuse when
   `def_mask & target->live_in (bb+36) != 0`.

The hook replaces only the refusal in (3), at the `t3 = t1 & t2` test in
`func_41d22c`, and only when all of these hold:

* the occurrence is selected;
* the def mask is exactly `0x40000000` (other def words zero — already required by
  (1)) **and** the word decodes as an ALU op writing `$at`: SPECIAL
  shift/arith/logic/slt with `rd==1`, or `addi/addiu/slti/sltiu/andi/ori/xori/lui`
  with `rt==1`;
* a walk of **every path out of the target block** (successor list `bb+16`,
  records `bb+12`, stride 0x28, `f_defuse` per record) meets a DEFINITION of `$at`
  before any USE on every path, or leaves the function through a block ending in
  `jr ra`. A use first → `USE-FIRST`, refused; a dead end without `jr ra`, or more
  than 256 blocks → `UNDECIDED`, refused.

Hazards, trap checks, the load refusal, `f_eligible` and the pc-contiguity test
before the rewind are untouched.

**Why as1 holds `$at` live there, and why the fill is sound.** In the only case seen
so far the live-in comes from a `break`: the `rem` / `div` macro's divide-by-zero
trap `break 7`. as1's liveness treats a `break` as reading every register (the
target's live-in is `0xdff7ffff`, nearly all set), although `f_defuse` gives the
`break` word empty masks. The hook's walk therefore passes it, and the trace marks
the verdict `(after break)`. On every path the program's own `$at` value is dead:
the next `$at` access is `li at,-1` (the macro's overflow check), and the other
path returns. The one observable change is the `$at` value a `break 7` handler
would see on a divide by zero — and `$at` is the assembler's temporary, not program
state; retail shows the same value there. On the fall-through path the word
executes exactly where it did.

**Retail evidence.** `func_8009607C` (library region, 0x8009607C): the 36-way
compare-tree dispatch has `beq a1,at,<case 19> ; sltiu at,a1,20` and
`beq a1,at,<case 104> ; sltiu at,a1,105`. Both targets begin `lw v0,0(v1) ; beqz a3`
and fall into `div ; mfhi ; bnez a3 ; nop ; break 7 ; li at,-1 ; ...`. Our as1
offered both words (fall-through candidates #22 and #44) and refused them in
`func_41d22c`; the E25 probe `x % shift` → `x & shift` (no `break`) committed the
fill, while `remu` (still `break 7`) refused. ⚠️ **Per occurrence, never a blanket
rule**: `=all` changes 6 of 2,021 corpus objects — besides `func_8009607C`, four
registered byte-perfect main functions (`_pre_opening_exit`, `clut_gray_set`,
`tel_code_check`, `func_8008C9A8`) and the byte-perfect overlay function
`EN_NICHI/func_80136A80`, whose retail slots were left unfilled.

**Validation.** Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` — 1247/1247 matched,
`Mismatched: 0`, `Output SHA1: 2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`.
Old-vs-new as1 over 2,021 TUs (all `src/main/matched` + `semi-matched` IDO files
and 600 random overlay files, each with its own `@CompileEnv`/`@CompileFlags`):
2,020 byte-identical objects; the one difference is `func_8009607C`, the only file
that sets the env (LIVE). `audit_hook_inertness.py --only IDO52_AS1_FTF_FORCE
--sample 60`: INERT on its 60-overlay random sample at every value, `all`
included. The hook is SAMPLE-BOUND: the shape needs a `break`-holding target. 42
registered byte-perfect main functions whose C has both `%` and `switch` (plus
the four `=all` movers) re-verified 100% with `test_single_match`.

**First use: `func_8009607C`** (semi). `IDO52_AS1_FTF_FORCE=occ:22,44` changes the
function from Body 617, 464/615, Fixup 50 to **Body 615, 615/615, BYTE-PERFECT**,
with `audit_reloc_positions` OK (45 relocs). Each index alone: `occ:22` 568/615
(Body 616), `occ:44` 464/615 (Body 616). Every other occurrence index in the file's
env was unchanged by the forced fills, because they commit at emit, after the
scheduling passes those indices key on.

## Hook 66: IDO52_UGEN_MOVE_LOOP_SHIFT — ugen block-move loop threshold (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_ugen_moveloop_hooks.py [--ugen PATH] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/ugen.new` and installs it with a rename). Independent
anchors (helper on `f_eval_mov`'s own definition, site on its recomp label
`L428bb8`, checked to lie inside `f_eval_mov`), idempotent (marker
`ugen_mloop_thresh`); run after the other ugen appliers (last in the chain). Default
OFF: with the env unset the shift is the stock `3` and every object is unchanged.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_UGEN_MOVE_LOOP_SHIFT` | `N`, 2..15 — replaces the stock shift `3`. Anything else (incl. `0`/`1`) is refused with one stderr warning and stock `3` is used |
| `IDO52_UGEN_MOVE_LOOP_TRACE` | `1` — `[UGEN-MOVELOOP] size=<bytes> unit=<u> thresh=<u<<N> -> LOOP\|UNROLL` for every block move that reaches the test |

**The decision.** ugen's **`f_eval_mov`** (the MOV/MOVV tree node: struct assignment,
by-value struct argument) picks the move unit `s0` (4; 8 only on the doubleword path,
never taken by our `-mips1` compiles) and holds the byte size in `s1`. At
**`L428bb8`**:

```c
t5 = s0 << 3;  at = t5 < s1;  if (!at) goto L428df4;   /* L428df4 = unrolled copy */
```

so it emits a LOOP iff `size > unit << 3` (> 32 bytes at unit 4), otherwise an
unrolled `lw/sw` (`lwl/lwr/swl/swr` when unaligned) sequence. The loop body copies
**3 units per iteration** (12 bytes: `move t2,src ; addiu t1,t2,<3u*k> ; move t3,dst ;
lwl/lwr x3 ... ; bne`) and the remainder falls through to the unrolled tail at
`L428df4`. The hook replaces the `3` with `N` (`t5 = ugen_mloop_thresh(s0, s1)`);
nothing else in `f_eval_mov` changes. `N < 2` is refused because the loop body is a
do-while over 3 units: at `N = 1` a 9..11-byte move would reach it and over-copy.
`N ≥ 2` guarantees `size > 4u > 3u`, at least one full iteration.

**Retail evidence — a compiler fingerprint, not a free parameter.** Whole-binary
census (run E35, recorded in `src/main/semi-matched/func_80093A88.c`): the
**library region (≥ 0x80086000) has zero unrolled block copies over 12 bytes** and
LOOPS its 18-, 20- and 32-byte copies (`func_80093A88`'s two by-value struct
arguments, 32 and 18 bytes; 20-byte loops at 0x800A0AA8 and 0x800A0B30); its only
unrolled copy is 12 bytes (0x800B4924). That is exactly `size > 16`, i.e. `N = 2`.
The **game region** unrolls a 20-byte by-value argument at 0x800411AC and loops only
at 64 and 92 bytes — stock `N = 3`, and the `=2` census below confirms it from the
other side: 5 registered byte-perfect game-region main functions and **90
byte-perfect overlay functions** break at `N = 2`, none improves. So the compiler
that built retail's library-region IDO code differs from stock 5.2 on this
constant (as it already does on assembly — ASPSX, not as1; see the third pipeline).
Per the **compiler-set rule** we are NOT building or probing another IDO version to
explain it: the project stays on IDO 5.2 + Psy-Q GCC, and this hook gates the one
constant, per translation unit, through `@CompileEnv`. (The IRIX IDO 5.2 binaries under
qemu-irix unroll the same moves our recomp does, so this
is not a recomp fidelity gap.)

**Validation.**
1. Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` — `C functions: 1256`, `Matched: 1256`,
   `Mismatched: 0`, `Output SHA1: 2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`,
   `Non-reloc fixups: 0`.
2. Old ugen vs new ugen, env unset, every file under its own `@CompileEnv` /
   `@CompileFlags` (`-S` for `@Compiler: ido52-*` files, whose `--` gccwrap flags are
   dropped): all `src/main/matched` + `src/main/semi-matched` IDO files (1,294) and
   600 random `src/overlay/**` files (seed 66) — **1,894 / 1,894 byte-identical**
   (178 GCC files skipped). Note: a copy of `cc` must be a real file, not a
   symlink — the driver resolves its pass directory through the link target and
   a symlinked "old" cc silently runs the new ugen.
3. `=2` forced on every file (census; the hook is **never** to be applied blindly):
   * registered byte-perfect main functions that change (all game region, all
     BYTE-PERFECT at stock): `_sprite_set_box_shade` 195 → Body 198, 44/195;
     `func_800410AC` 130 → 146, 63/130; `save_work_restore` 165 → 162, 48/164;
     `func_8005970C` 191 → 185, 64/188; `title_obj_move` 184 → 184, 101/184.
   * open library semis whose count moves: of 110 IDO library-region semis, only
     **`func_80093A88`** (Body 378 → 367).
   * overlays, full tree (6,220 `.c`): 94 change — 90 `byte_perfect`, 4 `asm_only`
     (`DATE/func_8013A9B4`, `EN_NICHI/func_8013903C`, `TACO/func_8013D758`,
     `TAIIKU/func_80138310`), 0 semis. TACO (26) and TAIIKU (21) carry half of them.
4. Micro TU (`S20 = S20; S12 = S12; S36 = S36`): stock → 20 UNROLL, 12 UNROLL, 36
   LOOP; `=2` → 20 LOOP, 12 UNROLL, 36 LOOP; `=1` → refused, stock.

**First use: `func_80093A88`** (library semi, 1520 B). Trace: SA 32 B and SB 18 B both
`thresh=16 -> LOOP`, retail's two loop shapes. Body / Exact / Fixup:

| env | Body | Exact | Fixup |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| none | 378 | 44/380 | 16 |
| `SHIFT=2` | 367 | 47/368 | 16 |
| `LDA_NOINREG_CLASSES=1:2:7` | 370 | 70/372 | 19 |
| `SHIFT=2` + `LDA 1:2:7` (shipped) | 358 | 72/360 | 19 |

Not count-exact (22 words short of 380). Opcode census target − ours: `nop` +8,
`sw` +4, `lw` +3, `move` +3, `b`/`beqz`/`li` +1, `bnez` −1 — the structural residual
the file already names (per-loop `s0`/`s1` shrink-wrap saves/restores, the
live-through `li v1,1` web, unfilled slots). The hook removed the copy-shape half
of the wall; the rest is not a threshold question. Still SEMI; not registered.

## Hook 67: IDO52_CFE_SWITCH_CHAIN — retail library-region `switch` lowering (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_cfe_switchchain_hooks.py [--cfe PATH] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/cfe.new` and installs it with a rename). The first
**cfe** hook: the jump-table-versus-compare decision is made in the front end,
not in ugen (the ucode already carries `XJP` or `equ/tjp` chains; at `-O1` there
is no uopt). Idempotent (marker `cfsw_enter`); independent of every other applier
including Hook 68 (both patch `cfe.c`, at unrelated functions, either order).
Default OFF: with the env unset nothing is selected and every object is unchanged.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_CFE_SWITCH_CHAIN` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` — 0-based index of the `switch` statement in the TU (every switch counts, source order) |
| `IDO52_CFE_SWITCH_TRACE` | `1` — `[CFE-SWITCH] #N cases=<c> range=<r> -> TABLE\|MIXED\|CHAIN[ (forced, retail tree)]`, one line per switch that has cases |

**The decision.** `func_4648f8` (switch-statement codegen) walks the sorted case
list into 16-byte cluster records `{value hi, value lo, count, case node}` at
`sp+0xa8`: a value within 25 of the running cluster's top extends it; a cluster
that closes with fewer than 5 values (`at = a1 < 0x5`, the two sites at
`L464c48` / `L464e88`) is split into single-value records; any surviving
multi-value cluster becomes an `XJP` table (`func_463aac`). The records go to
`func_4640e4`, which emits the dispatch right there, **before** the body: runs
of < 8 singles as a linear `equ ; tjp` chain, otherwise a two-way split
`leq mid ; tjp L ; <right> ; L: <left>`. The caller (`func_465444`, `L466a50`)
then emits the body and the break label. So 13 cases over range 45 (the `%`
conversion letters) is one cluster → a table at every option.

Retail's library-region trees (`0x800B5444`, `0x80096784`, `0x8009325C`) differ
in three coupled ways, all reproduced by one model:

* **no table** — every case is a single-value record;
* **placement** — `key -> $sN ; b dispatch ; <bodies> ; dispatch: <tree> ; break:`;
* **shape** — a **complete** binary search tree (last level filled from the left:
  left size = `(full-1)/2 + min(n-full, (full+1)/2)`, `full` = largest `2^k-1 ≤ n`)
  of three-way nodes `beq key,K,case ; bgt key,K,R ; <left> ; R: <right>`; a
  one-value leaf is `beq key,K,case ; b default`; an empty right subtree is
  `bgt key,K,default`. Checked against all three trees: n = 13 (root 'n',
  subtrees 7/5, 3/3, 3/1, 1/1), n = 15 (`select_girl_main`, roots 64 / 6,99 /
  1,10,91,101 — its 7 internal nodes are exactly the 7 `beq ; slti` pairs at
  `0x8009325C`), n = 36 (`func_8009607C`, root 96 with 20/15, then 12/7, 7/4,
  2/1 ... — the pivot order its matched goto-tree C spells out by hand).

For a selected switch the hook (1) makes both `a1 < 0x5` tests read true, so no
cluster survives; (2) at `L4653e8` — instead of calling `func_4640e4` — assigns
every case label up front (5.2 assigns them lazily in the dispatch emitter),
emits `ujp Ldisp` and parks the records and the key temp; (3) at `L466a6c` (after
the body, before the break label) emits `ujp Lbreak ; lab Ldisp ; <tree>` and
frees the temp. Opcodes: `EQU 0x23`, `GRT 0x29`, `TJP 0x7f`, `UJP 0x88`,
`LAB 0x42`; key loads via `f_load_var`, constants via `f_LDC_integer`, exactly as
`func_4640e4` does. ugen deletes the `ujp Lbreak` when the last body cannot fall
out (as in every case so far), and at `-O1` the key temp lands in `$s0` (the
bodies contain calls), which is retail's register.

**Soundness.** Every case value is still tested with `==` against the same key
temp and the same case label; the tree only orders the tests. Unselected switches
take the untouched stock path.
A selected switch with a single case (or none) never reaches `L4653e8` and keeps
5.2's `equ ; fjp default` form: no retail evidence for that shape.

**A compiler fingerprint, used per switch.** Retail's library region is not one
compiler for every switch: `CD_read`'s three 2-case switches are 5.2's linear
chain, and `func_8008B93C` uses a jump table, both registered byte-perfect under
stock 5.2. So this is opt-in by index, never a blanket `=all` on a file with more
than the one switch you have checked against the retail bytes.

**Validation.**
1. Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` — `C functions: 1259`, `Matched:
   1259`, `Mismatched: 0`, `Output SHA1: 2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`,
   `Non-reloc fixups: 0` (both hooks installed).
2. Old cfe vs new cfe (old compiler dir holding real copies — the driver resolves
   passes through a symlink's target), each file under its own `@CompileEnv` /
   `@CompileFlags`: all `src/main/matched` + `src/main/semi-matched` IDO files and
   600 random `src/overlay/**` files (seed 67) — **1,893 / 1,894 byte-identical**
   (178 GCC files skipped); the one difference is `func_800B48D0`, the only file
   that sets the env (LIVE).
3. `=all` forced on every file (census — **never apply blindly**): 307 of 1,894
   objects change. Registered byte-perfect main functions: **181 change, 0 stay
   byte-perfect** (172 game region; library region: `CD_read`, `ev_fadeout`,
   `func_8008B93C`, `func_8008BB24`, `func_80092BDC`, `func_80094078`,
   `func_8009F99C`, `func_800A1414`, `func_800A24FC`). Open main semis that change
   (Body before → after, target): `col2mono` 52→55 (52), `iris_show` 671→687
   (671), `icon_cursor_move` 252→259 (252), `mascot_move` 238→249 (238),
   `func_80074E98` 84→85 (80), `func_80094660` 247→252 (245), `girl_data_print`
   188→204 (188), **`select_girl_main` 258→256 (257)** (the n = 15 retail tree —
   the next consumer to try), `title_data_read` 57→62 (55). Overlays (600-file
   sample): 117 change — 113 `byte_perfect`, 4 `asm_only` (`TACO/func_8015C6F8`,
   `TEL/func_80138778`, `RPG_BAT/func_80134EBC`, `TACO/func_80145D74`).
4. Probes: `sw.c` (13 cases, range 45) stock → `TABLE`; forced → the retail tree
   (`beq 110 ; bgt 110 ; beq 100 ; bgt 100 ; beq 88 ; bgt 88 ; beq 76 ; b def ;
   ...`) after the bodies. Sparse 4-case `sc.c` with calls: key in `$s0`, stock
   linear chain first; forced `b $36 ; bodies ; $36: tree`.

**First use: `func_800B48D0`** (see Hook 68 for the combined score).

## Hook 68: IDO52_CFE_BITFIELD_WORD — word-unit bitfield store (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_cfe_bitfield_hooks.py [--cfe PATH] [--rebuild]`.
Idempotent (marker `cfbf_word`); independent of Hook 67 (either order). Default OFF.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_CFE_BITFIELD_WORD` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N..]` — N = 0-based index over the TU's bitfield stores that reach `L47ad64` (the non-exact-fit ones), codegen order. A bare number is **refused** (warning, stock): it is not an on/off switch |
| `IDO52_CFE_BITFIELD_TRACE` | `1` — `[CFE-BFSTORE] #N size=<bits> off=<bit> unit=<byte\|half\|word\|wider> value=<const\|expr> -> STOCK\|KEPT WORD (and/or)\|KEPT WORD (xor)` |

**The decision.** Bitfield stores narrow in **cfe**, not ugen: `x.plus = 1` is
already `LOD/STR` of 1 byte (dtype `Ldt`, size 1) in the ucode. The site is
`func_47a970`, the bitfield-assignment codegen (its only caller is
`func_487850`, the store of an assignment — reads never reach it, which is why
reads stay word-wide `lw ; sll ; srl`). It chooses the RMW unit `s2` from the
width `w` (`sp+108`) and bit offset `off` (`a3`): exact fits first (a whole
aligned byte/half/word field), then at **`L47ad64`**:

```
if (off % 8 + w <= 8)        unit = (u)char    /* lbu ; andi ; ori ; sb */
else if (off % 16 + w <= 16) unit = (u)short   /* lhu ; ...        ; sh */
else if (off % 32 + w <= 32) unit = (u)int     /* L47ae7c */
```

and then, on the unit size (`at = t2 < 0x11`): a unit of ≤ 16 bits takes the
mask-and-or insert (`((v << p) & M) | (x & (M ^ 0xffff))`), a 32-bit unit the
xor insert `x ^ (((v ^ (x >> p)) & m) << p)`.

Retail (`0x800B4968` ff., `0x800B5090`): every insert is word-wide, with
`lw ; li at,~M ; and ; ori ; sw` for a **constant** value and the xor insert for
an **expression** (`f.haveprec = f.islong`). So the hook, for a selected store:

1. jumps from `L47ad64` straight to `L47ae7c` (the word test) — the unit is the
   32-bit word;
2. when the value is a constant, takes 5.2's own and/or path at the word
   (`at = (t2 < 0x11) || cfbf_andor`) and widens that path's `M ^ 0xffff` to
   `M ^ 0xffffffff` at its two sites (the Sdt and int forms) — at 16 bits the
   stock xor is `~M`; at 32 bits the stock value would clear the word's top half;
3. leaves an expression value on 5.2's word path, whose xor insert is already
   retail's.

"Constant" = the ucode cursor has not moved since the last `f_LDC_integer`
record (the hook records the cursor at that function's `return`): the value
pushed immediately before the store is an `LDC`. Anything else is `expr`, which
is always correct code (the xor form) — the only effect of a miss is shape.
The exact-fit branches are untouched; no retail evidence covers them.

**Non-constant inserts.** They fall out: no `andi ; andi ; or` survives at the
word. The `f.haveprec = f.islong = 1` chain compiles to
`and -65 ; or 64 ; sw ; sll 25 ; srl 31 ; srl 4 ; xor ; sll 31 ; srl 27 ; xor ;
sw`, retail's words at `0x800B5090`–`0x800B50C0`.

**Validation.**
1. Env unset: the Hook 67 build above (both hooks in the one cfe) — `Mismatched:
   0`, SHA1 `2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`.
2. Old vs new cfe: the same 1,893 / 1,894 byte-identical run (the single
   difference is `func_800B48D0`, which sets both envs).
3. `=all` forced (census; never blanket): 16 of 1,894 objects change — **15
   registered byte-perfect main functions, all game region, none survive**
   (Body before → after): `parameter_init` 140→145, `girl_rec_init` 199→206,
   `clut_gray_set` 122→134, `func_8004BAD8` 268→387, `func_8004C50C` 143→151,
   `magazine_flag_set` 162→187, `basyo_flag_set` 220→220 (162/220), `touzyou_basyo_check`
   195→213, `schedule_day_main` 998→1004, `hizuke_init` 290→302, `join_club_init`
   317→324, `touzyou_fan_disp` 1173→1186, `update_anim_state` 492→503,

   `TACO/func_801345EC` (byte_perfect). The game region narrows exactly as stock

4. Probes (`bf/g.c`, `b1.c`): stock `lbu ; and 0xfffd ; or 2 ; sb`; forced
   `lw ; and -3 ; or 2 ; sw` (`value=const`), and the xor form for

## Hook 69: IDO52_AS1_NO_ADDIU_COMBINE — veto as1's pass-II merge of two immediate adds on one register (2026-09-25)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_addiucombine_hooks.py [--as1 PATH] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/as1.new` and installs it with a rename). Idempotent
(marker `as1_addc_veto`); anchors are func_4171dc's own recomp labels `L417364` and
`L417610`, which no other applier touches, so it is order-independent among the as1
appliers (run it after the as1.c regen). Default OFF.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_NO_ADDIU_COMBINE` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` \| `occ:N[,N..]` — 0-based merge sites, one counter per as1 run (= per TU) |
| `IDO52_AS1_ADDIU_COMBINE_TRACE` | `1` — `[AS1-ADDIU] #N rX=<reg> k1=<K1> k2=<K2> (sum <K1+K2>) rec <i1>+<i2> -> MERGED\|VETOED`; `[AS1-ADDIU] re-offer rX= rec i1+i2 -> VETOED` for a re-offered vetoed pair |

**The decision.** as1's pass-II dispatcher func_419268 hands every `addiu`
(opcode 9) to **func_4171dc**. Starting at an `addiu rX,rX,K2` (dest == src) it
walks BACK through the block's record list to the previous record that writes rX.
If that is an `addiu rX,rY,K1` with the +34 "optimisable" byte set, K1+K2 fits
s16, and no record in between READS rX other than as a load/store base, it merges:
`f_change_bb(i1, rX, 3, 0)` turns the earlier add into `move rX,rY` (deleted
outright when rY == rX), every intervening `off(rX)` access is rebased by K1, and
the later add's imm16 becomes K1+K2. So `addiu a2,a2,2 ; ... ; addiu a2,a2,3` →
`addiu a2,a2,5` (one word fewer), and `addiu s2,s3,4 ; ... ; addiu s2,s2,4` →
`move s2,s3 ; ... ; addiu s2,s2,8` (count-neutral). The hook sits at the two points
where the merge is proven and nothing has been mutated yet — `L417364` when no
intervening `off(rX)` exists (straight to the merge at `L417830`), `L417610` when
the intervening accesses have all been range-checked and are about to be rebased.
A veto returns 0, func_4171dc's own "no change" exit: the words are exactly what
ugen emitted (`cc -S` shows the separate `addu $6,$6,K`); the sum is the same,
performed in two steps.

**Re-offers (built in).** func_419268 re-walks a block after any change, so a
vetoed pair is offered again (`initModelingData_init`: pair rec 1+2 offered twice; a plain
occurrence veto of `#0` was undone by the merge at `#1`). The hook remembers every
vetoed pair by (block record array, i1, i2, word1, word2) and vetoes its re-offers
without taking a new number, so occurrence numbers do not depend on the veto set.

**Why retail needs it.** Retail's library region was assembled by ASPSX, which has
no such peephole: `func_80091570` keeps `addiu a2,a2,2 ; addiu a2,a2,3` and
`addiu a2,a2,2` / `addiu a2,a2,4`. Konami game code and the overlays were
assembled by as1, which did merge, so this is NOT a blanket rule there — see the
census.

**Validation.**
1. Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` → `Matched: 1278`, `Mismatched: 0`,
   Output SHA1 `2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`.
2. Old (pre-hook, real-file copy of `out/`) vs new as1, every file in
   `src/main/matched` + `src/main/semi-matched` + 600 random overlay files, each
   under its own `@CompileEnv`/`@CompileFlags`: `same 1894, skip-gcc 176, DIFF 1` —
   the one DIFF is `func_80091570`, whose header opts in; with the token removed it
   is byte-identical.
3. `=all` forced on every file (census of what the hook would change — the
   never-blindly-apply list; `.text` words of the whole TU before → after):
   - main, registered byte-perfect: `initModelingData_init` (40 → 40, the count-neutral
     `move` form), `system_config_pad_mode` (769 → 770).
   - main semis: none move (the only opted-in one is `func_80091570`).
   - overlays (all 6,220 files): 23 change — GEKO/func_80142BCC 917→918,
     OPTION/func_80139514 (asm_only) 230→233, TACO/func_80134804 40→40,
     TACO/func_8014764C 165→167, and 19 TT files (func_80132810, 80133B3C,
     80136C6C, 80137A80, 80137C20, 80138300, 80138808, 80138E28, 8013BD4C,
     8013BD80, 8013CC3C, 801426E0, 80142730, 80144D60, 80145FF0, 80148DA0,
     80148DD4, 801498B8, 8014A2A4). Every one is `byte_perfect` today, so as1's merge
     is retail there. Never apply the hook to them.

**First use: `func_80091570`** (library semi, 283 words). Trace `#0 rX=6 k1=2 k2=3
(sum 5) rec 17+30`, `#1 rX=6 k1=2 k2=4 (sum 6) rec 7+24`; `=all` vetoes both.
Body 281 → **283**, Exact 116/283 → 251/283, the `Fixup 1` line gone. With the count
right, `TIE_REJECT += 528,531,533` put the `move a1,t4` filler back before the `jal`
(255/283), and the remaining 28 words were the two pure reorders the E57 note
predicted (the rem guard vs `mfhi`/half macro, the deferred divu#4 guard), bridged
by 25 `swap_instructions` with a permutation-aware reaching-definition audit (0
data-producer changes, 4 trap-diamond join labels, negative control +1 detected).
**283/283 BYTE-PERFECT**, no Fixup line, `audit_reloc_positions` OK. Not yet
registered.

## Hook 70: IDO52_CFE_FRAME_TOP_BASE — a subscripted local array's base at the frame top (2026-09-26)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_cfe_frametop_hooks.py [--cfe PATH] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/cfe.new` and installs it with a rename). Idempotent
(marker `cfftb_arm`); independent of Hooks 67/68 (all three patch `cfe.c`, at disjoint
functions and labels: 67 in `func_4648f8`/`func_465444`, 68 in `func_47a970`/
`f_LDC_integer`, 70 in `f_UWRITE`/`f_expr_cg`/`f_load_addr`). Default OFF: with the env
unset no record is touched. Built from run E65's spec (the section below).

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_CFE_FRAME_TOP_BASE` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` — 0-based candidate site in the TU (every subscript whose base is an auto array counts, codegen order; a constant index `buf[0]` counts too) |
| `IDO52_CFE_FRAME_TOP_TRACE` | `1` — `[CFE-FTB] #N array=<name> off=<frame off> -> STOCK\|FRAME-TOP`, one line per candidate |

**The site.** `f_expr_cg`'s IXA case (L481668 ff.) evaluates the subscript's base
operand (node+24) with a recursive `f_expr_cg` — first (`L481734`), or second after
the index (`L481708`, the Sethi-Ullman swapped form that then emits `0x7e` SWP) — and
then emits `IXA`. A local array base comes out of that recursion through
`func_486a64 -> f_load_addr`, whose plain path (`L48a974`) writes the 24-byte LDA
record into the ucode buffer (`0x1002e620`, cursor `0x1002f620`): `+1` mtype<<5,
`+4` block, `+8` length, `+12` offset, `+16` offset2 (offset == offset2 == the
array's byte offset from the frame top, negative for an auto).

The hook ARMS before the base evaluation (records the cursor and an `f_UWRITE` flush
counter) and DISARMS when it returns. In `f_load_addr` a record is a candidate only
when it is the FIRST record written since the arm (same cursor, no flush in between —
so `s.b[i]`, `p->a[i]`, `bb[i][j]`'s outer subscript never qualify: their first
record is not the array's own LDA), the variable is an auto (the record's mtype is 1 — `f_U_MT` of storage class 1)
of ARRAY type (kind `0x13` after stripping a qualified wrapper, kind `0x18` with bits
`0xd0000000`, as `f_load_addr` does), and offset == offset2 < 0. For a selected
candidate the record's offset (`+12`) is written 0 — `offset2` keeps the array — and
at `f_load_addr`'s exit (`L48aae4`, the record committed and possibly flushed) the
hook emits, through cfe's own emitters,

```
LDC  J, 4, <array offset>    ; f_LDC_integer(6, 4, hi, lo) -- the call cfe makes for a struct member offset
ADD  A                       ; opcode 0x01, dtype Adt (0), as cfe's struct-member ADD
```

so uopt receives `LDA(M,0 ; offset2 = array) ; LDC off ; ADD ; <index> ; IXA`. Stock
uopt folds the constant into the ILOD/ISTR displacement (its struct-member rule), the
frame-top LDAs are one leaf, and ugen emits `addiu tN,sp,<framesize> ; l* -K(tN)`.
No record is edited after a flush; the only in-place edit is the LDA's own offset
field, before the record is committed.

⚠️ **The ADD's dtype must be written.** `f_load_addr` never sets the LDA's dtype bits
(they are stale buffer bytes), so copying them onto the ADD gave `Pdt`/`Ldt` ADDs and
uopt aborted in `f_genloadnum` (`uopt: Internal: <func>: code: 6320`, dbgerror 0x18b0)
as soon as strength reduction materialised the sum (a two-IV loop,
`for(i..){clr[i]=0; a[i]=a[i]+1; if(a[i]>=20U){}}`; 4 of 1,894 corpus files on the
first `=all` run). Fixed before any validation below.

**Soundness (probes `al.c`, `sw.c`, stock vs `=all` vs mixed subsets).** uopt's memory
disambiguation keys the rewritten LDA on `offset2`: (a) a frame-top load of `buf[i]`
is CSE'd across a store to a DIFFERENT array (`buf2[j] = x`) exactly as stock does,
and reloaded after a store to the SAME array (`buf[j] = x+1`), with both arrays at
their own distinct offsets (`-64` / `-128` off the one `addiu a1,sp,168` base); (b)
mixed forms — hooked store with stock loads and vice versa, selected per site — keep
every load after a possibly-aliasing store (`fb`, `fc` with `6,8,10` and `9`, `fe`
with `19` / `20`, `ff` with `21,25,26`); (c) a store through an escaped pointer
(`q = buf; q[k] = x`) forces the reload; (d) index shapes `buf[i+1]` (`sb -63(a0)`),
`buf[p->n]`, `bb[i][j]` / `bb[j][i]` (one inner base per access, 16-byte row stride,
store-to-load forwarding only for the identical address), `buf[h(i)]` (the swapped
path) and constant indices all give the stock semantics. **A stock 5.2 anomaly, not
the hook:** `y = buf[0]; buf[i] = x; z = buf[0];` reuses the first `buf[0]` load (no
reload after the indexed store) under the stock compiler and under the hook alike —
uopt treats the constant-index element as a scalar the IXA store does not kill.

**A fingerprint, used per file.** Retail has exactly two sites of the frame-top form,
both library (`func_8008E0E4`, `func_8008C114`), and every byte-perfect registered
main function with an auto-array subscript uses 5.2's stock form — including one
library function, `holiday_normal_init0`. Never blanket.

**Validation.**
1. Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` → `C functions: 1287`, `Matched: 1287`,
   `Mismatched: 0`, `Output SHA1: 2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`,
   `Non-reloc fixups: 0`.
2. Old cfe (pre-hook real-file copy of `out/`, Hooks 67/68 installed) vs new, every
   file under its own `@CompileEnv`/`@CompileFlags`: all `src/main/matched` +
   `src/main/semi-matched` + 600 random overlay files (seed 70) — `same 1894,
   skip-gcc 177`, **0 DIFF, 0 fail**.
3. `=all` forced (census — the never-blindly-apply list): 49 of 1,894 objects change,
   0 compile failures. Registered byte-perfect main functions: **37 change, none stays
   byte-perfect with Fixup 0** (36 game region + library `holiday_normal_init0`; Body
   before → after, target): `Hw_Start` 186→180, `file_select_copy` 729→729 (Fixup 8),
   `file_select_delete4` 111→111 (96/111), `vcount_meter_disp` 177→177 (Fixup 9),

**Consumers.**

| `func_8008C114` | Body 309, 133/309, Fixup 7 | Body 309, 133/309, Fixup 9 | Body 309, 133/309, **Fixup 0** (wall 6 closed) |

`func_8008E0E4` recipe: Hook 70 `=all`; `u8 pad[8];` declared first (native 368 frame,
buffers at retail's sp+232/sp+104 — no `patch_stack_complete`); the three table

44,399 TIE_TAKE=395,403` (→ 202); `remap_stack_slots --keep-frame` for the save slots;
