## Hook 71: IDO52_UGEN_NO_MOVE_NOALIAS — veto the `.noalias` hint around a block move (2026-09-26)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_ugen_movenoalias_hooks.py [--ugen PATH] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/ugen.new` and installs it with a rename). Independent
anchors (helper on `f_eval_mov`'s own definition, site on the area-compare block after
recomp label `L428df4`, checked to lie inside `f_eval_mov`), idempotent (marker
`ugen_mnoalias_veto`). Commutes with Hook 66 (same function, label `L428bb8`); run it
with the other ugen appliers, after `apply_ugen_moveloop_hooks.py`. Default OFF: with
the env unset the stock hint is emitted everywhere.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_UGEN_NO_MOVE_NOALIAS` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N..]` \| `N[,N..]` — 0-based index over the block moves in the TU that WOULD emit the hint (both addresses LDA, different areas), ugen emission order |
| `IDO52_UGEN_NO_MOVE_NOALIAS_TRACE` | `1` — `[UGEN-MOVENOALIAS] occ=<n> size=<bytes> dst=r<d> src=r<s> -> VETO\|KEEP`, one line per candidate |

**The decision.** Before the unrolled `lw/sw` tail of a block move (struct assignment,
by-value struct argument), ugen's **`f_eval_mov`** tests the two address trees: if
both are `LDA` nodes (opcode `0x47`) and their area/block words (`+36`) differ, it
sets a flag (`sp+242`) and calls `f_emit_alias(0x30, src_reg, dst_reg)` — binasm
`.noalias` — and at `L429310`, after the copy, `f_emit_alias(0x31, ...)` (`.alias`)
re-arms the dependence. With the hint, as1's list scheduler may hoist the source loads
over the destination stores (`lw t2,4 ; lw at,8 ; sw t2,4 ; sw at,8`); without it,
the memory order is kept and the load delays are filled with nops (`lw t2,4 ; nop ;
sw t2,4 ; lw at,8 ; nop ; sw at,8`). The hook turns `if (src_blk == dst_blk)` into
`if (src_blk == dst_blk || veto(occ))`: a vetoed occurrence takes **exactly** the path
the stock compiler takes for a same-area move — neither directive is emitted, the
flag stays 0. Soundness: dropping a no-alias hint only ADDS a dependence edge to the
scheduler; every word is still ugen's and as1's.

**Retail evidence.** The library band's only unrolled block copy (0x800B4924, a
12-byte zero template `D_800E8D40` -> a stack local inside the doprnt formatter
`func_800B48D0`) is the serial `lw ; nop ; sw ; lw at ; nop ; sw at` shape — as1's
schedule WITHOUT the hint. The game region and overlays keep the hint (see census:
every byte-perfect user of it breaks at `=all`). A third library-band ugen
fingerprint after Hooks 66-68; per-file only, **never a default**.

**Validation.**
1. Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` — `Mismatched: 0`, `Output SHA1:
   2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`, `Non-reloc fixups: 0`;
   `verify_overlay_bytes.py DATE` 670/670 and `TT` 301/301 effective before and
   after the rebuild.
2. Old ugen vs new ugen, env unset, every file under its own `@CompileEnv` /
   `@CompileFlags`: all 1,148 registered IDO main files (1,130 `ido52` + 18
   `ido52-gas`, the latter new-vs-new only), 143 `src/main/semi-matched` IDO files,
   all 670 DATE + 301 TT files — **0 differ** (171 + 5 GCC files skipped).
3. `=all` census (with `_TRACE`): 32 TUs have a hint-emitting move, **12 objects
   change, all byte-perfect at stock, none in the library band**:
   `main/_sprite_set_box_shade` (0x800553B0, 2 occ) and DATE `func_8013C12C`,
   `func_80144D98` (9 occ), `func_801504A0`, `func_801518B8`, `func_80152060`,
   `func_801560C8`, `func_80156428`, `func_80157278`, `func_8015906C`,
   `func_8015919C`, `func_8015A57C`. 20 more TUs (main `card_file_write`,
   `msg_str_disp`; 18 DATE) have occurrences whose schedule is unchanged. TT has
   none; no registered library-band function and no open main semi has one.
4. Micro-check on the first user: one candidate, `occ=0 size=12 dst=r8 src=r9`.

**First use: `func_800B48D0`** (libc doprnt formatter, 862 words + 2 alignment nops,
`@SymbolSize: 3456` — the recorded 3460 ran one word into `func_800B5650`). E108 had
859/862 aligned with the template copy the sole residual. With
`IDO52_UGEN_NO_MOVE_NOALIAS=occ:0` the copy comes out serial; removing the two
directives also removes 4 list-scheduler ties in that block, so the
`IDO52_AS1_TIE_*` lists were re-derived from `IDO52_AS1_TIE_TRACE` (TAKE `#46` — the
copy's own load order — dropped, every later index −4: TAKE `8,15,21,193`, REJECT
`165,215,78,100`); PASS1_PROP / FTF / Hook 36 indices trace-diffed unchanged.
**864/864 BYTE-PERFECT, Body 862, no Fixup line**, `audit_main_relocs` and
`audit_reloc_positions` OK.

## Frame-top base (`addiu tN,sp,<framesize>` + negative displacements): located (2026-09-25, run E65) — BUILT as Hook 70 above (2026-09-26, run E68)

**Status (E65, superseded: see Hook 70): diagnosed; no hook built.** Blocks `func_8008E0E4` (the last word
of E60's rewrite) and wall (6) of `func_8008C114`. These are the only two
retail functions with `addiu rX,sp,<framesize>`; the only two retail uses of
5.2's stock `addu rX,sp,rIdx` form are in the game region (`func_80050828`,
`file_select_copy`, both byte-perfect), so this is a library-region compiler
fingerprint, like Hook 67.

**Where ours comes from (ugen).** `f_eval`, ILOD/ISLD case (`L42eb94`): when
the address is `ADD(x, LDA)` with both used once, `LDA` of a local (mtype
1/2) and pic < 2, it takes `L42ed44`: `addu r,sp,x ; l* (frame_offset(lda) +
ilod.off)(r)` (sp FIRST). Any bail-out (`L42eec4`, generic path) gives
`addiu t,sp,fs+off ; addu r,x,t ; l* ilod.off(r)` — retail's operand order,
but based at the array. `f_eval`'s LDA case always emits `sp +
frame_offset(node)`; no 5.2 path materialises `LDA(M,0)` = sp+framesize. The
frame-pointer mode (flag `0x10019080`, forced in a scratch ugen) is
sp-bottom based. There is **no branch or constant in ugen** that selects
retail's form. ugen's own tree dumps: pass any `-d<x>` to ugen by hand
(`ugen ... -da in.O ...` prints "Tree dump after Build/Translate/...").

**What retail is (front end).** Retail = what **stock** 5.2 uopt + ugen + as1
emit when the indexed local-array base reaches uopt as

```
LDA  M, offset 0, offset2 = <array base>, length = <array size>
LDC  <array offset>          ; negative M offset
ADD
<index> ; IXA ; ILOD/ISTR
```

instead of cfe's `LDA M, offset <array offset>`. uopt then folds the constant
into the ILOD/ISTR offset (its struct-member rule: `s.b[i]` is `LDA(s) + LDC
132 + ADD`, and the 132 lands in the ILOD), the frame-top LDAs are one shared
leaf, and ugen evaluates it once as `addiu t7,sp,<fs>`. `func_8008C114` proves
the split must be upstream of uopt: retail carries `-1048` across a uopt CSE
temp shared by two arms and into a strength-reduced pointer IV initialised to
sp+1112 — neither is reachable from ugen. (uopt does NOT derive it from
`offset2`: an LDA with `offset2 = 0` and a non-zero offset passes through
unchanged.)

**Prototype (scratch only).** A wrapper `uopt` that rewrites its input ucode
(`LDA M off==off2<0 ; LOD(4 words) ; IXA` → `LDA(0, off2 kept) ; LDC off ;
ADD`; LDC = `49060000 0 4 0 <val> 0`, ADD = `01200000 00000003`, copied from
cfe's own struct-member output):

| function | before | prototype |
|---|---|---|
| `func_8008E0E4`, E60's rewrite (+ `IDO52_LDA_NOINREG_CLASSES=1`) | Body 206, 67/207, Fixup 4 | **Body 207, 179/207, Fixup 4**; loop head word-for-word retail (`addiu t7,sp,360 ; addu t8,s0,t7 ; lbu -128(t8) ; ... ; addu t0,s0,t7 ; lbu -256(t0)`, latch-slot `addiu t7`), frame 360 vs 368 |
| `func_8008C114` (shipped source) | Body 309, 133/309, Fixup 7 | Body 309, 133/309, Fixup 9; every `buf` access in retail's shape, at `-1060` vs `-1048` (layout: 12 more bytes of locals above `buf`) |

Without `LDA_NOINREG` uopt holds the frame-top LDA in a saved register
(`addu $18,$sp,368` in the preheader, Body 209).

**What to build (Hook 70 spec).** `IDO52_CFE_FRAME_TOP_BASE=all|N,...` in
**cfe**, at the ucode emission of an array-subscript base that is a local
(mtype M) array: emit the three-record form above. cfe buffers ucode at
`0x1002e620` (cursor `0x1002f620`, flushed by `f_UWRITE` past 0x800 bytes)
and its per-opcode record length table is at `0x100087d0 + opc*16 + 4`; a
record-stream rewrite must use that table (not a word scan, which can match
an LDC value) and must not split a pattern across a flush. Open before
shipping: (1) soundness — `offset` now lies outside `[offset2,
offset2+length)`; confirm uopt's alias test keys on `offset2`/length (the
struct case already produces `offset != offset2`) or give `ILOD/ISTR` a
negative-control probe (`buf[i] = x; y = buf[0];`); (2) the usual env-unset
SHA1 / old-vs-new corpus / `=all` census; (3) index shapes other than a
plain `LOD` (`buf[i+1]`, `buf[p->n]`).

## Hook 72: IDO52_AS1_KEEP_IDENTICAL_REWRITE — veto as1 pass I's deletion of a same-block identical rewrite (2026-09-26)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_identrewrite_hooks.py [--as1 PATH] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/as1.new` and installs it with a rename). Idempotent
(marker `as1_kir_veto`); anchors are func_418fb8's own definition and its recomp label
`L419180`, which no other applier touches, so it is order-independent among the as1
appliers — run it after them, after the as1.c regen. Default OFF.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_IDENTICAL_REWRITE` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` \| `occ:N[,N..]` — 0-based deletion candidates, one counter per as1 run (= per TU) |
| `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_IDENTICAL_REWRITE_TRACE` | `1` — `[AS1-KIR] #N blk=<ord>@<ptr> rec=<i> prev=<j> reg=<r> word=<insn> -> DELETED\|KEPT`; `[AS1-KIR] re-offer blk= rec= word= -> KEPT` for a re-offered kept record. `<ord>` is the ordinal of the distinct block records that produced a candidate, not a uopt bb number |

**The decision.** as1's pass-I driver `f_do_passI_opt` (func_419268) calls
**func_418fb8** for every record of a basic block, after func_414bf8's copy
substitution. func_418fb8 keeps a per-block table (`0x10022248[reg]` = index of the
register's last write; cleared by func_418f7c at each block start). When the current
record's destination already has a def in the block whose word is IDENTICAL, neither
record carries a relocation (`+4 == 0`), the word is not a load
(`f_loads_from_memory`), no source register has been rewritten since (the table
entries of `+29..+31` are older than the previous def), and the word is not an
`mtc1` (COP1 rs=4) nor an `mfhi`/`mflo` (SPECIAL funct 0x10/0x12), it DELETES the record
(`f_change_bb(idx, 0, 5, 0)`, label `L419180`). So `sll t8,v0,2 ; … ; sll t8,v0,2`
keeps only the first. The hook sits at `L419180`, where the deletion is proven and
nothing has been mutated; a veto jumps to `L4191dc`, func_418fb8's own "not redundant"
path, which records the kept word as the register's latest def — what as1 does for
any non-deletable write. Nothing is created: the words are the ones ugen emitted
(`cc -S` shows them).

**Re-offers (built in).** func_419268 ORs the result into its change flag and may
re-walk a block; every kept record is remembered by (block record, record index,
word) and a re-offer is kept without taking a new occurrence number, so numbering is
independent of the veto set (same discipline as Hook 69). No re-offer was observed in
any census file.

**Why retail needs it.** Retail's library region was assembled by ASPSX, which has no
such pass: `func_800A1E40` (libgpu `_addque2`) recomputes `sll t8,v0,2` in each of
unrolled copies 2–4. The IRIX as1 binary deletes them (E102 under qemu-irix), and so did
Konami's toolchain for game code and overlays — see the census. Per-file only; never a
default.

**Validation.**
1. Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` → `Matched: 1320`, `Mismatched: 0`,
   Output SHA1 `2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b` (identical before/after).
   `verify_overlay_bytes.py DATE` 670/670 effective before and after; `TT` 301/301
   effective before and after (its BP/reloc split moved during the run because another
   worker was converting TT `asm_only` files at the same time — the corpus check below
   shows the as1 change is not the cause).
2. Old (pre-hook real-file copy of `out/`) vs new as1, env unset, every file in
   `src/main/matched` + `src/main/semi-matched` + `src/overlay/DATE` + `src/overlay/TT`
   under its own `@CompileEnv`/`@CompileFlags`: `same 2246, DIFF 0, skip 194`
   (gcc / ido52-gas / ido52-aspsx TUs, which do not run as1).
3. `=all` forced on the same set (the never-blindly-apply list; body words before →
   after, with the trace):
   - `func_800A1E40` 265 → 268 in its pre-hook spelling — the opt-in (`#0-#2`, three
     `sll t8,v0,2`).
   - `func_800429B8` 17 → 17, `func_80042AB8` 14 → 14 (second `move v0,zero` kept, a
     different delay fill), `mod_set_gpu` 60 → 60 (two `move v0,tN` repeats),
     `warm_reset_init` 389 → 392 (three `negu at,tN` repeats), `DATE/func_80137E6C`
     113 → 113, `TT/func_8013E68C` 101 → 109 (eight `addiu at,s0,32767` repeats).
     All are byte-perfect today, so as1's deletion is retail there. Never apply the
     hook to them.

**First use: `func_800A1E40`** (library, 265 words). E102's source was count-short
by exactly these three words (Body 262, 164/264). With `=all`: Body 265, 249/265. The
three new records renumber as1's tie-breaks after them, so the TIE lists were
re-derived by matching the old decisions' `(bb, cand, best)` words in
`IDO52_AS1_TIE_TRACE`: `TIE_TAKE=186,224 TIE_REJECT=187` →
`TIE_TAKE=220,258 TIE_REJECT=221`. The uopt/ugen tokens (bitposes, bbs, the mpy
range) were unaffected — the hook acts in as1 only. **265/265 BYTE-PERFECT, no Fixup
line**, `audit_main_relocs` OK, `audit_reloc_positions` OK. Moved to
`src/main/matched/`; not yet registered.

## Hook 73: IDO52_NO_ENTRY_HOME_STORE — veto uopt's entry home store of a register-passed formal (2026-09-26)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_entryhome_hooks.py [build/5.2/uopt.c] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/uopt.new` and installs it with a rename). Idempotent
(marker `ido52_h73_entryhome`); anchors are f_prolog's own definition and its recomp
labels `L4201d4` (entry) and `L421140` (the single-word home `rstr`), which no other
applier touches, so it is order-independent among the uopt appliers — run it after the
uopt.c regen. Default OFF; there is no default value and none should ever be added.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_NO_ENTRY_HOME_STORE` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N..]` (bare `N[,N..]` also accepted) — **1-based** ordinal of the entry-home `rstr` at `L421140`, one counter per uopt run (= per TU), procedure order then formal order |
| `IDO52_NO_ENTRY_HOME_STORE_TRACE` | `1` — `[ENTRY-HOME] occ=N proc=P rstr $R -> blk=B off=O size=S : KEEP\|VETO` for every candidate (`$R` = the incoming machine register, `off` = offset in the formal's parameter memory; `sw $R,framesize+off($sp)` in the object) |

**Where the store comes from.** The wall filed as `entry-home-store` on
`func_80095620`: our 5.2 emits `sw a0,N(sp)` right after the prologue for an
ADDRESS-TAKEN formal even when that value is dead on every path (E97's probe
`unsigned f(unsigned s){ s = (s+8)&~7; g(&s); return s; }`). A uwrite trace of uopt's
OUTPUT U-code (a temporary `fprintf` in `f_uwrite`) shows the store is an `rstr`
(opcode 112, lexlev = machine register 4, memory = `s`'s parameter home) written by
**uopt's `f_prolog`**, not by ugen: for each formal, f_prolog emits
`rstr $aN -> home` when the parameter-table entry (`0x1001cbd8`, 16 bytes per word
slot) has its flag byte `+3` set — the formal's memory is referenced (here: its address
escapes) — or when the formal got no register at all; single-word formals take
`L421140`, multi-word formals the split path at `L42115c` (never touched). ugen
translates the `rstr` 1:1; its own `f_home_parameters` only homes varargs/flagged
registers and is not involved (E-note of 2026-09-02, confirmed).

**Why it is an emission veto and not a dead-store pass.** f_prolog runs at procedure
WRITE-OUT, after every optimisation pass, so the `rstr` never exists in any
dataflow uopt analyses — no dead-store pass can see it. The two routes the task
proposed ("veto the emission" / "treat the initial home write as dead when
overwritten before any read") are therefore the same site; with the veto the ugen
stream for the probe is exactly the unhooked one minus the `sw $4,24($sp)` (the
`move $5,$4`, the pre-call `rstr $5`, the post-call `rlod` are unchanged), and on
`func_80095620` it is retail's multiset.

**Soundness condition (asserted by the user when naming the occurrence; the hook does
not check it).** Every path from procedure entry to any READ of the formal's home — a
direct reload, or a call / indirect access that can reach it through the escaped
address — must pass a WRITE of the home first. Then the entry store is dead and
deleting it is ordinary dead-store elimination: no word is created or rewritten, the
compiler's own store is simply not emitted. If the incoming value itself must be in
memory on some path (`g(&s)` before any assignment of `s`), the veto MISCOMPILES.
Retail's object is the proof on each use: on `func_80095620` retail's first store to
`sp+24` is the value written in each arm of the rounding `if`, and the early-exit path
reads nothing.

**Validation.**
1. Env unset: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` → `Matched: 1321`, `Mismatched: 0`,
   Output SHA1 `2d8d474816e4bf937b09dc3b017f550f7ce4e14b`, non-reloc fixups 0.
   `verify_overlay_bytes.py DATE` 670/670 effective (9 BP + 661 reloc-only) and `TT`
   298/298 (40 + 258) with the pristine uopt and with the hooked uopt — identical.
2. Pristine uopt (pre-hook copy of `out/`) vs hooked uopt, env unset, every file in
   `src/main/matched` + `src/main/semi-matched` + `src/overlay/DATE` +
   `src/overlay/TT` under its own `@CompileEnv`/`@CompileFlags`: `same 2243, DIFF 0,
   skip 194` (gcc / ido52-gas / ido52-aspsx TUs skipped by the census script; with the
   env unset the patch adds only a counter and a `getenv`).
3. `=all` forced on the same set: **249 objects change** — 212 in `src/main/matched`,
   11 in `src/main/semi-matched`, 4 in DATE (`func_8014EF08`, `func_80152684`,
   `func_80152E34`, `func_8014AD0C`), 22 in TT. Every matched/DATE/TT one is
   byte-perfect today, so there the entry home store IS retail (e.g. `CdInit`,
   `CdMix`, `week_day_exit`, `vacation_day_init0`) — the store is the normal
   behaviour of both compilers and the veto is a per-file, per-occurrence exception.
   Never apply it blindly and never as a default.

**First use: `func_80095620`** (library, 29 words, filed `entry-home-store` since
2026-09-02, 13/29 count-exact). `IDO52_NO_ENTRY_HOME_STORE=occ:1` removes the entry
`sw a0,24(sp)` (Body 28). The two residuals the filing predicted then become reachable:
`IDO52_UGEN_FORCE_TARGET_SAFE=all` (Hook 41b) gives retail's in-place
`and a0,a0,at ; sw a0,24(sp)` in the rounding arm, and `IDO52_AS1_NO_LA_PULL=all`
(Hook 21) keeps `li a1,43` beside the call so the `bnez t6` delay slot stays empty as
retail (Body 29). The existing `IDO52_AS1_NO_COPY_PROP=1` stays load-bearing (28
words without it). **29/29 BYTE-PERFECT, Body 29, no Fixup line**,
`audit_main_relocs` OK, `audit_reloc_positions` OK. Moved to `src/main/matched/`; not
yet registered; its `c_unmatchable.txt` entry (`entry-home-store`) is now stale.

## Hook 74: IDO52_NO_FINDINDUCT_STRIDE_OCC — per-occurrence (per-loop) stride-creation veto, the keyed form of Hook 13 (2026-09-26)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_uopt_findinduct_occ_hooks.py [build/5.2/uopt.c] [--rebuild]`
(`--rebuild` compiles to `out/uopt.new` and installs it with a rename). Idempotent
(marker `ido52_h74_findinduct_occ`). Anchors are f_findinduct's own text: its
definition line, its entry label `L432e00:`/`//findinduct:`, the candidate-confirmed
test at `L433774:` (`if ((int32_t)t3 == (int32_t)0) goto L433834;`) and the raw
stride-creation statement `v0 = func_431cfc(mem, sp, v0, a0);` — the only call of
func_431cfc in uopt.c. Hook 13 (`apply_uopt_hooks.py`) anchors on that same statement
and wraps it in `if (ido52_no_findinduct_stride) { v0 = 0; } else { <stmt> }`; this
applier wraps the statement in its own guard and keeps the raw line intact, so **either
applier may run first** (both orders verified: Hook 13's `find_line` still finds its
anchor after Hook 74, and the reverse-order object compiles; the live chain is Hook 13
first). Order-independent among the uopt appliers; run after the uopt.c regen. Default
OFF; there is no default value and none should ever be added. Hook 13's global behaviour
is unchanged.

| Env | Values |
|---|---|
| `IDO52_NO_FINDINDUCT_STRIDE_OCC` | comma list of `all` \| `N` / `occ:N` \| `loop:N` \| `bb:N` |
| `IDO52_NO_FINDINDUCT_STRIDE_TRACE` | `1` — `[FINDINDUCT] occ=N loop=L proc=P bb=B var=V : KEEP\|VETO[ H13]` per candidate, then `[FINDINDUCT]   occ=N -> STRIDE\|none` for every kept one |

**The site.** f_findinduct walks the procedure's block list (`0x1001c898`, node+12 =
next); for each loop-header block `fp` it walks the loop's induction-candidate bitvector
(`fp+320`) one variable index `s5` at a time and, for each set bit, calls
`func_431cfc(record of var s5)` — stride creation for that (loop, variable). A zero
return clears the candidate bit: the variable is not strength-reduced in that loop,
which is exactly what Hook 13 forces for every candidate in the TU. The keys:

- `occ:N` — 1-based ordinal of the candidate reaching the site, per uopt run (= per
  TU): procedure order, then block-list order, then variable index. **A per-VARIABLE
  veto**, not only per loop — that is what closed TAIIKU (below).
- `loop:N` — every candidate of the N-th (procedure, header block) pair reaching the
  site (the ordinal increments when the pair changes).
- `bb:N` — every candidate whose header block number (`MEM_U16(node+8)`, the same
  number Hooks 20/24/59 print) is N, in every procedure of the TU (block numbers restart
  per procedure). Stable across edits that do not change the block structure; `occ`
  numbers shift on ANY source edit — re-trace after every change.
- The trace and the numbering are evaluated at the candidate test, BEFORE either guard,
  so they are identical with or without Hook 13 (the `H13` tag says Hook 13 is set and
  will veto everything regardless). The `-> STRIDE|none` line tells which candidates
  are real strength-reduction decisions; vetoing a `none` changes nothing.

Vetoing emits the loop as retail does when it did not strength-reduce that variable:
the index/address is recomputed per pass. It never creates or rewrites a word.

**Validation (2026-09-26, run E137).** 30 overlay TUs with loops (28 random + the two
below), each under its own `@CompileFlags`/`@CompileEnv`, pristine `out/` vs rebuilt:
(a) env unset → 30/30 objects `cmp`-identical; (b) `IDO52_NO_FINDINDUCT_STRIDE_OCC=all`
on the rebuilt uopt vs `IDO52_NO_FINDINDUCT_STRIDE=1` on the pristine one → 30/30
identical (14 of the 30 are Hook-13-live, so the equivalence is not vacuous); the
pristine driver prints 0 trace lines under the same env (it really runs its own uopt);
(c) second run prints `Already applied`, file unchanged; (d) chain placement: `apply_all_hooks.py`,
after `apply_uopt_entryhome_hooks.py`.

**First close: TAIIKU/func_80139DB8 — BYTE-PERFECT 203/203, zero PostProc**
(`test_overlay_match` ✅ TRUE WIN; resolver 203/203, len t=812 c=812, 0 unresolved;
`audit_raw_count` PASS delta=0; `audit_paint_bans` CLEAN). E135's wall was exactly the
per-loop question: retail strength-reduces the three init loops (pointer IVs, exit test
LFTR'd onto the last array) and NOT the 20x4 corner-table grid; the global Hook 13 fixed
the grid and broke the loops. `IDO52_NO_FINDINDUCT_STRIDE_OCC=bb:15,bb:18` (the grid's
outer/inner blocks from the trace) does the grid alone. Two more findings on the way:
- **`occ:` is per variable.** With a `k = i + 1` local, uopt created an `i+1` derived
  IV in each of the three init loops (var 202 in every loop: `addiu s7,zero,1 …
  slti at,s7,4` beside the pointer IVs); vetoing exactly that variable in each loop
  (`occ:58,occ:120,occ:182`) gave retail's loops while keeping their pointer strides.
  Spelling the E54/EF4 rows as `((i + 1) << 2) + 80` inline removed the local, the
  spurious IV and its vetoes at once.
- **The frame is a local-count fact, not a hook.** Retail's 96-byte frame = pic1 (16) +
  i/j (8) + one spill slot at sp+80 for the compiler temp `i + 1`; a declared `k` costs
  a third slot (104 under pic1, 88 under pic0). The `<< 2` form is what stops uopt
  reassociating `(i + 1) * 4 + 80` into `i*4 + 84` (which homes `i` in memory
  instead of spilling `i+1`).

**Second consumer: TT/func_8013D988 — NOT closed, wall re-classified.** Retail keeps
`kk` in a register and recomputes `(kk + hi) & 1023` per cell; ours folded kk into a
`kk+hi` derived IV. `occ:<kk's candidate in nest 2's inner block>` (occ:259 on scratch
`e137/tt10b.c`) reproduces retail's nest-2 structure (kept kk, per-cell `addu ; andi`,
both mesh cursors, the dead `move s2,zero ; lw s2,hi`, eight `lh` batched before eight
`sh`) at 288/290 words with nest 1 untouched. What remains is not stride-related: the two
y0/y1 web-home copies (Hook 32 `lod:3:deny` / `lod:4:deny` each restore one; they do not
stack — q renumbers), an s-reg colour permutation (45→s8 in retail; ours frees fp and
hoists the `ay+544` end pointer retail rematerialises), the t-reg permutation, and the
stack-slot map (+24 bytes: the eight corner locals cost 32 and retail's 12-before /
rest-after-the-arrays declaration order is not yet reproduced). Details in the file's
E137 note.

⚠️ Two traps for the next user: the veto is evaluated per uopt RUN, so a TU with several
procedures shares one `occ` counter (use `bb:`/`loop:` with `proc=` from the trace to
disambiguate); and a `bb:` veto on a loop with several candidates kills ALL of them —
when retail kept some IVs of a loop and dropped others, use `occ:` per variable.


## Hook 75: IDO52_AS1_PASS1_VOLATILE_OK — offer a `.set volatile` record to as1 pass-I copy substitution (2026-10-01)

Applier: `python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_pass1vol_hooks.py --rebuild`
(idempotent; its only anchor is recomp label `L419910` in `f_do_passI_opt`;
independent of every other applier, including Hook 44 in the same function).
Add it to the post-`make clean` chain with the other as1 appliers.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_PASS1_VOLATILE_OK` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` \| `occ:N[,N..]` (0-based) | offer the listed volatile records to `func_414bf8` as if record byte +33 (the `.set volatile` flag, global `0x1002f8f8`) were clear |
| `IDO52_AS1_PASS1_VOLATILE_OK_TRACE` | `1` | `[AS1-P1V] #N rec=<ptr> word=<insn> -> OFFERED\|SKIPPED` per arrival; re-offers of an offered record keep their verdict without a new number |

**Mechanism.** `f_do_passI_opt` walks each block's records. A record with
+34 set and +33 clear goes to `func_414bf8`, which substitutes a copy's
source register into later uses of its destination. A record with +33 set
is never offered; as1 calls `func_412b40` instead and flushes the copy
table. Hook 75 clears the local copy of +33 for the selected records.

**Class.** ugen emits `andi t7,..; move v0,t7; sb v0,VOLATILE`. Retail's
assembler stored `t7` and kept the copy because v0 stays live. Native as1
stores v0. Closed GYOZI/func_8013E478 with zero PostProc (`=all`).

**Soundness.** `func_414bf8` only renames source registers to a register
that holds the same value, and kills table entries the record redefines.
The volatile flag constrains memory ordering in the reorder passes; the
record's memory access (opcode, address, width, value) is unchanged.

**Inertness.** Default off: the helper returns 0 and the original control
flow runs. Verified 2026-10-01: `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` hits the
retail SHA1 with 0 mismatched; GYOZI/EVENT/TACO verify at 100% effective.

## Hook 76: IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP — keep a pass-I copy entry across a fall-through block boundary (2026-10-02)

Applier: `python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_peepreg_hooks.py --rebuild`
(idempotent; anchors are `func_412cc4`'s own head, entry label `L412cc4` and
the body of `L412d4c`, which no other applier touches; run after the as1.c
regen with the other as1 appliers). In the post-`make clean` chain.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` \| `occ:N[,N..]` (0-based, per TU) | keep the listed kill candidates in `func_412cc4` |
| `IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP_TRACE` | `1` | `[AS1-PRK] #N blk=<ord>@<ptr> reg=<r> kind=<k> src=<s> target=<blk> livein=<mask> -> KILLED\|KEPT` (mask bit 31-r = register r) |

**Mechanism.** as1's pass-I driver `f_do_passI_opt` calls `func_414bf8` for
every record. That is the peephole `-Wb,-peepdbg` prints as
`>>Peepreg (INST n) changed rs A => B`. It keeps a register-contents table at
`0x10022240` (8 bytes per register: +0 kind, +6 source register; kind 3 =
"copy of register +6") and renames a use of a copy to the copy's source
(`func_413038`). At each block start `func_412cc4(block, previous block)`
decides whether the table carries over:

* the block's only predecessor is the previous block in layout (a pure
  fall-through) and that block has records → the table is inherited, but
  every entry whose source register is live-in at the previous block's
  branch target (`f_find_branch_target`, mask at +36) is killed;
* otherwise the table is flushed (`func_412b40`).

In a compare ladder `lbu $3; move $2,$3; beq $2,0; beq $2,1; beq $2,2`, the
renames stop at the first compare block that follows a branch whose target
has `$3` live-in. The hook sits right before the kill (`MEM_U32(a0+0) =
zero`) and skips it for the selected candidates. Only non-empty
general-register entries (regs 1..31) are counted; the FP loop is untouched.

**Soundness.** A kept entry records a fact from the end of block P:
"D holds a copy of S". `func_412cc4` only inherits the table into a block F
whose single predecessor is P, so the fact holds on every path into F. S
being live at P's taken target says nothing about the fall-through edge.
as1 already keeps every entry whose source is dead at the target, so a kept
entry gets exactly the rename as1 performs for those: a source operand is
replaced by a register holding the identical value. No instruction is
created, deleted or changed in opcode. Liveness is recomputed after pass I
(`func_41ca4c`/`func_41ce84` run again when pass I reports a change in an
inherited block), so pass II and the scheduler see the renamed code.

**Inertness.** Default off: the helper returns 0 before counting unless the
variable or the trace is set. Verified 2026-10-02: 933 TUs (all 518
overlay/main files carrying an `IDO52_AS1_*` token plus a random 415)
compile byte-identically with the pristine and the hooked as1, env unset;
TRACE alone is also inert. `audit_hook_inertness.py --only
IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP` reports LIVE (via its own users).

**The class it closes: the dead selector copy.** Retail keeps a dead
`move v0,v1` in the first `beqz` delay slot of a switch, and every compare
reads v1. Three facts, all needed, found on six functions:

1. **The function returns int.** Konami wrote K&R-style implicit-int
   functions that return nothing. `$v0` is live at the exit, so on the
   default path the copy is not dead and as1 keeps it. With `void`, as1
   deletes it (pass II Rednt) and lets the scheduler pull a later `lui v0`
   into a delay slot. Write `s32 f(void)` with no return value. This is
   what the old "dead copy survives" notes could not explain.
2. **The selector copy is its own web in `$2`, the global's web is `$3`.**
   Arms that bump the step counter read the GLOBAL (`D += 1`, `D++`,
   `D = D + 1`), not the local. Retail reads v1 there; reading the local
   puts the arm on the copy (that was the old "`step + 1` in v0" residual).
   If the local is then used only by the switch, uopt substitutes the load
   into it; Hook 58 `IDO52_COPYPROP_VAR=<local's frame offset>` keeps it.
3. **Peepreg renames compares from the copy to the source,** except past a
   branch whose target has `$3` live-in. Hook 76 keeps that entry.

Results (all zero PostProc unless noted, every gate clean):

| Function | Env |
|---|---|
| main/title_init_Coordinate | `IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP=0` |
| main/continue_game_init | `IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP=0` |
| RPG_BAT/func_80150B98 | `IDO52_COPYPROP_VAR=-8 IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP=0` |
| ETC/func_80145170 | `IDO52_COPYPROP_VAR=-8 IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP=0` |
| ETC/func_801478F8 | `IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP=0` (+ its old case-2 swaps and a regenerated t-register paint layer, iso 0, SAME) |
| TACO/func_80138880 | `IDO52_COPYPROP_VAR=-12` only: no kill candidate; the join tests the global and the per-case reloads are uopt's |

Recipe: give the function an `s32` return type, make the arms read the
global, run `IDO52_AS1_PEEPREG_KEEP_TRACE=1` and keep the candidate whose
`src` is the global's register and `reg` the copy's. The candidate number
shifts if any other env token changes the stream, so read it under the final
env.

## Hook 77: IDO52_AS1_REPL_TGT_KEEP — veto as1's "Repl_reg_tgt" delete of a dead-out copy (2026-10-02)

Applier: `python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_as1_repltgt_hooks.py --rebuild`
(idempotent; anchors are `func_4142d8`'s own head and the body of recomp label
`L414438`, which no other applier touches; run after the as1.c regen with the
other as1 appliers). In the post-`make clean` chain.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_REPL_TGT_KEEP` | `all` \| `N[,N..]` \| `occ:N[,N..]` (0-based, per TU) | keep the copy at the listed candidates |
| `IDO52_AS1_REPL_TGT_KEEP_TRACE` | `1` | `[AS1-RRT] #N rec=<idx> word=<insn> dst=<rD> src=<rS> -> APPLIED\|KEPT` |

**Mechanism.** `func_4142d8(rec, kind, block)` is the pass-II peephole that
`-Wa,-peepdbg` prints as `>>Repl_reg_tgt (INST n) changed to NOP`. For a copy
record `rD <- rS` (record +28 / +29) it gives up if rD is live out of the
block (block masks +48/+52), then walks the later records of the block: it
gives up at a record it may not look through (+34 clear) or one that
redefines rS, and stops at the first redefinition of rD or at the block end.
Otherwise it deletes the copy (`f_change_bb(rec, 0, 5, 0)`) and renames rD to
rS in the walked records (`func_4139ec`). The hook sits at `L414438`, after
every give-up test, and a keep takes the function's own "not applied" exit
(`v0 = 0`, `L414560`).

**Soundness.** A keep declines an optimisation. The copy stays and the later
records keep reading rD, which still holds rS's value: the function only
reaches `L414438` when no record in between redefines rS or rD. No
instruction is created, deleted or changed in opcode by the hook. Re-offers of
a kept record (pass II iterates) are kept again without a new number.

**Inertness.** Default off: the helper returns 0 before counting unless the
variable or the trace is set. Verified 2026-10-02 against the pre-hook as1
(same cfe/uopt/ugen): 933 TUs (all 518 overlay/main files carrying an
`IDO52_AS1_*` token plus 415 random; the six files run K had in flux
excluded) compile byte-identically with the env unset, and again with each
file's own `@CompileEnv` applied (930 identical, 3 fail on both sides under
the harness's simplified flags); TRACE alone is also inert.
`build_level3_c.py --no-cache` hits the retail SHA1. `audit_hook_inertness.py
--only IDO52_AS1_REPL_TGT_KEEP` reports INERT because it compiles a user with
the hook ALONE; its one user needs three sibling hooks to produce a
candidate. With those set, `=0` / `=all` change the object and `=1` does not.

**The class: retail keeps a copy whose only reader was renamed.** ugen emits
`move a2,a3 ; sb b2 (volatile) ; move a0,a3 ; sw a2,44 ; jal` (the spill of
`tmp2 = tmp` across a call). Retail's object reads the spill from a3 and
still has the `move a2,a3`, pulled into the preceding `bne` delay slot. Our
as1 needs three vetoes in sequence to get there:

1. Hook 75 `IDO52_AS1_PASS1_VOLATILE_OK=<the sb>`: pass-I Peepreg otherwise
   flushes its copy table at the volatile `sb` and never renames the `sw`.
2. Hook 52 `IDO52_AS1_KEEP_DEAD_RELOAD=<the move>`: once the `sw` reads a3,
   `f_redundant` ("Rednt") deletes the now-dead move.
3. Hook 77 `IDO52_AS1_REPL_TGT_KEEP=<the move>`: then Repl_reg_tgt deletes it.

as1's own branch-delay fill then moves the kept copy into the `bne` slot.
Closed NAME_ENT/func_80140804 (`... PASS1_VOLATILE_OK=3
KEEP_DEAD_RELOAD=4 REPL_TGT_KEEP=0`), DIFFER -> SAME, iso 0. Read the
candidate numbers with the three `_TRACE` switches under the final env.

## Hook 78: IDO52_CFE_SWITCH_TABLE_MIN — minimum case cluster for a jump table, per switch (2026-10-03)

Applier: `python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_cfe_switchmin_hooks.py --rebuild`
(idempotent, marker `cfsw_tmin`). **Requires Hook 67** — it anchors on the two
cluster tests Hook 67 rewrote (`at = (a1 < 0x5) || cfsw_sel;`) and reuses its
per-TU switch counter, so run it right after `apply_cfe_switchchain_hooks.py`
in the post-`make clean` chain.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_CFE_SWITCH_TABLE_MIN` | `N` \| `IDX:N[,IDX:N..]` | the smallest case cluster cfe lowers to an XJP jump table (stock 5): for every switch, or for the IDX-th switch statement of the TU (0-based, source order -- the numbering `IDO52_CFE_SWITCH_TRACE=1` prints). N in 1..64. |

**Decision.** `func_4648f8` groups sorted case values into clusters (a value
within 25 of the running top extends one); a cluster closing with fewer than
5 values is split into single compares (`a1 < 0x5`, L464c48 / L464e88), so
IDO 5.2 lowers every 4-label switch to a ladder. The retail library band was
not all built with that threshold: ResetGraph dispatches a 4-label
`switch (arg0 & 3)` through a 4-entry table (0x800B9650). Its C used an
unreachable `case 4:` to get a table, which made the object's `.rodata` one
entry longer than retail's -- invisible until the main build began comparing
jump tables (2026-10-03). `IDO52_CFE_SWITCH_TABLE_MIN=0:4` gives retail's
4-entry table from the 4 real labels.

**Soundness / inertness.** Table and ladder dispatch every value to the same
label; the hook picks between cfe's own two lowerings. Env unset → threshold
5: 290 sampled IDO TUs compile byte-identically against the pre-hook cfe and
`build_level3_c.py` reproduces the retail SHA1.

## Hook 79: IDO52_UGEN_KEEP_JUMP_OVER — keep a conditional jump over an unconditional jump (2026-10-03)

Applier: `python3 tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_ugen_jumpover_hooks.py [--ugen PATH] --rebuild`
(idempotent, marker `ugen_jover_veto`; the rebuild writes `out/ugen.new` and
renames it over `out/ugen`, so a concurrent compile never sees a half-written
binary). Independent of every other applier: its helper anchors on
`func_4351b8`'s definition and its site on that function's label `L435510`
(Hook 61 patches `L435424` of the same function). Add it to the post-`make
clean` chain anywhere after the ugen.c regen. Default OFF.

**The transform.** cfe lowers an EMPTY then-arm -- `if (c) {} else { X }`,
or the first arm of an if/else-if ladder written `if (w < 281) { mode |= 0;
} else if ...` -- as `fjp/tjp c,L1 ; ujp END ; L1: X ; END:`. `-O0` ugen
emits exactly that (`bge $2,281,$32 ; b $35 ; $32:`). From `-O1` up,
`f_labelopt` -> `func_4351b8` takes every conditional jump s2 (tree opcode
`0x7f` FJP / `0x26` TJP) whose next statement s1 (past label nodes) is a UJP
(`0x88`), scans the label nodes after s1 and, when the conditional's own
target s0 is among them (`L435510`, `s0 == v0`), flips the conditional
(`0x7f` <-> `0x26`), retargets it to the UJP's target and deletes the UJP:
one inverted branch (`blt $2,281,$END`). This is why no source spelling of
an empty arm keeps the `b` at -O2 (uopt has already folded the arm to
nothing; ugen does the rest), and why `IDO52_AS1_NO_BRANCH_FOLD` /
`NO_JUMP_INVERT` were inert on it -- the branch reaches as1 already inverted.
The retail library band keeps the `b END` (libgpu PutDispEnv: `beqz at,L1 ;
slti ; b END ; nop` and `bnez at,ORI ; nop ; b END ; nop ; ORI:`).

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_UGEN_KEEP_JUMP_OVER` | `all` \| `N[,N...]` \| `occ:N,...` \| `lab:L,...` (a bare number is an INDEX) | keep candidate N (0-based arrival order over the TU and over BOTH `func_4351b8` calls) or every candidate whose CONDITIONAL targets ugen's INTERNAL label L (tree `+36`, as traced; not the `$N` of `cc -S`). Prefer `lab:` -- a kept candidate is re-offered by the second call. |
| `IDO52_UGEN_KEEP_JUMP_OVER_TRACE` | presence | `[UGEN-JOVER] #N op=0xOP label=L ujp_label=U [-> KEPT]` |

**Soundness.** The veto takes `L4355bc`, the path `func_4351b8` takes for
every jump that is not a jump-over-jump (stamp the target label, advance):
the conditional keeps its own target and the UJP stays. Nothing is created,
no operand or opcode is rewritten, and both forms reach the same code.
`func_4350c4(s1)`, which runs on the UJP just before the scan, only resolves
the UJP's own jump chain -- it happens anyway when the walk reaches the UJP.

**Inertness (2026-10-03).** Env unset: 332 of 332 comparable TUs (every
12th `src/main/matched` IDO file, every 60th overlay file, every IDO main
semi; 2 more fail on both sides under the harness's approximated command
line) compile to byte-identical objects with the pre-hook and the hooked
ugen. `build_level3_c.py --no-cache` reproduces the retail SHA1.

**Consumer.** `main/func_800A0614` (libgpu PutDispEnv) **BYTE-PERFECT
323/323**: exactly two candidates in the TU (`label=53`, `label=61` -- the
disp.w ladder's empty first arm and the disp.h test's empty arm), both kept
(`=all`). It was the 4-word half of that function's 6-word count wall.

## Hook 80: IDO52_UGEN_CMPBR_RIGHT_FIRST — evaluate a compare-and-branch's right operand first (2026-10-04)

Applier: `tools/ido-static-recomp/apply_ugen_cmpbr_order_hooks.py [--ugen PATH] [--rebuild]`.
⚠️ **Add it to the post-`make clean` chain** (independent of every other
ugen applier: helper anchored on `f_jump`'s definition, site on the `f_jump`
label `L422fc8`, which nothing else patches). Idempotent (marker
`ugen_cmprf_pure`). Default OFF.

**The transform.** `f_jump` lowers `fjp/tjp (rel a b)` with two non-constant
operands at `L422fc8` as `f_eval(a) ; f_eval(b) ; f_reg(a) ; f_reg(b) ;
f_emit_branch_rrll` — always the LEFT operand first (unlike `f_eval_2ops`,
which evaluates the costlier operand first). uopt hands relations over in a
canonical operand order: `60000000 % t > t / 2` reaches ugen as `geq (div t 2)
(rem K t)` for every source spelling tried (operands swapped, `<`/`<=`/`!`,
a temp, `vs->tempo` reloads — all byte-identical), so the stream is `div
t7,t,2 ; rem t6,K,t ; bge t7,t6`. Retail's library band has the `rem` first
(its `div zero,t2,v0`, `mfhi t6`, checks), then the signed pow2 expansion,
then `slt at,t7,t6 ; beqz at` (InitSoundSeq 0x800912D4, InitSoundSep
0x800916C4). The hook emits the right operand's tree first; the `f_reg`
reads and the branch emission are unchanged, so the branch compares the same
(left, right) registers. Every instruction is ugen's own.

**Soundness.** Applied only when both operand trees are order-insensitive:
`Uldc`, non-volatile `Ulod` (lexlev bit 0 clear), and the pure integer
operators abs add and cvt cvtl dec div inc ior lnot mod mpy neg not rem shl
shr sub xor — no store, call or indirect load — with at most ONE operation
that can trap (div/rem/mod whose divisor is not a constant other than 0/-1).
Everything else in the trees is unobservable, so moving it across that one
operation changes no observable order. An impure occurrence keeps the stock
order (TRACE prints `IMPURE`). Positive/negative controls: `if (k % t > G /
2)` swaps; the same with `volatile int V` stays stock.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_UGEN_CMPBR_RIGHT_FIRST` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N...]` \| `N[,N...]` | occurrence = arrival at `L422fc8`, 0-based over the TU |
| `IDO52_UGEN_CMPBR_RIGHT_FIRST_TRACE` | presence | `[UGEN-CMPRF] occ=N op=REL L=OP R=OP pure\|IMPURE -> RIGHT FIRST\|stock` |

## Hook 62e: IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO_MFFIRST_FLUSH — mfhi/mflo before the check at an unclassified flush (2026-10-04)

Same applier as Hooks 62/62b/62c/62d (`apply_as1_divmacro_hooks.py
[--rebuild]`); it upgrades an as1.c carrying the 62d helper block in place
(new helper marker `divm_mfu_hit`). Default OFF; acts only on a divide that
Hook 62 deferred.

When a record the Hook 62 classifier does not know (a branch macro, the
signed `div rd,rs,2^k` expansion of Hook 62b, ...) flushes a pending check
AND its pending mflo/mfhi, stock Hook 62 emits the check first. With the
deferred divide's occurrence listed, the mflo/mfhi goes first (still
check-first when rd is rs or rt, as `divm_emit_mf` already guarantees) —
the `aspsx-mffirst` rule extended to the unclassified-record path, per
occurrence. Retail: `div zero,t2,v0 ; ... ; mfhi t6 ; <signed check> ; bgez
v0 ; addu at ; addiu at ; sra t7,at,1`. Nothing is created or dropped: the
same emitters, in a different order.

| Variable | Format | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `IDO52_AS1_DIV_MACRO_MFFIRST_FLUSH` | `all` \| `occ:N[,N...]` | N = the Hook 62 divide occurrence (`[AS1-DIVM] #N`) |

**Inertness (Hooks 80 + 62e, 2026-10-04).** Env unset (each file's own
`@CompileEnv`, the two new variables removed): **7,255 of 7,255** comparable
IDO TUs — every registered main C file and every overlay C file; 234 gcc /
third-pipeline files skipped, 3 fail on both sides under the harness's
approximated command line — compile to byte-identical objects with the
pre-hook ugen/as1 and the hooked ones. `build_level3_c.py --no-cache`:
1,374 matched, 0 mismatched, retail SHA1. (`audit_hook_inertness.py` reads
INERT on a random 10-file sample: sample-bound, as documented; the two
consumers below are the live evidence.)

**Consumers.** `main/InitSoundSeq` (func_80091160) and `main/InitSoundSep`
(func_80091570), BYTE-PERFECT with their 28 / 25 `swap_instructions`
bridges retired (they had moved branch words, so the prover saw a different
CFG: UNPROVEN branch-kind). Now P == S modulo `convert_or_to_addu` (+ Sep's
`remap_stack_slots`), `audit_plain_vs_shipped` SAME, `prove_equivalence`
PROVEN. Recipe: Hook 80 occ 1 + Hook 62e occ 0 for the tempo arm; divides
0,3,4,5 deferred with Hook 62d `MUL_PASS/QUEUE/SPLIT_CMPBR=all` for the h6E
arm (the func_80094660 recipe); Seq also vetoes one fall-through fill
(`NO_FALLTHRU_FILL=3`) so `FORCE_BOPT=2` can copy the target's `addiu
t5,zero,-1` into the slot as retail does. Hook 36 forces that had painted the
swapped order's registers were dropped (Seq `19:t7,26:t7`, Sep `24:t6`).
